### [1] [A World Model of Radiologist Reading for Medical Image Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23992)
*Yiwei Li,Zihao Wu,Huaqin Zhao,Yifan Zhou,Chao Cao,Dajiang Zhu,Tianming Liu,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GazeWorld是一种医学影像世界模型，将放射科医生的眼动注视序列视为在图像世界中的轨迹，通过自回归预测注视区域和空间补全未访问区域来学习，无需真实眼动数据即可生成特征，在多个医疗影像基准上达到最优诊断和注视预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对放射科医生眼动数据的利用不充分，要么仅作为静态空间先验，要么作为与诊断脱节的辅助预测目标，未能充分挖掘专家阅读过程中的时序搜索和证据积累信息。

Method: 将图像视为世界，放射科医生的注视序列视为轨迹；使用自回归方式从已访问的补丁预测下一个注视补丁的潜在表示，同时通过空间补充分支覆盖未访问区域；推理时仅需图像即可生成长序列的补丁表示，无需真实注视数据。

Result: 冻结的GazeWorld特征在CheXpert、RSNA Pneumonia、SIIM-ACR Pneumothorax三个基准的九项监督设置中均达到最高诊断准确率，并在零样本设置中同样最优；在GazeSearch基准上，使用相同冻结特征的通用解码器在ScanMatch和SED指标上分别超过专用模型LogitGaze-Med 16%和22%。

Conclusion: 建模专家如何阅读（过程）而非仅关注其结论（结果），为医学影像AI提供了一种有前景的预训练范式。

Abstract: Radiologist eye-tracking data provide a rich record of how experts search, compare, and accumulate evidence during image reading; yet, existing methods exploit this signal only partially, either as a static spatial prior or as an auxiliary prediction target decoupled from diagnosis. We propose GazeWorld, a medical imaging world model that treats the image as the world and the radiologist's fixation sequence as a trajectory through it. GazeWorld autoregressively predicts the latent representation of the next fixated patch from all previously visited ones, while a spatial-completion branch covers unvisited regions. At inference, GazeWorld generates a sequence of patch representations from the image alone without requiring real gaze data. Frozen GazeWorld features achieve state-of-the-art diagnostic accuracy across all nine supervised settings on CheXpert, RSNA Pneumonia, and SIIM-ACR Pneumothorax, as well as the highest zero-shot accuracy on all three benchmarks. On the GazeSearch benchmark, a generic decoder trained on the same frozen features outperforms the purpose-built LogitGaze-Med by over 16\% in ScanMatch and 22\% in SED, despite not being explicitly trained to predict gaze. GazeWorld demonstrates that modeling how experts read, not just what they conclude, offers a promising pretraining paradigm for medical imaging AI.

</details>


### [2] [Nano World Models: A Minimalist Implementation of Future Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23993)
*Siqiao Huang,Partha Kaushik,Michael Chen,Hengkai Pan,Omar Chehab,Fernando Moreno-Pino,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Nano World Models 是一个极简代码库，基于扩散强迫（diffusion forcing）实现未来视频预测，统一了生成目标、模型规模、动作条件、潜在观测空间、数据集、评估协议和长程 rollout 流程，为世界模型研究提供可复现、可扩展的实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管工业级交互式视频生成发展迅速，但研究社区缺乏紧凑、可复现且易于扩展的实现来系统研究现代世界模型的设计选择。

Method: 构建以扩散强迫为核心的极简代码库，统一接口支持多种生成目标、模型规模、动作条件机制、潜在观测空间、数据集、评估协议和长程 rollout 流程，实现世界模型各组件的受控研究。

Result: 通过简单控制环境、游戏模拟和真实机器人数据实验，系统考察了预测参数化、架构规模、动作注入、采样预算和领域复杂度对视频预测质量和自回归 rollout 行为的影响。

Conclusion: Nano World Models 通过开源代码、配置、评估脚本和预训练检查点，为开放、可复现、科学化的世界模型研究提供了一个紧凑且可扩展的实验基础。

Abstract: World models have become a central paradigm for learning predictive simulators that support generation, planning, and decision-making. Yet, despite rapid progress in industry-scale interactive video generation, the broader research community still lacks compact, reproducible, and easily extensible implementations for studying the design choices underlying modern world models. We introduce Nano World Models, a minimalist codebase for future video prediction centered around diffusion forcing. Nano World Models provides a unified interface for generative objectives, model scales, action-conditioning mechanisms, latent observation spaces, datasets, evaluation protocols, and long-horizon rollout procedures. This design enables controlled studies of world-modeling components that are often entangled across separate implementations. Through experiments across simple control environments, game simulation, and real-robot data, we examine how prediction parameterization, architecture scale, action injection, sampling budget, and domain complexity affect video prediction quality and autoregressive rollout behavior. By releasing code, configurations, evaluation scripts, and pretrained checkpoints, Nano World Models aims to provide a compact yet extensible experimental substrate for open, reproducible, and scientific world-model research.

</details>


### [3] [RAW: Robust Avatar Watermarking -- Benchmarking and Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23994)
*Jack Parry,Jack Saunders,Vinay Namboodiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAW基准测试（包含50个来自5家商用提供商的合成头像视频及6种攻击），发现现有7种方法在头像水印中鲁棒性不足。作者提出WALT方法，通过在UV纹理空间嵌入水印（基于3D人脸重建），在缩放攻击（92.4%）和背景移除（95.6%）上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字头像水印面临独特挑战：头像在部署前常经历背景替换、重新构图和格式转换等后处理，现有水印方法对这些头像特定攻击的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 构建RAW基准（50个头像视频+6种攻击），评估7种现有方法；提出WALT方法，通过3D人脸重建在UV纹理空间嵌入水印。

Result: WALT在缩放攻击上达到92.4%鲁棒性，在背景移除攻击上达到95.6%，均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 头像特定攻击（如背景移除）会显著降低现有水印方法的恢复率，WALT通过纹理空间水印有效提升了鲁棒性，作者公开基准以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Digital avatar watermarking presents unique challenges: avatars are routinely post-processed with background replacement, reframing, and format conversion before deployment. We introduce \textbf{RAW} (Robust Avatar Watermarking), a benchmark comprising 50 synthetic avatar videos from 5 commercial providers and 6 attacks simulating real-world avatar workflows. Evaluating 7 existing methods reveals that avatar-specific attacks such as background removal significantly degrade watermark recovery. We propose \textbf{WALT} (Watermarking Avatars with Learned Textures), which embeds watermarks in UV texture space via 3D face reconstruction. WALT achieves the highest robustness to zoom attacks (92.4\%) while maintaining strong performance on background removal (95.6\%). We release our benchmark to facilitate research into avatar-specific watermarking.

</details>


### [4] [Brain-to-Image Retrieval and Reconstruction via Multimodal EEG Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23996)
*Chi Kit Wong,Yan Liu,Haowen Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于EEG信号的脑-图像解码系统，在自然图像观看时从脑电信号中解码视觉刺激。系统包含两个任务：EEG-to-image检索（Top-1准确率86.30%，Top-5准确率98.55%）和EEG-to-image重建（CLIP分数0.903，SSIM 0.409）。检索采用多级模糊方法结合EVNet特征和InfoNCE损失；重建采用CognitionCapturerPro对齐多模态CLIP嵌入并用SDXL-Turbo生成图像。结果证明了利用现代多模态对齐和生成建模技术从EEG信号解码丰富视觉表示的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索从非侵入性脑电图（EEG）信号中解码丰富的视觉表征，验证多模态对齐和生成建模技术在脑-图像解码中的可行性。

Method: 检索任务：采用多级模糊方法结合生物启发的EVNet特征，使用InfoNCE损失训练。重建任务：CognitionCapturerPro方法将EEG表示对齐到多模态CLIP嵌入（图像、文本、深度、边缘嵌入），通过IP-Adapter条件化SDXL-Turbo生成图像。

Result: 检索：单被试10个随机种子的平均Top-1准确率86.30%，Top-5准确率98.55%。重建：10个种子平均CLIP分数（ViT-H-14）0.903，（ViT-L/14）0.870，SSIM 0.409。

Conclusion: 证明了利用现代多模态对齐和生成建模技术从EEG信号解码丰富视觉表示的可行性，在检索和重建两个任务上都取得了高性能。

Abstract: We present a brain-to-image system that decodes visual stimuli from EEG signals recorded during natural image viewing. Our system addresses two tasks: (1) EEG-to-image retrieval, which ranks the correct stimulus image among 200 candidates given an EEG segment, and (2) EEG-to-image reconstruction, which generates an image consistent with the perceived stimulus. For retrieval, we implement a multi-level blurring approach improved with biologically inspired EVNet features and trained with the InfoNCE loss. Evaluated over 10 random seeds for a single subject, the retrieval model achieves a mean final-epoch Top-1 accuracy of 86.30% and Top-5 accuracy of 98.55%. For reconstruction, we implement CognitionCapturerPro, which aligns EEG representations to multi-modal CLIP embeddings, including image, text, depth, and edge embeddings, and synthesizes images with SDXL-Turbo conditioned via IP-Adapter. Averaged over 10 seeds, the reconstruction model achieves a CLIP score of 0.903 using ViT-H-14, a CLIP score of 0.870 using ViT-L/14, and an SSIM of 0.409. These results demonstrate the feasibility of decoding rich visual representations from EEG signals using modern multi-modal alignment and generative modeling techniques.

</details>


### [5] [Remote sensing data imputation using deep learning for multispectral imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24003)
*Shuang Liua,Fiona Johnson,Rohitash Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了线性插值与深度学习模型（CNN、Inception Resnet、Autoencoder及其与LSTM的组合）在重建四个湖泊的缺失光谱波段方面的表现。结果表明，深度学习模型显著优于线性插值，其中CNN表现最佳，有效提升了PlanetScope SuperDove影像中藻华监测的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学卫星数据常因云覆盖导致观测缺失，这可能导致错过关键事件（如藻华）的检测，因此提高光学卫星数据集的完整性对改善藻华监测和预测至关重要。

Method: 在四个有历史藻华记录的湖泊上，比较了传统线性插值与多种深度学习模型（CNN、Inception Resnet、Autoencoder、CNN-LSTM、Resnet-LSTM、Autoencoder-LSTM）在重建缺失光谱波段上的表现，并评估了基于重建影像的藻华指数（Green/Red和NDCI）与观测数据的一致性。

Result: 深度学习模型在人工掩膜区域的光谱波段插补中显著优于线性插值；其中CNN在大多数湖泊中表现最佳；基于插补影像计算的藻华指数与观测数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型能有效插补PlanetScope SuperDove影像中的缺失数据，从而支持更可靠的水体监测应用。

Abstract: Remote sensing techniques have been increasingly utilised in aquatic applications in recent years. A common challenge in using optical satellite data is the presence of missing observations due to cloud cover. These data gaps can lead to missed detection of critical events, such as algal blooms, in lakes of high interest to water authorities. As a result, enhancing the completeness of optical satellite datasets is crucial for improving the monitoring and prediction of algal blooms. In this study, we compared a traditional data imputation method (i.e., linear interpolation) with deep learning models for reconstructing missing spectral bands across four lakes with historical records of algal blooms. The deep learning models adopted include CNN-based architectures (i.e., CNN, Inception Resnet, and Autoencoder) and CNN-LSTM-based architectures (i.e., CNN-LSTM, Resnet-LSTM, and Autoencoder-LSTM). Our results demonstrated that deep learning models substantially outperformed the baseline linear interpolation method in imputing spectral band values within artificially masked regions. Among these models, CNN delivered the best performance across most lakes. Furthermore, we evaluated the performance of algal bloom indices (i.e., Green/Red and NDCI) derived from the imputed imagery by comparing them with the observed data. Our results demonstrate that deep learning models are effective for imputing missing data in PlanetScope SuperDove imagery, enabling more reliable applications in water monitoring.

</details>


### [6] [ActQuant: Sub-4-bit Action-Guided Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24011)
*Arash Akbari,Arman Akbari,Masih Eskandar,Qitao Tan,Yixiao Chen,Jingwu Luo,Bertha Pangaribuan,Liyun Zhang,Jennifer Dy,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Gaowen Liu,Stratis Ioannidis,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ActQuant，一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型的动作引导混合精度后训练量化框架，结合OmniModel.cpp部署管线，在低于3比特/权重的极端量化下仍保持高性能，实现5.3倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在具身智能中展现出色动作生成能力，但计算开销过大导致边缘部署不可行；现有后训练量化方法在激进的低比特（<4比特）量化下性能严重退化。

Method: 提出两阶段ActQuant框架：(1) 张量间比特分配器：根据每个权重矩阵对动作预测的贡献分配比特宽度；(2) 张量内尺度优化器：利用动作感知曲率调整逐块量化尺度，聚焦关键权重。同时提出OmniModel.cpp，将模型转换为原生C/C++运行时并支持高效低比特核。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，ActQuant是唯一能在≤3 bpw下运行的方法，OpenVLA-OFT保持95.0%，π₀.₅保持94.8%；2.5 bpw时OpenVLA-OFT达90.1%，骨干从14.3GB压缩至2.7GB（5.3×）；真实UR3机械臂上π₀.₅量化后成功率不变，内存减少2.5×。

Conclusion: ActQuant通过动作引导的混合精度量化策略，显著提升了极端低比特下VLA模型的部署可行性，在仿真和真实机器人上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit remarkable action generation for embodied intelligence, but their heavy compute make deployment on edge platforms impractical. Aggressive, sub-4-bit weight quantization is the natural solution, yet existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suffer severe performance degradation in this regime. To address this, we introduce ActQuant, an action-guided mixed-precision PTQ framework that operates in two stages: (1) an inter-tensor bit allocator that assigns each weight matrix a single bit-width based on how much it contributes to predicting the agent's actions; (2) an intra-tensor scale optimizer tunes per-block quantization scales using action-aware curvature, so that dynamic range is concentrated on the weights most influential for control. To deliver the on-device benefits of our aggressive quantization, we further introduce OmniModel.cpp, an agentic conversion pipeline that ports architectures into a native C/C++ runtime with efficient low-bit kernels. We evaluate ActQuant both in simulation and on a real-world 6-DoF UR3 arm, with all models deployed through OmniModel.cpp. On the LIBERO benchmark, ActQuant is the only method that operates at or below 3 bits-per-weight, retaining 95.0% on OpenVLA-OFT and 94.8% on $π_{0.5}$. Pushed further, ActQuant reaches 2.5 bpw at 90.1% on OpenVLA-OFT, compressing the backbone from 14.3 GB to 2.7 GB (5.3$\times$). On the physical UR3 arm, $π_{0.5}$ quantized with ActQuant retains the baseline's success rate while reducing the memory footprint by 2.5$\times$.

</details>


### [7] [SkySeg: Collaborative Onboard Semantic Segmentation with Heterogeneous UAVs in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24014)
*Anqi Lu,Yun Cheng,Youbing Hu,Zhiqiang Cao,Jie Liu,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkySeg是一个异构多无人机空-空协作框架，通过融合低分辨率广角和高分辨率聚焦图像，结合跨设备测试时自适应策略，在资源受限的无人机平台上实现了实时、准确的语义分割，推理速度提升约3.6倍，准确率提升5.91%，野外环境平均提升10.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机遥感任务需要实时语义分割，但资源受限的无人机平台面临两大挑战：1)硬件限制阻碍实时分割；2)飞行中环境变化导致数据分布漂移，偏离原始训练数据。

Method: SkySeg框架包含两部分：1)高效信息融合推理方法，结合低分辨率广角图像与高分辨率聚焦图像；2)跨设备测试时自适应(TTA)策略，通过多无人机协同应对测试数据流的分布偏移。

Result: 推理延迟加速约3.6倍，机载分割准确率提升5.91%，野外环境下平均准确率提升10.91%。

Conclusion: SkySec通过多无人机协作与自适应策略，有效解决了资源受限无人机平台的实时语义分割难题，为无人机遥感任务提供了可行的机载计算方案。

Abstract: The demand for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based image acquisition and analysis has surged, with UAVs increasingly utilized for semantic segmentation tasks. To meet the real-time analysis requirements of UAV remote sensing missions, performing onboard computation and making decisions based on the results is a natural approach. However, deploying semantic segmentation on resource-constrained UAV platforms presents two significant challenges: 1) hardware constraints limit the ability of UAVs to perform real-time semantic segmentation, and 2) environmental variations during flight cause data distribution shifts, deviating from the original training data. To address these issues, this paper introduces SkySeg, a heterogeneous multi-UAV air-air cooperation framework that integrates computer vision and flight pattern to enable onboard semantic segmentation using low-cost sensors. SkySeg employs an efficient information fusion inference method, combining low-definition, wide-area images with high-definition, focused-area images. Additionally, it incorporates a cross-device test-time adaptation (TTA) strategy to enhance segmentation performance in dynamic environments by collaboratively addressing distribution shifts of test data streams across UAVs. Experimental results demonstrate that our SkySeg framework accelerates inference latency by approximately 3.6x, improves onboard segmentation accuracy by 5.91\%, and achieves a 10.91\% average accuracy gain in the wild.

</details>


### [8] [MGVQ: Synergizing Multi-dimensional Sensitivity-Aware and Gradient-Hessian Fusion for Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24019)
*Zhong Wang,Zukang Xu,Xing Hu,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MGVQ框架，通过敏感度引导的混合精度量化和梯度-海森融合的二阶误差补偿，解决了视觉-语言模型在超低位向量量化中跨模态权重分布差异和梯度信息缺失的问题，在2-bit量化下最高提升4.9个百分点的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型(VLM)参数量巨大，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上。现有向量量化(VQ)直接应用于VLM存在两个核心局限：一是单一码本无法拟合视觉和文本输入的跨模态权重分布差异；二是当前二阶误差补偿忽略了一阶梯度信息，导致权重偏离预训练最优状态、梯度偏移和补偿结果偏差。

Method: MGVQ框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 敏感度引导的结构化混合精度量化——通过全局与局部联合敏感度分析，根据通道敏感度动态分配不同位宽；(2) 梯度感知的二阶误差补偿——将一阶梯度嵌入误差校正中，并采用Kronecker分解和Block-LDL分解来保证低计算成本。

Result: 在LLaVA-onevision、InternVL2和Qwen2-VL等主流VLM上的实验表明，在2-bit量化设置下，MGVQ显著超越现有先进的后训练量化方法，在InternVL2-26B上最高提升4.9个百分点（71.4% vs 67.0%）。

Conclusion: MGVQ实现了稳定高效的超低位VLM量化，极大促进了多模态大模型在资源受限环境中的实际部署。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve outstanding performance, yet their huge model size severely hinders deployment on edge devices with limited resources. As an efficient model compression technique, vector quantization (VQ) excels in ultra-low-bit representation, which maps model weights to discrete codewords in a compact codebook to cut memory consumption and transmission overhead while preserving model capability. Direct VQ application to VLMs still has two core limitations. First, cross-modality weight distribution differences brought by visual and textual inputs cannot be well fitted by a single unified codebook. Second, current second-order error compensation ignores first-order gradient information, causing weight deviation from pre-trained optimal states, gradient drift and biased compensation results. This work proposes MGVQ, a novel vector quantization framework integrating multi-dimensional sensitivity perception and gradient-Hessian fusion. It consists of two core modules: sensitivity-guided structured mixed-precision quantization dynamically assigns different bit-widths according to channel sensitivity via combined global and local sensitivity analysis for refined resource allocation; gradient-aware second-order error compensation embeds first-order gradients into error correction, and adopts Kronecker and Block-LDL decomposition to ensure low computational cost. Extensive experiments on mainstream VLMs including LLaVA-onevision, InternVL2 and Qwen2-VL verify the effectiveness of MGVQ. In 2-bit quantization settings, MGVQ surpasses existing advanced post-training quantization methods significantly, achieving a maximum accuracy improvement of 4.9 points (71.4% vs 67.0% on InternVL2-26B). The proposed method realizes stable and efficient ultra-low-bit VLM quantization, greatly promoting the practical deployment of multimodal large models in resource-limited environments.

</details>


### [9] [Machine Intelligence that Understands Visual and Linguistic Information and Interacts with Humans and Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24020)
*Van Quang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出三种新型架构分别改进图像描述、视觉对话和交互式指令跟踪任务：GRIT（基于网格和区域的图像描述Transformer）、LTMI（轻量级多输入Transformer）以及一个两阶段指令解析框架，在多个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉与自然语言处理的交叉领域对辅助技术、多媒体查询和机器人等应用至关重要，但现有方法在视觉表征的全局性、多输入交互效率和具身AI指令跟随方面存在局限。

Method: 1) GRIT：基于DETR检测器融合网格与区域特征的Transformer-only架构，实现端到端训练；2) LTMI：专用注意力块设计，以不到十分之一的参数匹配标准Transformer扩展的表征能力；3) 两阶段指令解析框架：先独立于视觉上下文解码语言指令预测动作-对象序列，再与多视角视觉特征融合执行。

Result: GRIT在推理精度和速度上均超越先前方法；LTMI在VisDial数据集上以极低参数量达到可比性能；指令跟随框架在ALFRED数据集上达到8.37%的未见场景成功率。

Conclusion: 所提架构在三个视觉-语言任务上均取得显著改进，验证了从视觉表征优化、多输入高效建模到具身指令解析不同层面的创新有效性。

Abstract: Advancements at the intersection of computer vision and natural language processing are crucial for applications like assistive tech, multimedia querying, and robotics. This dissertation proposes novel architectures to improve intelligent agents across three key vision-language tasks: image captioning, visual dialog, and interactive instruction following.
  First, we address limitations in visual representation for image captioning. Traditional models rely on region-based features from CNN detectors, which lack global context and suffer from high computational overhead. We propose GRIT (Grid and Region-based Image captioning Transformer), a transformer-only architecture. By integrating grid and region features using a DETR-based detector, GRIT enables end-to-end training and out-performs prior methods in both inference accuracy and speed.
  Second, we tackle visual dialog, which requires multi-turn conversation about an image. The challenge lies in efficiently modeling interactions between multiple inputs (image, question, history). We introduce LTMI (Light-weight Transformer for Many Inputs). Utilizing a specialized attention block, an LTMI layer matches the representational power of a standard Transformer extension while utilizing less than one-tenth of its parameters, as validated on the VisDial dataset.
  Finally, we study interactive instruction-following for embodied AI using the ALFRED dataset. We propose a framework featuring a two-stage instruction interpretation: it first decodes language directives independently of visual context to predict a tentative action-object sequence, which is then fused with visual features for final execution. Using multiple egocentric views and hierarchical attention, our method accurately localizes objects and achieves a state-of-the-art unseen success rate of 8.37%.

</details>


### [10] [Soft Tuy-Completeness for Robust Projection Selection in Cone-Beam CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24023)
*Linda-Sophie Schneider,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连续软近正交评分和分辨率感知的饱和覆盖目标函数，用于感兴趣区域锥束CT的投影选择，基于Tuy完全性理论。通过将二值命中/未命中模型替换为可微的连续形式，在保持与可实现特征尺寸直接联系的同时，实现了高效优化。证明了底层离散决策问题是NP完全的，并提出了具有(1-1/e)近似保证的子模贪心算法和提供最优性保证的混合整数线性规划（MILP）。实验表明贪心算法与MILP的目标比值中位数为0.998，大量案例被证明全局最优。此外引入了有效空间分辨率（ESR）作为轨迹级诊断指标，可靠地关联重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Tuy完全性采用二值命中/未命中模型，无法精确捕捉投影采样的连续性质，且缺乏对感兴趣区域投影选择的可微优化框架。现有方法在投影选择问题上缺乏理论保证和计算可行性之间的平衡。

Method: 1) 提出连续软近正交评分和分辨率感知的饱和覆盖目标函数，将Tuy完全性推广为可微形式；2) 通过Set Cover的归约证明问题NP完全性；3) 设计子模贪心算法（(1-1/e)近似保证）和MILP（提供最优性证书）；4) 引入有效空间分辨率（ESR）作为轨迹级诊断指标。

Result: 在六个目标区域、多个投影预算和四种遮挡条件下的系统基准测试中，贪心算法与MILP的目标比值中位数为0.998，大量案例被证明全局最优。ESR与重建质量可靠相关。

Conclusion: 所提方法在理论上有严格的最优性保证，在实践中贪心算法几乎达到最优，ESR可作为无需重建的实用桥梁连接投影选择与图像域质量。

Abstract: This work introduces a continuous soft near-orthogonality score and a resolution-aware saturated coverage objective for projection selection in region-of-interest focused cone-beam CT, grounded in Tuy's completeness theory. Replacing the binary hit-or-miss model of classical Tuy completeness with a graded, differentiable formulation preserves a direct link to achievable feature sizes while enabling both efficient approximate and exact optimisation.
  We establish that the underlying discrete decision problems are NP-complete via polynomial-time reductions from Set Cover, motivating a submodular greedy algorithm with proven $(1-1/\mathrm{e})$ approximation guarantees and a mixed-integer linear program (MILP) that provides certified optimality bounds. The MILP serves as a quality certificate for the greedy solution rather than a competing optimiser.
  The primary empirical finding confirms this relationship: across a systematic benchmark spanning six target regions, multiple projection budgets, and four controlled occlusion conditions, the pooled median greedy-to-MILP objective ratio was 0.998, with a substantial fraction of cases certified globally optimal. A binary formulation is included as a diagnostic baseline; it strengthens hard directional completeness but is weaker on the continuous coverage scale.
  We additionally introduce Effective Spatial Resolution (ESR), a physically interpretable trajectory-level diagnostic that maps directional sampling gaps to achievable feature sizes. ESR correlates reliably with matched reconstruction quality across projection budgets and occlusion levels, providing a practical bridge between the selection stage and the image domain without requiring reconstruction.

</details>


### [11] [Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models via Causal Route Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24024)
*Zhe Cheng,Wenyu Chen,Fode Zhang,Dehuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、基于决策对齐的干预方法，通过分解注意力头中的视觉和文本路径并选择性抑制文本路径，有效减少LVLM的幻觉问题，仅带来少量推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM常产生流畅但与图像不符的幻觉内容，其根本原因在于文本路径主导了最终token决策，即使视觉token获得了注意力，解码器仍更倾向于遵循语言先验而非视觉证据。

Method: 提出训练无关的决策对齐干预方法：(1) 将每个注意力头分解为视觉路径和文本路径；(2) 利用一次前向/一次梯度近似高效估计token级路径效应；(3) 识别路径冲突中的先验主导头，选择性抑制文本路径而保留视觉路径。

Result: 在5个基准测试（涵盖判别式和生成式任务）上，该方法一致地减少了与幻觉相关的错误，对多模态整体性能影响有限，推理开销适度。

Conclusion: 通过分解注意力头路径并选择性抑制文本路径，可以在不重新训练模型的情况下有效缓解LVLM的幻觉问题，提升模型可靠性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate content that is fluent yet unsupported by the image, limiting their reliability in real-world deployment. We show that a key failure mode arises from route competition: even when visual tokens receive attention, the final token decision can be dominated by the textual pathway, causing the decoder to follow linguistic priors over visual evidence. To mitigate this, we propose a training-free, decision-aligned intervention that decomposes each attention head into a visual route and a text route, and estimates their token-level effects using an efficient one-forward/one-gradient approximation. These estimates reveal route conflict within heads and identify prior-dominant ones, enabling selective suppression of only the text route while keeping the visual route intact. Across five benchmarks spanning discriminative and generative settings, our method consistently reduces hallucination-related errors across models with limited impact on overall multimodal performance, while incurring a modest inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [12] [Towards Large Model Feature Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24025)
*Youwei Pang,Changsheng Gao,Dong Liu,Huchuan Lu,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对大模型特征编码（LaMoFC）的基准评测框架LaMoFCBench，涵盖4类16个场景、多种架构和分裂计算设置，揭示了现有特征编码范式与大模型异质特征之间的深层不匹配，为驱动范式转变提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在实际部署中受限于计算/内存预算和隐私需求，分裂执行虽可缓解这些问题，但会引入中间特征的大量传输与存储。现有特征编码方法主要针对CNN的同质空间激活图，而大模型产生的是具有不同统计分布和压缩容限的异质特征（如多级/多模态表示、自回归上下文缓存），亟需将大模型特征编码作为基础系统组件并建立系统化评估框架。

Method: 1) 构建特征数据集LaMoFCBench，覆盖4类任务、16种场景，集成广泛使用的架构和多种分裂计算设置；2) 根据实际应用场景指定代表性分裂点以提取中间特征，建立统一的可重复比较管线；3) 对主流通用特征编码器进行基准测试，评估其与大模型异质特征的匹配程度。

Result: 现有主流通用特征编解码器与大模型特征的异质性质之间存在深层不匹配，表明LaMoFC需要从根本上脱离现有编码范式。

Conclusion: LaMoFCBench为驱动大模型特征编码的范式转变提供了共享的实证基础，数据和代码将开源。

Abstract: Large models have delivered remarkable performance across a wide range of perception and generation tasks, yet practical deployment is increasingly constrained by computational and memory budgets, as well as privacy requirements. Split execution alleviates these constraints by partitioning computation across devices, but it inevitably introduces intensive transmission and storage of intermediate features. Unlike conventional feature coding for CNNs that typically targets homogeneous spatial activation maps, modern large models generate heterogeneous features with varying statistical distributions and compression tolerances, e.g., multi-level/multi-modal representations and autoregressive context caches. These characteristics necessitate treating large model feature coding (LaMoFC) as a fundamental system component and call for a systematic evaluation framework. In this paper, we present a comprehensive benchmark and evaluation framework for LaMoFC. We first build the feature dataset LaMoFCBench, covering diverse task requirements across 4 categories and 16 scenarios while integrating widelyadopted architectures and various split-computing settings. We then specify representative split points according to practical application scenarios to extract intermediate features, establishing a unified pipeline for fair and reproducible comparisons. Finally, we benchmark mainstream universal feature codecs, exposing the profound misalignment between existing coding paradigms and the heterogeneous nature of large model features. These findings reveal that LaMoFC demands a fundamental departure from existing paradigms, and LaMoFCBench provides the shared empirical foundation to drive this transition. The data and code will be available at https://github.com/lartpang/LaMoFCBench.

</details>


### [13] [Mode-as-Sequence: Translating Multimodal Motion Prediction into Unified Sequential Mode Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24037)
*Zikang Zhou,Haibo Hu,Xinhong Chen,Yifan Zhang,Nan Guan,Yung-Hui Li,Chun Jason Xue,Jianping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mode-as-Sequence框架，将无序的模态预测转化为有序的模态序列，通过显式建模模态间依赖关系来解决多模态轨迹预测中的模式坍塌和置信度校准问题。在此框架下设计了ModeSeq（递归解码）和Parallel ModeSeq（并行解码）两种实现，结合Early-Match-Take-All损失函数和排序正则化器，在Waymo等多个大规模基准上取得了SOTA性能，并在两项竞赛中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态轨迹预测面临标签稀疏（每个场景只有一个真实未来轨迹）导致的模式坍塌（假设冗余、覆盖不足）和置信度排序不可靠问题。

Method: 1. 提出Mode-as-Sequence框架，将无序模态集转化为有序模态序列，显式建模模态间依赖关系。2. ModeSeq：递归解码，每个模态基于之前生成的模态生成。3. Parallel ModeSeq：通过掩码模态间自注意力实现因果依赖，一次前向传播并行解码所有模态。4. 提出Early-Match-Take-All (EMTA)及其联合场景扩展MA-EMTA损失函数，以及轻量级排序正则化器。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset挑战中，ModeSeq获得2024年LiDAR-free运动预测赛道第一名，Parallel ModeSeq获得2025年Interaction Prediction Challenge第一名。在多个大规模基准上，排序指标和Best-of-K准确率均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: Mode-as-Sequence框架通过将模态预测建模为序列化依赖关系，有效缓解了多模态轨迹预测中的模式坍塌问题，同时提升了置信度校准质量。并行版本在保持因果依赖的同时实现了高效的大K值推理和可扩展的联合场景预测。

Abstract: Multimodal motion forecasting is inherently under-supervised: each training scene provides only one realized future, yet multiple plausible futures exist. This sparse supervision often leads to mode collapse (redundant hypotheses and insufficient mode coverage) and unreliable confidence ranking when predicting a small set of trajectories. We propose Mode-as-Sequence, a unified decoding framework that translates an unordered mode set into an ordered mode sequence and explicitly models mode-to-mode dependency. Under this framework, we develop two complementary instantiations. ModeSeq performs recurrent mode decoding, where each mode is generated conditioned on the previously generated modes, encouraging diverse, non-redundant hypotheses with calibrated confidence ordering. To remove the mode-by-mode autoregressive bottleneck, we further propose Parallel ModeSeq, which preserves the same causal dependency using masked mode-to-mode self-attention while decoding all modes in a single forward pass, enabling efficient large-$K$ inference and scalable joint-scene prediction. To learn representative modes and calibrated confidence under sparse labels, we introduce Early-Match-Take-All (EMTA) and its joint-scene extension MA-EMTA, together with a lightweight ranking regularizer that reduces confidence inversions. Extensive experiments on large-scale benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in both ranking-oriented metrics and best-of-K accuracy across datasets, horizons, and object types. In the Waymo Open Dataset challenges, ModeSeq achieves 1st place in the 2024 LiDAR-free motion prediction track, and Parallel ModeSeq achieves 1st place in the 2025 Interaction Prediction Challenge, validating the effectiveness of Mode-as-Sequence for both accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [14] [Learning to See Like Humans: Gaze-Aligned Cycling Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24040)
*Luís Maria Perdigão,Miguel Costa,Carlos Santiago,Manuel Marques*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EG-PCS框架，通过集成眼动追踪数据监督视觉Transformer的注意力机制，使模型学习到的注意力图更符合人类注视模式，在保持排序性能的同时提升了感知安全预测的准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于街景图像比较的骑行安全感知学习方法未能显式建模人类视觉注意力机制，而注意力在人类安全感知中起核心作用。

Method: 提出EG-PCS框架，将眼动追踪数据集成到基于视觉Transformer的成对学习流程中，通过眼动信号监督模型的注意力机制，使学习到的注意力图与人类注视模式对齐。

Result: 眼动引导模型在排名性能上与现有最先进方法相当，同时生成的注意力图能更准确地反映人类视觉注意行为。

Conclusion: 融入眼动追踪信息能提升基于感知的城市分析中预测的准确性和模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Cycling delivers significant public-health and environmental benefits, yet its uptake in cities is often limited by perceived safety. When street environments appear unsafe, individuals are less likely to cycle, making perception a key barrier to adoption. Recent work has shown that pairwise comparisons of street-view images provide a scalable way to learn subjective safety judgments. However, existing approaches do not explicitly model human visual attention, which plays a central role in how humans perceive safety. We propose an Eye-Tracking-Guided Perceived Cycling Safety framework (EG-PCS) that integrates gaze data into a pairwise learning pipeline based on vision transformers. By supervising the model's attention mechanism with eye-tracking signals, we encourage alignment between learned attention maps and human fixation patterns. Experiments show that gaze-guided models achieve similar ranking performance compared to state-of-the-art approaches while producing attention maps that more accurately reflect human visual attention behavior. Our results demonstrate that incorporating eye-tracking information enhances both predictive accuracy and interpretability in perception-based urban analytics.

</details>


### [15] [WideDepth: Millimeter-Accurate Benchmark for Fisheye Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24074)
*Ilia Indyk,Ignat Penshin,Ivan Sosin,Maxim Monastyrny,Aleksei Valenkov,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了WideDepth——首个面向鱼眼相机深度估计的室内数据集，包含101个场景、5K高分辨率立体对及毫米级真实深度/视差标注，并配套了鱼眼立体生成流水线和模型适配方法，在多个SOTA模型上进行了全面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼眼相机在机器人近场操作、导航和沉浸式感知中应用日益广泛，但缺乏具有精确真实值的室内深度基准数据集，制约了相关研究的发展。

Method: 1) 构建包含101个场景、5K高分辨率立体对的WideDepth数据集，提供毫米级深度和视差真值；2) 提出将针孔训练的两眼立体模型适配到鱼眼图像的方法；3) 提出基于高分辨率激光雷达扫描的立体鱼眼图像生成流水线；4) 提供18K激光雷达导出的稀疏深度训练样本。

Result: 在鱼眼数据上对单目深度、立体匹配和深度补全等SOTA模型进行了全面评估，微调针孔立体模型后性能提升高达62%。

Conclusion: WideDepth的高精度和多功能性为鱼眼深度估计和机器人感知研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Fisheye cameras are increasingly adopted in robotics for near-field manipulation, navigation, and immersive perception, yet indoor depth benchmarks with accurate ground truth are still missing. To address this, we introduce WideDepth - the first indoor dataset for fisheye depth estimation, featuring 101 scenes containing 5K high-resolution stereo pairs labeled with millimeter-level ground truth depth and disparity. Our dataset also includes paired pinhole and fisheye samples across varying fields of view and baselines in both horizontal and vertical stereo setups. We further propose a method to adapt pinhole-trained stereo models to fisheye images and introduce a novel stereo fisheye image generation pipeline based on high-resolution LiDAR scans. Leveraging these methods, we thoroughly evaluate state-of-the-art monocular depth, stereo matching, and depth completion models on our benchmark. Additionally, we provide 18K LiDAR-derived sparse depth training samples, achieving up to a 62% performance boost on fisheye data when fine-tuning pinhole-based stereo models. In summary, the high precision and versatility of our benchmark set a strong foundation for advancing research in fisheye depth estimation and robotics perception. Project page: https://ilyaind.github.io/WideDepth

</details>


### [16] [COSY: Compositional 3DGS Synthesis for Disentangled Human Head Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24114)
*Florian Barthel,Shalini De Mello,Koki Nagano,Wieland Morgenstern,Anna Hilsmann,Peter Eisert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3DGS-GAN人像生成中语义属性控制困难的问题，本文提出了一种独立组件式生成器架构，利用稀疏颜色信息（而非分割掩码或几何先验）实现头发、皮肤、眼镜等区域的完全解耦编辑，并通过上下文令牌共享最小化的形状与光照信息，在保持视觉质量的同时实现了更优的解耦性与精确编辑控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-GAN生成的人像在身份和外观上具有多样性，但其隐空间高度纠缠，导致语义属性编辑（如发色、眼镜）时产生非预期的身份或外观改变；已有的后训练解耦方法无法保证完全解耦且依赖预训练分类器。

Method: 提出独立组件式生成器架构，为头发、皮肤、眼镜、躯干等各区域生成独立的3DGS表示，仅使用稀疏颜色信息（如发色、肤色）实现区域分离，无需分割掩码或几何先验；引入上下文令牌在各独立生成器间共享最小化形状与光照信息，确保编辑时的一致性。

Result: 相比现有基于GAN的生成与编辑方法，该方法在解耦性、编辑精度上表现更优，视觉质量具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过独立组件生成与稀疏颜色条件控制，实现了人像语义属性的精确解耦编辑，避免了传统方法中隐空间纠缠和额外标注依赖的问题。

Abstract: Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) GANs for human heads synthesize and render photorealistic 3D models in real-time and offer a vast variety in identity and appearance. However, controlling specific semantic attributes such as hair color or glasses remains challenging, as edits in the entangled latent space often induce unintended changes in identity or appearance. Although there are several methods that aim to disentangle the latent space post training by estimating directions that only modify certain features, these methods cannot guarantee complete disentanglement and often require pre-trained classifiers. In our approach, we propose a new generator architecture that synthesizes components, such as hair, skin, glasses, and torso, completely independently. This allows for changing the latent vector for one region while keeping the remaining parts fixed. Further, we achieve this separation using only sparse information such as the hair or skin color, eliminating the requirement of segmentation masks or geometric priors, often seen in prior work. To ensure matching shape and lighting conditions during editing, we allow minimal shared information via context tokens between the independent generators. These tokens even allow us to control the shape and light, without any prior annotation. Compared to existing works on GAN-based generation and editing, our method shows better disentanglement, more precise editing control, and competitive visual quality.

</details>


### [17] [ImPartial: Multi-channel Whole-Cell Segmentation using Partial Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24128)
*Gunjan Shrivastava,Saad Nadeem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ImPartial是一种基于深度学习的框架，通过自监督多通道量化插补和稀疏涂鸦标注，在病理图像细胞分割中仅需少量标注即可达到与全监督模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理图像中准确的细胞分割通常需要密集的像素级标注，成本高昂且耗时；新兴生物成像模态和多通道数据集缺乏专家标注数据，亟需低标注量下的高效分割方法。

Method: 采用稀疏涂鸦标注和有限监督，通过自监督多通道量化插补增强分割目标；引入自监督分类目标（而非像素级重建或去噪），更好地对齐分割任务。

Result: 在多重细胞成像和单重临床亮场免疫组化数据集上，ImPartial以远少于全监督模型的标注量达到与之相当的性能，且一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了病理图像分割对密集标注的依赖，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Accurate cell segmentation in pathology images typically requires dense pixel-wise annotations, which are costly and time-consuming to obtain. This challenge is especially important for emerging biological imaging modalities and multiplexed datasets with variable channel configurations, where expert-labeled data are scarce. In this work, we introduce ImPartial, a deep learning framework designed to achieve state-of-the-art segmentation performance in low-annotation regimes using sparse scribbles and limited supervision. ImPartial augments the segmentation objective via self-supervised multi-channel quantized imputation. This approach leverages the observation that perfect pixel-wise reconstruction or denoising of the image is not needed for accurate segmentation, and thus, introduces a self-supervised classification objective that better aligns with the overall segmentation goal. We demonstrate that ImPartial achieves performance at par with fully supervised models while requiring substantially fewer annotations. Extensive experiments on benchmark multiplexed cellular imaging and single-plex clinical brightfield immunohistochemistry datasets show consistent improvements over strong baselines with only partial annotations. All benchmark datasets and code are available via our Github: https://github.com/nadeemlab/ImPartial.

</details>


### [18] [EchoVQA: Enabling Conversational Assistance for Point-of-Care Cardiac Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24159)
*Filippos Bellos,Yutong Li,Jessie N Dong,Zaiyang Guo,Emily Mackay,Yayuan Li,Yannis Avrithis,Alison Pouch,Jason J. Corso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了EchoVQA，首个大规模超声心动图VQA数据集，包含14,299张图像和74,819个问答对，涵盖高质量和次优图像及采集指导问题，并开发了基于多模态可学习提示的参数高效方法，在多个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超声心动图VQA数据集规模有限、仅包含高质量图像且视图种类少，无法满足实际床旁超声检查中图像质量参差不齐、操作者经验不足的需求，缺乏采集指导能力。

Method: 构建了EchoVQA数据集，整合EchoNet-Dynamic、CAMUS等公开源以及使用Lumify和Clarius两款手持探头采集的床旁数据；开发了基于多模态可学习提示的参数高效方法。

Result: 数据集含14,299张图像和74,819个问答对，涵盖多种视图和质量等级；提出的方法在包括EchoVQA在内的大多数基准上达到最优性能，且可训练参数显著少于现有方法。

Conclusion: EchoVQA填补了超声心动图VQA领域大规模、多样化数据集的空白，采集指导问题的引入为新手操作者提供了实用帮助，参数高效方法展示了良好的性能与效率平衡。

Abstract: Point-of-care transthoracic echocardiography (TTE) enables cardiac assessment in virtually any clinical setting, yet its diagnostic utility remains constrained by the expertise required for image acquisition and interpretation. Visual question answering (VQA) offers a promising paradigm for bridging this expertise gap through interactive clinical assistance, but existing echocardiography VQA datasets are limited in scale, restricted to high-quality images, and only cover a few views. We introduce EchoVQA, the first large-scale VQA dataset for echocardiography, comprising 14,299 images and 74,819 question-answer pairs. The dataset integrates public sources (EchoNet-Dynamic, CAMUS) with our own point-of-care acquisitions from two handheld probes (Lumify, Clarius), spanning diverse views and including both high-quality and suboptimal images. Uniquely, EchoVQA includes acquisition guidance questions to help users optimize transducer positioning toward a diagnostic apical 4-chamber view for left ventricular ejection fraction estimation -- a challenging task for novice operators in point-of-care settings. We further develop a parameter-efficient method based on multimodal learnable prompts achieving state-of-the-art performance on most benchmarks, including EchoVQA, with significantly less trainable parameters than existing state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [19] [Loki: Representation over Architecture for Diffusion-Based Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24176)
*Pouyan Navard,Sernam Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loki提出了一种基于参数化面部模型（而非RGB）提取驱动表情和姿态的方法，通过将身份与表情/姿态解耦，实现了更轻量、无需跨ID训练数据的面部动画生成，在减少43%推理参数、使用1496倍更少训练视频的情况下，在两项直接衡量驱动跟随度的新指标上达到领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散-based面部动画系统通过堆叠独立训练的模块分别处理表情、姿态和身份，导致参数量大、依赖专有数据集，且各控制轴之间存在残余纠缠。问题的根源在于从RGB中学习表情和姿态——这种表示中身份、姿态和表情天然耦合，需要大量数据和解耦学习才能分离。

Method: Loki的关键创新在于不在条件路径上使用RGB，而是使用参数化面部模型（如3DMM）编码驱动者的表情和头部姿态，该模型的参数轴与身份正交（即天然解耦）。然后将这些参数栅格化为空间图，作为扩散主干的原生输入。身份信息则通过轻量级的键值注入（KV injection）单独送入扩散主干预训练的特征中。推理时，跨身份复现只需替换系数即可。

Result: Loki比领先的扩散基线少约43%的推理参数，训练数据减少1496倍视频样本。在两项直接衡量生成视频是否跟随驱动者头部姿态轨迹和面部表情的新指标上，Loki达到领先或并列领先水平。

Conclusion: 从RGB中学习表情和姿态是现有方法复杂性的根源；改用参数化面部模型作为条件信号，可以在极低数据和参数开销下实现更好的解耦控制。该工作还定义了两个直接对齐任务目标的评估指标。

Abstract: Portrait animation transfers a driver clip's facial expression and head pose onto a single reference image while preserving the reference's identity. State-of-the-art diffusion systems address this by stacking trained modules for expression, pose, and identity in turn, paying for it in trainable parameters, proprietary corpora, and residual entanglement between the very axes the system is meant to control independently. This complexity compensates for an upstream choice -- learning facial expression and head pose from RGB, a representation in which identity, pose, and expression are inseparable without being learned apart. Loki steps out of RGB on the conditioning path. Driver expression and head pose are encoded by a face model whose parameter axes are identity-orthogonal by construction, then rasterised into a spatial map that the diffusion backbone consumes natively. Identity is routed separately through the diffusion backbone's own pretrained features via lightweight key-value injection. Because the parametric representation factorises identity from expression and pose, cross ID reenactment reduces to a coefficient substitution at inference, requiring no cross ID training data. Loki requires ~43% fewer inference parameters than leading diffusion baselines and trained on 1496x less video samples. We define two metrics that directly measure whether the generated head pose trajectory and facial expression followed the driver's -- the questions portrait animation actually asks; Loki leads or co-leads on both.

</details>


### [20] [Single View Seafloor Recovery from Imaging Sonar via Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24195)
*Sevan Brodjian,Michael Hobley,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的、基于可微分渲染的单视图声纳测深恢复方法，能在30秒内从单张前视成像声纳图像中恢复水下地形高度场，且仅需已知海床倾斜角作为条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下地形测绘方法需要多视角观测、昂贵的多传感器配置或大量训练数据，限制了其在新环境中的适应性和实用性。声纳成像中存在垂直结构被压缩的歧义性，使得三维重建极具挑战。

Method: 基于可微分声纳光线追踪，显式优化高度场以重现目标声纳图像，利用已知海床倾斜角作为先验条件约束优化过程，无需任何训练数据。

Result: 在合成数据集上，该方法在分布偏移场景下优于有监督CNN，在粗糙地形上与CNN性能接近；而CNN仅在同分布数据上占优。

Conclusion: 通过建模声纳过程的物理先验，该方法无需训练数据即可跨传感器配置和环境迁移，为单视图声纳三维重建提供了高效且自适应的新范式。

Abstract: Sonar is often the only modality suitable for high-resolution imaging underwater due to light attenuation and turbidity. Forward-looking imaging sonar provides measurements over range and horizontal angle but collapses vertical structure into a flat image, creating ambiguities that make 3D recovery challenging. A common use case for imaging sonar is underwater terrain mapping (bathymetry), yet current methods require many views, expensive multi-sensor setups, or significant training data, which limits use and adaptability to new environments.
  We present a training-free method that recovers bathymetry from a single sonar image in under 30 seconds via differentiable rendering, conditioned on a known seafloor tilt. To our knowledge, this is the first differentiable rendering approach for single-view height recovery in sonar. Our method implements differentiable sonar ray tracing and optimizes an explicit height field to reproduce the target image. On synthetic datasets, our approach outperforms a supervised CNN under distribution shift and remains close on rough terrain, while the CNN wins in-distribution. By modeling physically grounded priors of the sonar process, our method adapts across sensor configurations and environments without training data.

</details>


### [21] [Radiuma: A Unified Zero-Code Executable Graphical Workflow Generator for Reproducible and Shareable Medical Image Analysis and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24201)
*Mohammad Salmanpour,Mehrdad Oveisi,Isaac Shiri,Arman Rahmim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Radiuma是一个免费、模块化的医学影像分析平台，集成图像读取、可视化、配准、分割、放射组学特征提取和机器学习模块，支持可视化工作流构建，无需编程即可创建可重复的分析流程，适用于多模态医学影像研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学影像计算软件缺乏标准化、用户友好且可复现的环境，限制了高级医学影像分析工作流的广泛采用。

Method: Radiuma采用模块化设计，集成图像读取、可视化、配准、融合、处理、分割、放射组学特征提取及机器学习（分类、回归、聚类）模块，通过可视化工作流系统连接各模块，支持独立执行或串联成多步骤流水线。

Result: 每个模块的结果可直接在可视化窗口检查，支持保存和共享自定义工作流，无需编程即可创建可执行、可复现的多步骤流水线。

Conclusion: Radiuma通过结合灵活性、易用性和标准化分析工具，为放射组学和机器学习研究提供了实用的环境，适用于放射科医生、物理师、临床医生和数据科学家等不同背景的用户。

Abstract: Medical image computing software is essential for identifying imaging biomarkers that can support diagnosis, prognosis, treatment planning, and clinical research. However, the lack of standardized, user-friendly, and reproducible software environments has limited the broader adoption of advanced medical image analysis workflows. We present Radiuma, a freely available modular platform designed to support reliable and reproducible medical image analysis across multiple modalities and file formats. Radiuma integrates image reading, visualization, registration, fusion, processing, segmentation, radiomics feature extraction, and machine learning modules for classification, regression, and clustering. Its modular design allows users to execute each component independently or connect modules through a visual workflow system, where the output of one step can be graphically passed to the next. This enables the creation of custom, executable, and reproducible multi-step pipelines without requiring extensive programming expertise. Results from each module can be inspected directly in the visualization window, providing immediate feedback on processing quality and workflow accuracy. Radiuma also supports saving and sharing customized workflows, promoting transparency, reusability, and consistency across collaborative studies. By combining flexibility, usability, and standardized analysis tools, Radiuma provides a practical environment for radiomics and machine learning research in clinical and translational settings. The platform is designed to be accessible to users with diverse expertise, including radiologists, physicists, clinicians, and data scientists.

</details>


### [22] [CRISP -- Clustering-Based Redundancy-Reduced Instance Sampling for Pathology Case Representation and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24253)
*Zahra Rahimi Afzal,Wataru Uegami,Saghir Alfasly,Saba Yasir,Judy C. Boughey,Matthew P. Goetz,Krishna R. Kalari,H. R. Tizhoosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRISP，一种无监督的两阶段框架，通过聚类降采样从病例内所有WSI中选择代表性图像块构建病例级表征，避免了人工选择单张切片的局限性，在乳腺癌数据集的检索任务中达到或超越现有标准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字病理分析方法大多依赖病理学家单张切片选择，忽略了多张WSI中分布的肿瘤异质性信息，尚无针对多WSI病例级别的自动化分析框架。

Method: 提出CRISP两阶段框架：第一阶段在单张WSI内进行冗余降采样；第二阶段基于聚类方法从所有WSI中采样出紧凑且具代表性的图像块集合，构建病例级表征并直接用于检索索引。

Result: 在两个Mayo Clinic乳腺癌数据集（诊断和治疗规划）上，CRISP在患者/病例检索任务中一致达到或超越现有模型与病理学家联合选择切片的基准方法。

Conclusion: CRISP实现了病例级自动化处理，消除了主观切片选择，能够挖掘当前被忽视的跨多张WSI分布的临床相关信息。

Abstract: Digital pathology archives increasingly contain multiple whole-slide images (WSIs) per case, capturing spatially distinct tumour regions and reflecting intrinsic morphological heterogeneity. However, most existing approaches rely on a single pathologist-selected slide, thereby discarding potentially informative evidence distributed across the remaining WSIs. To date, no autonomous framework has been proposed for comprehensive multi-WSI case processing. Here, we present an unsupervised framework for case-level analysis that integrates information from all available slides within a case. Rather than relying on a single designated slide, the proposed approach constructs case-level representations by selectively distilling informative patches across WSIs. We introduce Clustering-Based Redundancy-Reduced Instance Sampling for Pathology (CRISP), a two-stage framework that first reduces redundancy within individual WSIs and subsequently applies clustering-based sampling to select a compact yet representative set of patches for the entire case. The resulting patch set captures case-level heterogeneity while avoiding exhaustive processing of gigapixel images, and directly serves as a retrieval index. Using two Mayo Clinic breast cancer datasets for diagnosis and treatment planning, we demonstrate that CRISP consistently matches or surpasses the current standard practice of combined model and pathologist slide selection for patient/case search and retrieval. By automating case-level processing and eliminating subjective WSI selection, CRISP potentially enables the exploitation of clinically relevant information distributed across multiple WSIs that is currently overlooked.

</details>


### [23] [Plume Segmentation from MethaneSAT with Cross-Sensor Transfer Learning and Physics-Informed Postprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24273)
*Manuel Pérez-Carrasco,Maya Nasr,Zhan Zhang,Apisada Chulakadabba,Javier Roger,Raia Ottenheimer,Sébastien Roche,Maryann Sargent,Chris Chan Miller,Daniel Varon,Jack Warren,Luis Guanter,Kang Sun,Jonathan Franklin,Jia Chen,Cecilia Garraffo,Xiong Liu,Ritesh Gautam,Steven Wofsy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mask R-CNN的机器学习框架，用于从MethaneSAT卫星图像中自动检测和掩蔽单个甲烷羽流。通过跨传感器迁移学习（从MethaneAIR预训练权重微调）解决标注数据稀缺问题，并设计了物理信息后处理管线，提供高灵敏度（精度0.71，召回0.94）和高精度（精度0.92，召回0.70）两种运行模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像中甲烷羽流的自动检测与掩蔽对于排放归因和量化至关重要，但面临MethaneSAT标注数据稀缺以及需要在多样的大气和地表条件下保持推理可靠性的双重挑战。

Method: 采用Mask R-CNN（ResNet-50骨干）进行实例分割，优于U-Net语义分割；通过从MethaneAIR预训练权重微调实现跨传感器迁移学习；设计物理信息后处理管线，包含高灵敏度模式（形态学滤波+邻近合并）和高精度模式（额外分布分类器）。

Result: Mask R-CNN在MethaneAIR和MethaneSAT数据上像素级F1分别提升10.49和5.48；迁移学习后实例级精度0.60、召回0.98；高灵敏度模式精度0.71、召回0.94；高精度模式精度0.92、召回0.70。

Conclusion: 该框架有效实现了MethaneSAT甲烷羽流的自动化检测，两种运行模式分别适用于全面排放筛查和可信源归因。部分被标记为假阳性的检测实际对应真实甲烷增强，表明报告的精度值是真实检测性能的下界。

Abstract: Automated detection and masking of individual methane plumes from satellite imagery is important for operational emission attribution and quantification. We present a machine learning framework for plume detection from MethaneSAT retrieved column-averaged dry-air mole fractions of methane. We address two core challenges: the scarcity of labeled MethaneSAT data and the need for inference reliability across diverse atmospheric and surface conditions. We first demonstrate that Mask R-CNN with a ResNet-50 backbone outperforms U-Net semantic segmentation on both MethaneAIR (an airborne version of MethaneSAT) and MethaneSAT data, with pixel-level F1 score gains of 10.49 and 5.48 respectively. To address MethaneSAT data scarcity, we evaluate three cross-sensor transfer strategies leveraging MethaneAIR flights and synthetic plumes. Mask R-CNN with ResNet-50 fine-tuned from MethaneAIR pre-trained weights is the most effective strategy, achieving instance-level precision of 0.60 and a near-perfect recall of 0.98 at the baseline operating point. A physics-informed post-processing pipeline converts detections into two operationally distinct modes. The first is a high-sensitivity mode that applies morphological filtering and proximity-based merging for comprehensive emission screening, achieving precision of 0.71 and recall of 0.94. The second is a high-precision mode that additionally applies a distribution-based classifier for confident source attribution, achieving precision of 0.92 and recall of 0.70. Manual review of detections classified as false positives against our wavelet-based ground truth labels reveals that a meaningful fraction of cases correspond to real methane enhancements excluded by conservative labeling criteria, indicating that precision values reported are lower bounds on true detection performance... Our data and code are available at: https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/FR959H

</details>


### [24] [ArtSplat: Feed-Forward Articulated 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Multi-State Uncalibrated Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24304)
*Inseo Lee,Yoonji Kim,Eugene Sohn,Jiwoong Lee,Jungmin You,Joonseok Lee,Jin-Hwa Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtSplat 是首个前馈式框架，可从稀疏多视角图像中同时重建铰接物体的几何结构和关节参数，速度比基线方法快400倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 NeRF 和 3DGS 的铰接物体重建方法依赖密集视图或强先验（如深度图、关节类型、预定义关节数），且需要逐个物体进行昂贵的优化，缺乏高效的前馈式解决方案。

Method: 提出逐像素关节图表示将关节参数估计集成到前馈流水线中；设计带有状态令牌的跨状态注意力机制，有效捕捉输入状态间的离散运动。

Result: 在 PartNet-Mobility 的68个铰接物体（含单关节和多关节配置）上，几何和关节估计均达到竞争性能，推理速度比基线快400倍以上。

Conclusion: ArtSplat 首次实现了铰接物体的单次前馈式3D高斯重建，在保持高质量重建的同时大幅提升了效率。

Abstract: Articulated object reconstruction from sparse-view images is an ill-posed problem that requires simultaneous inference of geometry and underlying articulation structure. Existing methods for articulated object reconstruction based on NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) typically rely on dense views or strong priors (e.g., depth maps, joint types, predefined number of joints) and require costly per-object optimization. In this paper, we propose ArtSplat, the first feed-forward framework for articulated 3D Gaussian Splatting. It reconstructs both geometry and joint parameters from sparse multi-view images across multiple articulation states in a single forward pass. To address the challenges of single-pass articulated reconstruction, we introduce a per-pixel joint map representation that enables the integration of joint parameter estimation into the feed-forward pipeline. We further propose a Cross-State Attention (CSA) mechanism with state tokens, which effectively captures discrete motion across input states. Experiments on 68 articulated objects from PartNet-Mobility, including both single- and multi-joint configurations, demonstrate that ArtSplat achieves competitive performance in both geometry and joint estimation, while being over 400 times faster than baselines.

</details>


### [25] [CoDA: Color Distribution Probing for Efficient and Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24306)
*Zexi Jia,Zhiqiang Yuan,Xiaoyue Duan,Jinchao Zhang,Jie Zhou,Anil K. Jain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FakeForm基准（37万图、62领域）和CoDA检测器（148万参数），通过颜色分布探测实现高效且跨域鲁棒的AI生成图像检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测面临泛化性与效率的权衡：轻量级方法在未见过的生成器或域上性能下降，而大规模模型计算成本高；现有基准主要关注跨模型评估，跨域鲁棒性未被充分探索。

Method: 提出CoDA检测器，基于噪声量化探针对颜色分布进行探测，利用真实照片颜色模式更平滑稳定、合成图像存在颜色不平衡的观察，仅需148万参数。

Result: CoDA在标准基准上达到SOTA，在FakeForm跨域评估中取得最佳结果，在跨模型逼真设置中保持强竞争力。

Conclusion: 持续的生成伪影可为高效鲁棒的AI生成图像检测提供实用基础，颜色分布探测是有效的互补线索。

Abstract: AI-generated image detection faces a persistent trade-off between generalization and efficiency: lightweight artifact-based methods often degrade on unseen generators or domains, whereas more robust large-scale models are computationally expensive. Meanwhile, existing benchmarks mainly focus on cross-model evaluation in photorealistic settings, leaving cross-domain robustness underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce FakeForm, a large-scale benchmark with approximately 370,000 images across 62 diverse domains for both cross-model and cross-domain evaluation. Motivated by this broader setting, we revisit color-distribution probing as an efficient complementary cue for AI-generated image detection. We observe that, especially for photographic content, real photographs tend to exhibit smoother and more stable color patterns, whereas synthetic images often show characteristic color imbalances introduced by neural generation. Based on this observation, we propose CoDA, a compact 1.48M-parameter detector built on a Noise-Quantization Probe, together with a theoretical analysis linking probe responses to color non-uniformity. Experiments show that CoDA achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks and the best results on the challenging cross-domain evaluation of FakeForm, while remaining highly competitive in cross-model photorealistic settings. These results suggest that persistent generative artifacts can provide a practical foundation for efficient and robust AI-generated image detection. The models and FakeForm benchmark will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [26] [Unified 3D Scene Understanding Through Physical World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24321)
*Wanhee Lee,Klemen Kotar,Rahul Mysore Venkatesh,Jared Watrous,Honglin Chen,Khai Loong Aw,Daniel L. K. Yamins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3WM，一个统一的物理世界模型，将3D场景理解中的多种任务（深度估计、新视角合成、物体操作）整合到一个概率图模型中，通过不同的推理路径实现零样本任务泛化，并在多个任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将3D视觉任务（深度估计、新视角合成、物体操作等）孤立处理，无法共享统一表示或跨任务迁移知识。作者希望构建一个统一模型，将不同任务从独立的训练目标简化为不同的提示，实现跨数据集的联合训练。

Method: 将3WM形式化为概率图模型，节点代表多模态场景元素（RGB、光流、相机位姿等），不同的推理路径对应不同任务：新视角合成（RGB+密集光流）、物体操作（RGB+稀疏光流）、深度估计（RGB+相机条件）。所有任务零样本完成，无需特定任务微调。

Result: 3WM在无需微调的情况下超越专门化基线模型，在新视角合成和3D物体操作任务上达到SOTA，具备精确可控性、强几何一致性和真实场景鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 统一模型可以作为碎片化任务特定系统的实用替代方案，支持可组合推理路径（如在3D环境中移动物体进行导航），向通用视觉世界模型迈进一步。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes requires flexible combinations of visual reasoning tasks, including depth estimation, novel view synthesis, and object manipulation, all of which are essential for perception and interaction. Existing approaches have typically addressed these tasks in isolation, preventing them from sharing a common representation or transferring knowledge across tasks. A conceptually simpler but practically non-trivial alternative is to unify these diverse tasks into a single model, reducing different tasks from separate training objectives to merely different prompts and allowing for joint training across all datasets. In this work, we present a physical world model for unified 3D understanding and interaction (3WM), formulated as a probabilistic graphical model in which nodes represent multimodal scene elements such as RGB, optical flow, and camera pose. Diverse tasks emerge from different inference pathways through the graph: novel view synthesis from RGB and dense flow prompts, object manipulation from RGB and sparse flow prompts, and depth estimation from RGB and camera conditioning, all zero-shot without task-specific training. 3WM outperforms specialized baselines without the need for finetuning by offering precise controllability, strong geometric consistency, and robustness in real-world scenarios, achieving state-of-the-art performance on NVS and 3D object manipulation. Beyond predefined tasks, the model supports composable inference pathways, such as moving objects aside while navigating a 3D environment, enabling complex geometric reasoning. This demonstrates that a unified model can serve as a practical alternative to fragmented task-specific systems, taking a step towards a general-purpose visual world model.

</details>


### [27] [Causal Physics Steering in Video World Models via Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24322)
*Nahid Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的物理学引导方法（physics steering），通过在VideoMAE模型的物理学涌现区（PEZ）注入概念激活向量（CAV），在不改变模型权重的情况下，可逆地调控模型对物理合理性的判断，证明了视频世界模型中的物理推理不仅可读，而且可直接操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型能够学习物理动态的表征，但如何在推理时控制其物理期望仍是一个开放问题。尽管已有研究发现VideoMAE中存在物理学涌现区（PEZ），其中物理合理性与其他视觉特征分离表示，但尚不清楚能否利用这一结构直接控制模型的物理推理。

Method: 提出physics steering方法：利用PEZ层线性探头的权重向量作为概念激活向量（CAV），在推理时将其注入隐藏状态，通过改变注入的正负符号来调控模型的物理期望判断，整个过程无需训练或修改模型权重。

Result: 在IntPhys基准测试上，该方法能够可靠地沿两个方向改变模型的物理合理性判断；该效果仅在PEZ层内进行干预时才出现；进一步发现物理学表征与运动方向分离编码，且不同的直观物理原理在表征空间中占据不同的方向。

Conclusion: VideoMAE中的物理推理不仅是可读的（可被解释），而且是可直接操控的（可通过CAV注入进行引导），为控制视频世界模型的物理期望提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Video world models learn representations of physical dynamics, but controlling their physical expectations at inference time remains an open problem. Recent interpretability work identified a Physics Emergence Zone (PEZ), a group of middle transformer layers in VideoMAE where physical plausibility is represented separately from other visual features. However, it remained unclear whether this structure could be used to directly control the model's physics reasoning. We present physics steering, a training-free method that uses the weight vector of a linear probe at a PEZ layer as a Concept Activation Vector (CAV) and injects it into hidden states during inference. This shifts the model's physical expectations without changing any model weights. On the IntPhys benchmark, this intervention reliably shifts the model's plausibility judgment in either direction, depending on the steering sign. The effect appears only when the intervention is applied within the Physics Emergence Zone, suggesting that the relevant physics representation is localized there. We further find that physics is encoded separately from motion direction, and that different intuitive physics principles occupy distinct directions within this representation space. Together, these results show that physical reasoning in VideoMAE is not only readable, but also directly steerable.

</details>


### [28] [ViViD-5K: Vineyard vision dataset for field-based berry detection and segmentation and grape cluster closure estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24353)
*Xiangzhi Tong,Chengrui Zhang,Mac Flaherty,Andre Matteo Garcia,Dominic Gorman,Jonathan Jaramillo,Justine E. Vanden Heuvel,Yu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ViViD-5k大规模葡萄园图像数据集（含5,000张图像、648,000+浆果质心和13个品种的簇分割掩码），并基于此开发了GrapeSAM两阶段视觉流水线（点定位+提示分割+Transformer簇分割），实现自动化、可扩展的葡萄簇紧密度估计，替代传统人工评分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统葡萄簇紧密度（cluster closure）视觉评分方法劳动密集、主观性强且缺乏时间分辨率；现有数据集很少支持精细的浆果级分析，限制了鲁棒深度学习模型的发展。

Method: 构建ViViD-5k数据集（13个品种、5,000张图像、648,000+浆果质心和分割掩码）；提出GrapeSAM两阶段流水线：第一阶段基于点的浆果定位，结合Segment Anything的提示分割；第二阶段使用Transformer进行簇分割，实现自动化簇紧密度估计。

Result: 在多种条件下取得了强分割和计数精度；可视化结果验证了在域内和域外样本上的鲁棒性；实现了较传统方法更客观、可扩展的紧密度评分。

Conclusion: 该工作为人工紧密度评分提供了可扩展的客观替代方案，以增强的空间细节支持高通量葡萄表型分析。

Abstract: Cluster closure, defined as the progressive filling of gaps between the berries in a grape bunch, is a key trait in vineyard management, impacting disease risk. However, traditional visual scoring methods are labor-intensive, subjective, and lack temporal resolution. Existing datasets rarely support fine-grained berry-level analysis, limiting the development of robust deep learning models. In this work, we present ViViD-5k, a large-scale in-field Vineyard Vision Dataset containing 5,000 images with dense annotations, including over 648,000 berry centroids and cluster segmentation masks spanning 13 grape varieties. Building on this dataset, we introduce GrapeSAM, a two-stage visual pipeline that combines point-based berry localization with prompt-based segmentation using Segment Anything, followed by transformer-based cluster segmentation. The pipeline enables automated, in-field estimation of cluster closure with minimal supervision. Quantitative results demonstrate strong segmentation and counting accuracy across diverse conditions, while visualizations confirm robustness on both in-domain and out-of-domain samples. This work provides a scalable and objective alternative to manual compactness scoring and supports high-throughput grape phenotyping with enhanced spatial detail.

</details>


### [29] [SparseWorld: Enhancing End-to-End Autonomous Driving via World Models with Sparse Scene Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24354)
*Ruoyu Wang,Jingke Wang,Yukai Ma,Yuehao Huang,Shuangming Lei,Guanglin Xu,Aixue Ye,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparseWorld提出了一种轻量级的世界模型，通过仅预测场景中的关键布局元素（地图要素和周围智能体）来实现高效的端到端驾驶系统。该方法采用自回归方式在潜在空间中预测未来实例，并利用这些预测改进运动预测和轨迹规划。在nuScenes数据集上以0.05%的碰撞率实现了开环规划SOTA，并在Bench2Drive闭环指标上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型依赖于密集场景表示，导致计算成本高且存在冗余信息，需要更高效的场景预测方法。

Method: 提出SparseWorld轻量级世界模型：(1) Sparse Dreamer通过联合时空注意力在潜在空间中自回归预测未来实例（地图元素和周围智能体）；(2) 利用预测的未来实例与运动规划器交互，捕捉更精确的运动模式，生成更安全、信息更丰富的轨迹。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集开环规划指标上达到SOTA，碰撞率仅0.05%；在Bench2Drive基准的闭环规划指标上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 稀疏场景表示的世界模型能够有效降低计算冗余，同时通过预测关键场景布局显著提升端到端驾驶的安全性和规划性能。

Abstract: Recently, world models have made significant progress in enhancing end-to-end driving systems through both future situation forecasting and improved scene understanding. However, existing driving world models are typically built upon dense scene representations, causing high computational costs and redundant information. In this paper, we present SparseWorld, a lightweight world model that focuses on predicting only the critical layout of the scene, enabling efficient future forecasting for end-to-end driving systems. SparseWorld first performs autoregressive rollout to forecast future map elements and surrounding agents, enabling the model to learn how driving scenarios evolve over time. It then leverages these predicted futures to refine downstream motion prediction and trajectory planning. Specifically, we propose a Sparse Dreamer that anticipates future instances in the latent space through joint temporal and spatial attention. By interacting with predicted future instances, the motion planner captures more accurate motion patterns and generates more informed and safety-aware trajectories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SparseWorld significantly reduces collision risk and achieves state-of-the-art performance on the open-loop planning metrics of the nuScenes dataset with a collision rate of 0.05\%. Moreover, it substantially outperforms the baseline method in closed-loop planning metrics on the Bench2Drive benchmark. Supplementary material is available at the project page: https://wryzju.github.io/SparseWorld/.

</details>


### [30] [SliceWorld: A Predictive and Controllable World-State Model for CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24371)
*Yuanhe Tian,Yan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SliceWorld提出了一种CT专用世界状态框架，将CT扫描视为沿z轴的有序序列，通过因子感知的潜在状态（解剖、病变、不确定性）编码CT证据，并利用世界令牌进行未来切片特征预测、病变因子干预和LLM报告生成，在M3D-Cap和CT-RATE数据集上提升了自然语言生成和临床评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT报告生成方法通常学习直接的图像到文本映射，缺乏对CT证据如何跨切片演变以及报告如何响应病变相关因子变化的建模机制。

Method: 将CT扫描视为沿z轴的有序序列；将前缀CT证据编码为包含解剖、病变和不确定性组件的因子感知潜在状态；将这些状态投影为世界令牌，用于多步未来切片特征预测、病变因子干预和基于LLM的报告生成。模型先通过预测性、因子感知性和反事实目标在CT切片序列上预训练，再在配对的CT-报告数据上微调。

Result: 在M3D-Cap和CT-RATE数据集上，SliceWorld在自然语言生成指标和临床导向自动评估上均有提升。进一步分析展示了多步未来切片预测、可测量的因子对齐、降切片鲁棒性以及选择性病变敏感的报告调控能力。

Conclusion: SliceWorld通过引入世界状态框架有效建模了CT切片间的时序依赖和病变因子操控，为CT报告生成提供了更可控、更可解释的范式。

Abstract: CT report generation (CTRG) requires models to summarize three-dimensional anatomical context and pathological findings from hundreds of axial slices. Existing methods typically learn a direct image-to-text mapping, providing limited mechanisms for modeling how CT evidence evolves across slices or how reports respond to controlled changes in latent lesion-related factors. We propose SliceWorld, a CT-specific world-state framework that treats an axial CT scan as an ordered sequence along the z-axis. SliceWorld encodes prefix CT evidence into factor-aware latent states containing anatomy, lesion, and uncertainty components, and projects these states into world tokens used for multi-step future-slice feature prediction, lesion-factor intervention, and LLM-based report generation. The model is first pretrained on CT slice sequences with predictive, factor-aware, and counterfactual objectives, and is then fine-tuned on paired CT-report data. Experiments on M3D-Cap and CT-RATE show that SliceWorld improves natural language generation metrics and clinically oriented automatic evaluation. Further analyses demonstrate multi-horizon future-slice prediction, measurable factor alignment, reduced-slice robustness, and selective lesion-sensitive report modulation.

</details>


### [31] [VectorArk: Learning Practical Image Vectorization with Rounded Polygon Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24398)
*Tarun Gehlaut,Difan Liu,Charu Bansal,Krutik Malani,Souymodip Chakraborty,Ankit Phogat,Matthew Fisher,Vineet Batra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VectorArk提出了一种基于VLM的图像矢量化方法，采用圆角多边形表示和退化模型，在真实场景下实现了优于现有方法的几何完整性和伪影抑制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM-based图像矢量化方法仅在合成基准上表现良好，泛化到真实场景（如未知光栅化方法或文生图模型输出）时性能显著下降，亟需一种鲁棒且实用的矢量化方案。

Method: 1）提出圆角多边形（rounded polygon）表示，简化学习过程并自然生成平滑视觉元素；2）设计退化模型（degradation model），增强对多样化、不完美输入的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个数据集上，VectorArk相比先前方法实现了更优的几何完整性和伪影抑制，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: VectorArk通过新颖的表示和退化建模，有效缩小了合成与真实场景之间的性能差距，为实用化图像矢量化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent vision-language model (VLM)-based approaches have achieved impressive results on image vectorization tasks. However, they are typically evaluated on synthetic benchmarks, where clean SVGs are rasterized at high resolution and then re-vectorized. As a result, these methods generalize poorly to real-world scenarios, such as images with unknown rasterization methods or those generated by text-to-image models. We introduce VectorArk, a new VLM-based model designed for robust and practical image vectorization. VectorArk employs a novel rounded polygon representation that simplifies the learning process while naturally producing smooth, visually appealing primitives. We also propose a degradation model that enhances robustness across diverse and imperfect inputs. Our experiments show that, in contrast to previous methods, VectorArk achieves superior geometric completeness and artifact suppression across multiple datasets, with comprehensive ablations validating the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [32] [Dual Prototype-Conditioned Diffusion Model for Scalable Multi-Class Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in Large Category Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24402)
*Yaoxuan Feng,Yuxin Li,Weijiang Lv,Zixuan Zhao,Yubiao Wang,Wenchao Chen,Bo Chen,Hongwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DPDiff-AD，一种基于双原型条件扩散模型的大规模多类异常检测方法。通过局部原型（最近邻原型聚合捕获细粒度结构）和全局原型（最优传输正则化调控整体特征几何）构建结构化的正态性空间，并利用原型感知注意力进行扩散重建，实现对异构正态分布的精确建模。在160类大规模数据集上，图像级和像素级AUROC分别比先前最优方法Dinomaly+提升5.3和2.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类异常检测中，随着类别数量增加，模型性能因正态分布日益复杂和异构而严重下降。现有统一模型难以有效建模跨类别的多样化正态模式。

Method: DPDiff-AD采用双原型条件扩散模型：(1) 局部原型通过最近邻原型聚合捕获细粒度结构模式；(2) 全局原型通过最优传输正则化调控整体特征几何。二者构成结构化的正态性空间，再通过原型感知注意力进行扩散重建，联合利用双原型指导生成过程。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上验证有效性。在160类大规模数据集上，图像级AUROC提升5.3点，像素级AUROC提升2.9点，超越Dinomaly+，且性能随类别数增加保持稳定。

Conclusion: DPDiff-AD通过双原型条件扩散框架实现了大规模多类异常检测中精确的正态性建模，保持了类别增长下的结构可分离性，具备可扩展的异常判别能力。

Abstract: Multi-class anomaly detection aims to build unified models across diverse product categories. However, as the number of categories grows, its performance often degrades due to increasingly complex and heterogeneous normal distributions. To address this challenge, we propose DPDiff-AD, a Dual Prototype-conditioned Diffusion model for large-scale multi-class Anomaly Detection. DPDiff-AD models heterogeneous normal distributions through complementary local and global prototypes. Local prototypes capture representative fine-grained structural patterns via nearest-prototype aggregation, while global prototypes regulate holistic feature geometry through optimal transport regularization. Together, these dual-scale representations define a structured normality space. This space is refined through diffusion-based reconstruction conditioned on both local and global prototypes via prototype-aware attention. By jointly leveraging dual prototypes during generation, DPDiff-AD achieves precise normality modeling, preserves structured separability as category cardinality grows, and enables scalable anomaly discrimination. Extensive experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of DPDiff-AD. On the 160-category large-scale dataset, it improves image- and pixel-level AUROC by 5.3 and 2.9 points over the previous state-of-the-art method Dinomaly+, while maintaining stable performance as category cardinality increases.

</details>


### [33] [Artiverse: A Diverse and Physically Grounded Dataset for Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24403)
*Denys Iliash,Jiayi Liu,Egor Fokin,Qirui Wu,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Manolis Savva,Angel X. Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Artiverse是一个包含5.4K个人工标注的高质量可动3D物体数据集，涵盖88个类别，具有功能部件、内部结构、运动关系、多自由度关节及物理属性等注释，通过半自动标注流程将人工标注时间减少30%以上，并在部件运动分析、可动物体生成和物理交互任务上验证了其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D数据集缺乏对可动物体的物理真实感功能建模与仿真支持，难以满足对物体功能理解、运动分析和物理交互的研究需求。

Method: 构建了半自动标注流程，结合少样本分割、几何推理和多阶段人工验证，从多个3D静态数据仓库中聚合并注释了5.4K个物体，包含功能部件、内部结构、运动学关系（含多自由度关节）以及物理属性（公制尺度、材质、质量）。

Result: 数据集包含5.4K个人工标注物体，覆盖88个类别；半自动标注流程将人工标注时间减少30%以上；在部件运动分析、可动物体生成和物理交互任务上展示了有效性。

Conclusion: Artiverse为可动3D物体的功能理解提供了高质量的数据资源，有望推动现实功能建模与仿真领域的研究进展。

Abstract: We present Artiverse, a diverse and physically grounded dataset of high-quality articulated 3D objects designed for realistic functional modeling and simulation. Artiverse contains 5.4K human-authored objects across a broad range of 88 categories, aggregated from multiple 3D static repositories. Objects are annotated with functional parts, interior structures, realistic kinematic relationships and articulated joints including multi-DoF joints, and physical attributes such as metric scale, material, and mass. We develop a semi-automated annotation pipeline that combines few-shot segmentation, geometric reasoning, and multi-stage human verification to achieve high-quality and efficient annotation, reducing manual annotation time by over 30%. We demonstrate the value of Artiverse on tasks of part mobility analysis, articulated object generation, and physics-based interaction. Artiverse provides a data resource to advance functional understanding for articulated objects.

</details>


### [34] [EgoProx: Evaluating MLLMs on Egocentric 3D Proximity Reasoning Across a Cognitive Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24456)
*Jinzhao Li,Yinuo Chen,Dongxu Piao,Panwang Pan,Yifan Yu,Dong Wang,Honglei Yan,Liang Yue,Shaofei Wang,Yixin Chen,Siyuan Huang,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoProx基准，用于评估多模态大模型在自我中心视角下的3D空间邻近推理能力。通过构建认知链任务（意图、探索、利用、动作链推理）和基于智能体的数据引擎生成多样化QA对，实验发现当前MLLMs虽具备一定空间知识，但难以有效用于空间推理VQA，跨领域微调可带来显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在日常生活中持续进行关于身体与周围物体间3D邻近关系的推理，然而当前多模态大模型是否具备这种具身3D推理能力尚不清楚，因此需要建立专门的评估基准来探究这一问题。

Method: 1) 构建了EgoProx基准测试，围绕认知链组织任务（意图、探索、利用、动作链推理）；2) 设计了基于智能体的数据引擎，能够规模化生成多样且一致的问答对；3) 在EgoProx上对主流多模态大模型进行基准测试，并进行数据集特定和任务特定的指令微调分析。

Result: 实验发现：1) 当前MLLMs具备一定的空间知识（跨领域微调带来显著提升）；2) 但模型仍难以有效利用这些知识进行空间推理视觉问答。

Conclusion: 现有MLLMs在自我中心3D邻近推理方面存在明显不足，虽通过指令微调可受益于跨领域知识迁移，但其空间推理能力仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Humans constantly reason about 3D proximity, the relations between their body and surrounding objects, to guide perception and action in daily life. Whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can perform such embodied 3D reasoning remains unclear. To this end, we introduce EgoProx, a benchmark for egocentric 3D proximity reasoning. We organize our tasks along a cognitive chain, covering intention, exploration, exploitation, and chain-of-actions reasoning. We also design an agent based data engine that produces diverse and consistent QA pairs at scale. We benchmark prevailing MLLMs on EgoProx and conduct additional analyses with dataset specific and task specific instruction tuning. We observe large cross-domain gains, indicating that current MLLMs contain some spatial knowledge; however, they still struggle to effectively leverage it for spatial reasoning VQA.

### [35] [Coarse-to-Fine Domain Incremental Learning with Attentive Distillation for Mining Footprint Segmentation in Multispectral Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24460)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化全球采矿足迹映射对监测采矿的社会环境风险至关重要，但精细注释数据的稀缺以及粗粒度数据与细粒度分割任务之间的域偏移阻碍了进展。
