TL;DR: MVCHead提出一种仅从随机采样的2D单视图图像学习3D高斯头部模型的方法，无需多视图数据、3D监督或中间视图生成。该方法通过层级状态空间块（HiSS）和双向状态扫描（HiBiSS）增强多视图一致性，并设计了SE(3)多视图评判器确保跨视图像素对齐。同时发布了首个大规模3D高斯头部数据集FaceGS-10K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯头部生成方法依赖多视图数据集、3D捕获或中间2D视图合成，限制了数据获取的便利性和可扩展性。本文旨在从随机采样的2D单视图图像中直接学习高质量3D头部模型，降低对多视图数据的依赖。

Method: 1) 提出MVCHead单次状态空间模型，直接在3D表示中强制执行多视图一致性；2) 设计层级状态空间块（HiSS），从粗到细渐进式细化高斯体并捕获长程依赖关系；3) 在每个HiSS块中提出层级双向状态扫描（HiBiSS），沿多视图不一致性最强的轴对齐循环；4) 设计SE(3)多视图评判器，评估自渲染图像是否来自同一底层3D配置，无需真实多视图配对。

Result: MVCHead在感知质量上达到最先进水平，在纹理一致性和几何一致性上超越先前方法，形状一致性保持可比。同时发布了FaceGS-10K数据集以支持3D头部模型训练与评估。

Conclusion: 仅从单视图2D图像学习3D高斯头部模型是可行的，MVCHead通过状态空间建模和多视图一致性约束实现了高质量3D头部生成，为数字人、AR/VR等应用提供了更易获取的解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D Gaussian head avatar generation is critical for applications such as AR/VR, telepresence, and digital humans. Existing methods depend on multi-view datasets, 3D captures, or intermediate 2D view synthesis. In contrast, we learn both conditional and unconditional 3D head models from randomly sampled 2D images alone, without using multi-view data, 3D supervision, or intermediate view generation. We introduce MVCHead, a single-shot state space model that enforces multi-view consistency (MVC) directly in the 3D representation while regressing 3D Gaussians under these constraints. At its core, we propose a Hierarchical State Space (HiSS) block that progressively refines Gaussians from coarse to fine, while capturing long-range dependencies. Within each HiSS block, we modify Mamba's standard unidirectional scan with the proposed Hierarchical Bi-directional State Scan (HiBiSS) that aligns recurrence with the axes along which multi-view inconsistencies are strongest. Finally, we design an SE(3) Multi-view Critic that judges whether a set of self-renders arises from a single underlying 3D configuration, rewarding cross-view pixel alignment without observing real multi-view pairs. MVCHead achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality, surpasses prior methods in both texture and geometric consistency, and maintains comparable shape consistency. To demonstrate scalability, we release FaceGS-10K, the first large-scale dataset of ready-to-use 3D Gaussian head assets for training and evaluation of 3D head models. Project Page and code: https://humansensinglab.github.io/MVCHead/

</details>


### [108] [Semantics-Guided Multimodal Masked Autoencoder Pretraining for 3D BEV Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25262)
*Prabuddhi Wariyapperuma,Rajitha de Silva,Marc Hanheide,Thomas Bohné,Leonardo Guevara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种语义引导的多模态掩码自编码器框架，通过语义引导的LiDAR体素掩码和辅助点级语义解码分支，在3D BEV目标检测预训练中引入语义信息，相比UniM2AE基线在nuScenes数据集上提升了mAP和NDS指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对相机和LiDAR输入采用均匀随机掩码，平等对待所有区域，且仅通过掩码重建学习表征，缺乏语义信息的引导。

Method: 提出语义引导的多模态掩码自编码器框架，包含两个组件：(i) 语义引导的LiDAR体素掩码，保留语义重要区域；(ii) 辅助点级LiDAR语义解码分支，在重建之外注入语义监督。

Result: 在BEVFusion 3D目标检测上，语义引导的LiDAR体素掩码相比UniM2AE基线mAP提升1.49%，NDS提升1.66%；解码端点语义监督mAP提升1.39%，NDS提升3.22%。

Conclusion: 语义引导的预训练策略能有效提升多模态3D BEV目标检测性能。

Abstract: Accurate 3D bird's-eye view (BEV) object detection is essential for autonomous driving, and depends strongly on effective multimodal representations from complementary sensors such as cameras and LiDAR. Multimodal masked autoencoders have shown strong potential for learning such representations for downstream 3D BEV object detection. However, existing methods typically apply uniform random masking to camera and LiDAR inputs, treating all regions equally, and learn representations only through masked reconstruction. We propose a semantics-guided multimodal masked autoencoder framework that introduces semantic information during pretraining through two separate components: (i) semantics-guided LiDAR voxel masking, which preserves semantically important LiDAR regions more strongly, and (ii) an auxiliary point-wise LiDAR semantic decoder branch that injects semantic guidance in addition to reconstruction. On BEVFusion 3D object detection, our semantics-guided pretraining strategy improves performance on the nuScenes mini validation set compared to the standard UniM2AE baseline: semantics-guided LiDAR voxel masking yields +1.49% mean Average Precision (mAP) and +1.66% nuScenes Detection Score (NDS), while decoder-side point semantic supervision yields +1.39% mAP and +3.22% NDS over the baseline.

</details>


### [109] [DeltaCam: Differential Intrinsic Camera Modeling for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25266)
*Debabrata Mandal,Zhihan Peng,Yujie Wang,Praneeth Chakravarthula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaCam是一个视频扩散框架，通过Δ参数化的神经相机适配器建模相机行为，从相对变化而非绝对状态学习，实现对焦距、光圈、ISO、色温、镜头畸变等内参的可控生成，并支持真实视频的微调与风格迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型主要关注外参控制（如相机姿态和运动），而将内参视为隐式或固定的。缺乏带有精确、多样化的时变相机元数据的大规模视频数据集，导致难以学习绝对相机参数化，因此模型无法以可控且时域一致的方式模拟景深转换、曝光变化、镜头畸变和色彩处理等摄影行为。

Method: 提出DeltaCam框架，采用Δ参数化神经相机适配器，学习相机运动与内参的相对变化而非绝对状态；利用合成视频数据训练，避免对真实相机标签的依赖；通过两种机制扩展到真实视频：在真实图像-元数据对上微调控制以实现精确镜头匹配，以及提取解耦嵌入实现隐式视频到视频的风格迁移。

Result: DeltaCam实现了对焦距、光圈、ISO、色温、镜头畸变等成像因素的平滑、一致的时域控制，并支持相机一致的视频生成和编辑操作，在现有模型难以完成的场景下表现出色。

Conclusion: 通过有效分离场景内容与内生成像行为，DeltaCam建立了一种桥接合成控制与真实摄影模拟的实用且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Incorporating camera intrinsics into video generation models offers a principled way to control not only scene dynamics but also the imaging process that governs visual appearance. Prior work has primarily focused on extrinsic control, such as camera pose and motion, while treating intrinsic camera parameters as implicit or fixed. A key bottleneck is the lack of large-scale video datasets with accurate and diverse temporally varying camera metadata, which makes learning absolute camera parameterizations difficult. As a result, current models struggle to incorporate photographic camera behavior, including depth-of-field transitions, exposure variations, lens distortions, and color processing, in a controllable and temporally consistent manner. We introduce DeltaCam, a video diffusion framework that models camera behavior through $Δ$-parameterized neural camera adaptors, operating on relative changes in camera motion and intrinsics instead of absolute states. By learning this differential formulation from synthetic video data, we mitigate reliance on precise real-world camera labels and enable smooth, consistent control over imaging factors such as focal length, aperture, ISO, color temperature, and lens distortion. We extend this framework to real-world footage through two mechanisms: finetuning the controls on real image-metadata pairs for precise shot matching, and extracting disentangled embeddings for implicit video-to-video style transfer without requiring explicit camera parameters. By effectively separating scene content from intrinsic imaging behavior, DeltaCam enables camera-consistent video generation and editing operations that are difficult to achieve with existing models. Ultimately, our results establish a practical and scalable approach for bridging synthetic control and real-world photographic emulation.

</details>


### [110] [Geometry-Aware Image Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25294)
*Junho Lee,Kwanseok Kim,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现自然图像语义信息主要编码在方向分量中，而范数分量可被全局平均近似，据此提出在超球面上建模图像生成，并设计了球面最优传输流匹配（SOT-CFM）和球面流匹配（SFM）两种几何感知方法，实验证明优于欧几里得基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型在流形约束场景中已展现出几何感知建模的优势，但在自然图像领域仍局限于欧几里得假设，未能利用数据内在的几何结构。本文旨在探索自然图像的几何特性，并利用其内在的流形结构提升生成模型性能。

Method: 首先观察到自然图像的语义信息主要编码在方向分量中，范数分量可由全局平均近似，这一性质在RGB空间和潜空间均成立。基于此发现，提出两种方法：Spherical Optimal Transport Flow Matching (SOT-CFM)，利用角距离进行最优传输；Spherical Flow Matching (SFM)，直接在超球面流形上约束动力学过程。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的几何感知方法（SOT-CFM和SFM）在性能上优于欧几里得基线方法，验证了在超球面上建模自然图像的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过揭示自然图像的方向编码特性，将黎曼流形建模与自然图像生成之间的鸿沟连接起来，为几何感知生成模型在图像领域的发展提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models highlight the power of geometry-aware modeling in manifold-constrained settings. Yet, for natural images, the field remains confined to Euclidean assumptions, failing to exploit the potential of intrinsic geometric structures within the data. In this work, we investigate the geometry of natural images and observe that semantic information is predominantly encoded in directional components, while norm components can be approximated by the global average. This property holds across both RGB and latent spaces, suggesting that natural images can be effectively modeled on a hypersphere. Building on this finding, we introduce Spherical Optimal Transport Flow Matching (SOT-CFM), which utilizes angular distance, and Spherical Flow Matching (SFM), which constrains dynamics directly on the manifold. Our experiments demonstrate that these geometry-aware methods achieve superior performance against Euclidean baselines. Ultimately, this work provides a novel perspective that bridges the gap between Riemannian manifold-based modeling and natural image generation.

</details>


### [111] [Recursive Class Connectivity Classification (R3C) Applied to Binary Image Segmentation for Improved Infant Fingerprint Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25307)
*Joao Leonardo Harres Dall Agnol,Luiz Fernando Puttow Southier,Jefferson Tales 0liva,Marcelo Teixeira,Rodrigo Mineto,Marcelo Filipa,Dalcimar Casanova,Erick Oliveira Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R3C框架，通过迭代优化已有增强方法的二值分割输出，在无需训练数据或修改分类器的情况下，将儿童指纹识别的真实接受率提升高达4%，新生儿提升超40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童指纹因手指尺寸小、脊线结构细薄，采集时图像质量差，现有增强方法未针对高分辨率扫描仪设计，导致儿童指纹识别率远低于成人。

Method: R3C框架通过将初始分类图像反复馈回分类过程，每次结合原始输入图像，迭代细化二值分割结果，延伸脊线结构，且不依赖训练数据，无需修改底层分类器。

Result: 在三个指纹数据集上使用四种增强分类器，R3C将儿童TAR提升最多4%，新生儿提升超40%；定性分析显示R3C能重新连接断裂脊线，提升分割视觉质量。

Conclusion: R3C是一种与增强方法无关的通用二值分割优化方案，灵活且广泛适用，能显著提升低质量儿童指纹的识别性能。

Abstract: Image enhancement plays a crucial role in infant fingerprint matching, as child-specific characteristics such as smaller finger dimensions and thinner ridge structures often degrade image quality during acquisition. To address these limitations, enrollment typically depends on specialized highresolution scanners, which most existing enhancement methods are not designed to support. Consequently, identification rates for children remain significantly lower than those achieved with adult fingerprints. This study introduces Recursive Class Connectivity Classification (R3C), a novel framework that iteratively refines binary segmentation outputs from existing enhancement methods by extending ridge structures. R3C does not require modifications to the underlying classifier and operates without training data, which is not currently available for infant fingerprints. Instead, the method improves segmentation by repeatedly feeding the classified image back into the classification process, while combining each intermediate segmentation with the original input image. Experiments conducted on three fingerprint datasets using four different enhancement classifiers show that R3C can increase the True Acceptance Rate (TAR) by up to 4% for children and over 40% for newborns, compared to using the enhancement methods alone. A qualitative analysis further demonstrates that R3C reconnects fragmented ridge patterns, improving the visual quality of segmentation. Because it functions independently of the enhancement method used, R3C provides a flexible and broadly applicable solution for improving binary segmentation.

</details>


### [112] [Perceive-then-Plan: Layout-as-Policy for Monocular 3D Scene Layout Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25326)
*Junwei Zhou,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Layout-as-Policy (LaP)框架，将单目3D布局估计转化为感知-规划问题，利用视觉-语言模型和策略学习进行迭代优化，生成更物理合理且与视觉观察一致的3D场景布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像构建结构化3D场景布局需要调和视觉观察与物理/空间约束之间的矛盾，直接预测方法难以有效处理这些全局约束和复杂物体交互。

Method: 提出Layout-as-Policy (LaP)框架：（1）Perceiver先验模块利用几何增强的视觉语言模型定位3D物体；（2）Planner将布局优化建模为策略学习问题，通过平移、旋转、缩放等离散动作迭代优化；（3）结合监督轨迹初始化和基于偏好的优化进行训练，无需显式奖励工程。

Result: 实验表明该方法生成的布局在物理一致性和视觉观察对齐方面优于现有方法，并能自然地支持场景编辑和操作等下游任务。

Conclusion: 将布局估计从单次预测任务转化为迭代细化过程，能更好地处理全局约束和复杂物体交互，为单目3D场景理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Building structured 3D scene layouts from a single image requires reconciling visual observations with physical and spatial constraints, a challenge that is difficult to address with direct prediction alone. In this work, we formulate monocular 3D layout estimation as a perceive-then-plan problem with vision-language models, where a Perceiver first grounds the 3D objects and then a Planner iteratively refines the scene hypothesis through actions that improve physical plausibility while preserving consistency with the input image. We propose Layout-as-Policy (LaP), which casts the planning stage as a policy learning problem: 3D layouts are represented as structured states, and refined via discrete actions such as translation, rotation, and rescaling. Starting from an observation-aligned initialization with the geometry-enhanced Perceiver, the LaP Planner is trained to produce action sequences that progressively resolve geometric inconsistencies and enforce realistic spatial relations. To enable effective learning, we combine supervised trajectory initialization with preference-based optimization, allowing the model to learn corrective behaviors without requiring explicit reward engineering. This formulation transforms layout estimation from a one-shot prediction task into an iterative refinement process, enabling better handling of global constraints and complex object interactions. Experiments demonstrate that our approach produces layouts that are more physically coherent and better aligned with visual observations, while naturally supporting downstream tasks such as scene editing and manipulation.

</details>


### [113] [Teaching Video Generators to Remember: Eliciting Dynamic Memory for Out-of-Sight State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25333)
*Tianshuo Xu,Yichen Xie,Depu Meng,Chensheng Peng,Quentin Herau,Bo Jiang,Yihan Hu,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReMind 提出了一套基于记忆导向数据、事件感知训练和缓存自适应的方法，将视频扩散Transformer的KV-cache机制激活为动态记忆，显著提升了视频世界模型在中断状态下的状态保持与恢复能力，并在STEVO-Bench和图像到视频任务上取得最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在观测中断时往往冻结隐状态，无法维持演化中的动态记忆。作者认为这并非容量限制——预训练的视频扩散Transformer已具备能进行非局部检索的KV-cache机制，但很少被训练用作动态记忆。

Method: (1) 构建含100+动态事件分类的带相机标注训练混合数据，包括VLM筛选的真实视频、生成的硬动态、合成相机循环及记忆中断增强；(2) 将每个片段转换为帧图，包含保护锚点、退化区间和显式时间间隔；(3) 节点结构课程训练（节点丢弃、噪声记忆、前沿连续、参考缓存训练）强迫模型跨中断检索相关过去状态；(4) PM-RoPE（相机相位RoPE扩展）实现单注意力开销下的时空检索，同时保留预训练通路。

Result: 在STEVO-Bench和恢复任务上取得最佳总体得分，通用图像到视频评估证实该课程训练不会导致灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: ReMind通过针对性的数据构建与训练策略，成功激活视频扩散Transformer中的动态记忆能力，为视频世界模型的状态保持提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Video world models should maintain evolving states when evidence is unobserved, yet current generators often freeze hidden states upon interruption. This is not simply a capacity problem: pretrained video diffusion transformers already possess KV-cache mechanisms capable of non-local retrieval, but they are rarely trained to use them as dynamic memory. We introduce ReMind, a framework eliciting dynamic memory behavior via memory-oriented data, event-aware training, and cache adaptation. Organized around a taxonomy of 100+ dynamic events, we build a camera-annotated training mixture combining VLM-filtered real videos, generated hard dynamics, synthetic camera loops, and memory-interruption augmentations. Each clip is converted into a frame graph with protected anchors, degraded intervals, and explicit temporal gaps. A node-structured curriculum, including node-drop, noisy memory, frontier continuation, and reference-cache training, forces the model to retrieve relevant past states across interruptions rather than relying solely on local continuity. PM-RoPE, an elegant camera-phase RoPE extension, unlocks spatiotemporal retrieval at a single-attention cost while preserving pretrained pathways. ReMind achieves the best overall scores on STEVO-Bench and recovery tasks. Furthermore, general image-to-video evaluations confirm this curriculum avoids catastrophic forgetting. We will open-source our code, data, and models.

</details>


### [114] [Toward Native Multimodal Modeling: A Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25343)
*Siyu An,Junru Lu,Junnan Dong,Qiufeng Wang,Yinghui Li,Weizhi Fei,Zichao Yu,Zheng Yuan,Biao Liu,Haopeng Wang,Renzhao Liang,Yixuan Yang,Yunhang Shen,Bo Ke,Keyu Chen,Linhao Luo,Difan Zou,Xiao Huang,Di Yin,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了从模态无关推理到原生多模态建模（NMM）的转变路线图，明确定义了架构原生性，按输入-输出对偶性将现有模型分为三类（多到文本、多到目标、多到多），并从工业视角全面梳理了架构协调、数据构建、训练推理及评估的全栈流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态建模多依赖后期融合方式（编码器+冻结语言骨干+输出头），缺乏对原生多模态架构设计空间的系统定义与工业级指导，亟需一个正式的路线图来推动向真正原生统一建模范式的转变。

Method: 1) 正式定义架构原生性，区分中期融合、早期融合与非原生范式；2) 基于输入-输出对偶性将原生模型分为Multi-to-Text、Multi-to-Target和Multi-to-Multi三类；3) 从工业级视角系统拆解端到端流程，涵盖架构协调、大规模数据构建、全栈训练配方、推理部署及综合评估。

Result: 提出了原生多模态建模的正式定义与分类体系，并构建了从理解到生成的统一Transformer范式下的工业级全栈指导框架。

Conclusion: 原生多模态建模是实现真正世界建模的关键路径，通过明确的架构原生性定义和系统化的工业级流程梳理，为社区提供了迈向统一多模态框架的清晰路线图。

Abstract: Multimodal modeling represents a vital step from modality-agnostic reasoning toward world modeling. While early approaches predominantly rely on late-fusion that assembles encoders and frozen language backbones with output heads, recent efforts have shifted the paradigm toward native multimodal modeling (NMM) with the intrinsic integration of modalities for superior multimodal performance. Despite its potential, the design space of native architectures remains insufficiently defined. In this paper, we present the community with a formalized roadmap for this transition. Specifically, we formally define the architectural nativity, distinguishing mid-fusion and early-fusion from non-native paradigms. We further organize the existing native models through the lens of input-output duality into three categories: (i) Multi-to-Text for cross-modal comprehension with text-only output; (ii) Multi-to-Target for scenario-oriented generation, e.g., image, audio and video generation, and (iii) Multi-to-Multi for unified modeling with symmetric input-output. We deliver a comprehensive and industrial-grade investigation into the transition toward the definitive NMM framework, where understanding and generation seamlessly coexist within a unified transformer paradigm. We systematically unpack the end-to-end pipeline from industrial perspectives from architectural coordination, massive data curation, to full-stack training recipes, inference & deployment, and the comprehensive evaluation for truly native modeling.

</details>


### [115] [ERNIE-Image Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25347)
*Jiaxiang Liu,Zhida Feng,Pengyu Zou,Zhenyu Qian,Tianrui Zhu,Jun Xia,Yuehu Dong,Yanzheng Lin,Honglin Xiong,Anqi Chen,Yunpeng Ding,Jinghui Duan,Lin Gao,Chao Han,Tiechao He,Jiakang Hu,Ranjun Hua,Xueming Jiang,Qingli Kong,Yuting Lei,Tianyu Li,Yunlin Liu,Changling Liu,Yaxin Liu,Yi Liu,Xuguang Liu,Xiaolong Ma,Yan Pan,Yiran Ren,Nan Sheng,Yu Sun,Siyang Sun,Yixiang Tu,Yang Wan,Huanai Wang,Siqi Wang,Yang Wu,Youzhi Yang,Xiaowen Yang,Jianwen Yang,Yehua Yang,Quanwen Zhang,Xinmin Zhang,Haoxin Zhang,Xiang Zhang,Jun Zhang,Qian Zhang,Qiao Zhao,Qi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ERNIE-Image是一个基于8B单流DiT架构的开源文生图模型，通过自底向上的预训练数据构建流水线和自顶向下的后训练数据构建流水线，结合DPO对齐、高效蒸馏和轻量级提示增强器，在指令遵循、文本渲染和美学质量上达到开源领先水平，接近顶级商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源文生图模型与闭源商业系统之间存在显著性能差距，主要原因在于大规模预训练数据的挖掘不够有效以及训练过程中的监督质量不足。ERNIE-Image旨在通过更高效的数据利用和监督策略来缩小这一差距。

Method: 预训练阶段采用自底向上的数据构建流水线，包含细粒度图像分类、丰富字幕标注、美学评估和分层采样。后训练阶段采用自顶向下的数据构建流水线，针对高需求场景进行多样化提示标注，并使用稳定的DPO策略对齐人类审美偏好。此外，训练了ERNIE-Image-Turbo实现高效8-NFE生成，提出MT-DMD缓解蒸馏过程中的能力漂移，配备轻量级Prompt Enhancer，以及开发了工业级美学模型ERNIE-Image-Aes和基准数据集ERNIE-Image-Aes-1K。

Result: ERNIE-Image在指令遵循、文本渲染和美学质量方面达到开源模型领先水平，接近顶级商业模型。发布了训练好的模型和美学资源。

Conclusion: ERNIE-Image通过更有效的数据挖掘和监督质量提升，成功弥合了开源模型与闭源系统之间的差距，为AIGC社区的学术研究和技术进步提供了有价值的开源资源和美学评估基准。

Abstract: We introduce ERNIE-Image, an open-source text-to-image generation model built upon an 8B single-stream DiT architecture. ERNIE-Image aims to bridge the gap between current open-source models and leading closed-source systems through more effective mining of large-scale pre-training data and improved supervision quality throughout training. During pre-training, we adopt a bottom-up data construction pipeline that combines fine-grained image categorization, rich caption annotation, aesthetic assessment, and hierarchical sampling. This strategy reduces data noise while preserving long-tail concepts and detailed real-world knowledge, providing a stronger foundation for complex generation tasks. In the post-training stage, we use a top-down data construction pipeline for high-demand scenarios, diversify prompt annotations to better match real user inputs, and apply a stabilized DPO strategy to align the model with human aesthetic preferences. We further train ERNIE-Image-Turbo for efficient 8-NFE generation and propose MT-DMD to mitigate capability drift during distillation. To make the model easier to use in practical scenarios, we equip it with a lightweight Prompt Enhancer that expands concise user intents into structured visual descriptions. In addition, we develop ERNIE-Image-Aes, an industrial-grade aesthetic model, together with ERNIE-Image-Aes-1K, a human-annotated benchmark for realistic aesthetic evaluation. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments show that ERNIE-Image achieves leading performance among open-source models and approaches top-tier commercial models in instruction following, text rendering, and aesthetic quality. We release the trained models and aesthetic resources to facilitate further academic research and technical progress in the AIGC community.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Reliable Fetal Ultrasound Interpretation with Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25357)
*Xiaotian Hu,Mingxuan Liu,Junwei Huang,Kasidit Anmahapong,Yifei Chen,Yiming Huang,Xuguang Bai,Zihan Li,Hongjia Yang,Yingqi Hao,Hong Xu,Yu Jiang,Tian Tian,Yi Liao,Haibo Qu,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FetUSAgents，一个基于工具增强的多智能体系统，用于胎儿超声全面解读，通过协作LLM智能体协调视觉工具，并引入双路径证据仲裁（DPEA）机制，在VQA任务上比最强基线提升超过25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"一个任务一个模型"范式限制了胎儿超声多步骤流程中的系统性证据整合，而多模态大模型（MLLMs）存在领域知识不足和幻觉风险，可靠性受限。

Method: 提出FetUSAgents多智能体系统，包含：(1) 通过LLM智能体协作协调任务特定视觉工具；(2) 双路径证据仲裁（DPEA）融合LLM审慎推理与结构化计算证据；(3) 检索增强证据库存储中间发现。同时构建FetUS-VQA基准数据集。

Result: 在分布外实验中，FetUSAgents在VQA准确率上超越通用和医学MLLMs，比最强基线提升超过25%。

Conclusion: 该方法为实现循证临床辅助产前影像诊断提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Automated fetal ultrasound interpretation requires a workflow from visual perception, including plane recognition and anatomical segmentation, to clinical understanding, including biometric measurement and diagnostic reporting. However, the prevailing "one-task, one-model" paradigm limits systematic integration of evidence across this multi-step process. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) show promising visual understanding, their limited domain-specific grounding and hallucination risks restrict reliability in fetal ultrasound analysis. To address these limitations, we propose FetUSAgents, a tool-augmented multi-agent system for comprehensive fetal ultrasound interpretation, supporting visual question answering (VQA), report generation, image captioning, and video summarization. FetUSAgents coordinates task-specific visual tools through collaborative LLM agents and decomposes clinical queries into subtasks that progress from anatomical recognition to quantitative measurement. We further introduce Dual-Path Evidence Arbitration (DPEA), which integrates LLM-based deliberative reasoning with structured computational evidence from specialized visual tools. A retrieval-enhanced evidence bank consolidates intermediate findings to support traceable and clinically grounded conclusions. In addition, we construct FetUS-VQA, a dedicated VQA benchmark for fetal ultrasound, comprising 1,892 images and 3,205 question-answer pairs across 10 clinical tasks. Extensive out-of-distribution experiments show that FetUSAgents outperforms general and medical MLLMs, exceeding the strongest baseline by more than 25 percent in VQA accuracy. These results suggest a scalable route toward evidence-driven clinical assistants for prenatal imaging. Code is available.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Document Classification Pattern Recognition via Information Fusion: A Systematic Review of Multimodal and Multiview Representation Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23910)
*Marcin Michał Mirończuk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述139篇原始研究，提出了信息融合在文档分类中的统一框架，并通过元分析量化了多模态融合（准确率提升+5.28%）和多视图融合（准确率+4.67%、F1+3.08%、召回率均显著提升）的效果，同时揭示了方法论严谨性不足的问题（仅11.8%~23.3%的研究使用了统计检验）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息融合虽广泛应用于文档分类，但缺乏统一框架、定量效果综合以及给从业者的清晰指导。

Method: 对139篇原始研究进行系统综述，引入形式化框架，开展定性分析识别关键趋势，并执行随机效应元分析（据我们所知，首个聚焦于文档分类的元分析）以量化性能提升。

Result: 多模态融合显著提升准确率（平均+5.28个百分点，p=0.0016），F1分数方向性正向但主模型中统计不显著；多视图融合对准确率（+4.67%）、F1分数（+3.08%）和召回率均提供一致但适度的提升（均p<0.05）；仅11.8%（多模态）和23.3%（多视图）的研究使用了统计检验。

Conclusion: 成功的信息融合不取决于算法复杂度，而在于融合方法与任务上下文的战略对齐以及更严格的验证承诺。

Abstract: Information fusion is used widely to improve document classification by the integration of multiple data sources (multimodal) or representations (multiview). However, the field lacks a unified framework, a quantitative synthesis of its effectiveness, and clear guidance for practitioners. This systematic review addresses these gaps by analysing 139 primary studies. It introduces a formal framework to structure the field, presents the results of a qualitative analysis to identify key trends, and performs a random-effects meta-analysis (to our knowledge, the first focused on document classification) to quantify performance gains. Our meta-analysis reveals that multimodal fusion improves accuracy (mean gain of +5.28 percentage points, $p=0.0016$) significantly -- the F1-score effect is directionally positive but statistically non-significant in our primary model. Multiview fusion provides consistent but modest gains for accuracy (+4.67\%), F1-score (+3.08\%), and recall (all $p<0.05$). Critically, our qualitative synthesis uncovers challenges in reproducibility in methodological rigour: only 11.8\% (multimodal) and 23.3\% (multiview) of the studies use statistical tests to validate their findings, which undermines the reliability of many of their results. This review's primary contributions are a unifying framework, the first quantitative evidence base, and data-driven guidelines. This review concludes that successful information fusion depends not on algorithmic complexity, but on the strategic alignment of the fusion method with the task context and a commitment to more rigorous validation.

</details>


### [118] [Raon-Speech Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23912)
*Beomsoo Kim,Changho Choi,Dohyun Kim,Dongki Lee,Ethan Ewer,Eunchong Kim,Gyeongman Kim,Haechan Kim,Hyeonghwan Kim,Inkyu Park,Jihun Yun,Jihwan Moon,Jiyun Kim,Joonghyun Bae,Junhyuck Kim,Minkyu Kim,Sehun Lee,Seungjun Chung,Sungwoo Cho,Dongmin Park,Dongwon Kim,Hara Kang,Jonghyun Lee,Keon Lee,Kangwook Lee,Jaewoong Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Raon-Speech是一个9B参数的多模态语音语言模型，通过三阶段训练（语音模块对齐、端到端预训练+知识蒸馏、多任务偏好优化后训练）在42个英韩语音与文本基准上超越同类模型；其全双工扩展Raon-SpeechChat通过119K小时时序对齐对话数据训练，在轮换与中断敏感性任务上表现突出，并开源所有资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练大语言模型有效扩展为既能理解又能生成语音、同时保持强文本能力的语音语言模型，并进一步实现自然全双工实时对话。

Method: 三阶段训练方案：(1) 语音模块对齐；(2) 端到端SpeechLM预训练+知识蒸馏；(3) 多任务偏好优化后训练。全双工扩展通过(1)因果编码器适配、(2)全双工预训练、(3)全双工微调（语音与角色控制）三个阶段在119K小时时序对齐对话数据上持续训练。

Result: 在42个英韩语音与文本基准上，Raon-Speech在语音任务上建立最强综合表现，超越Qwen2.5-Omni、Fun-Audio-Chat等8个同期音频基础模型；Raon-SpeechChat在全双工基准FDB v1.0的轮换和中断敏感性行为上表现突出。

Conclusion: 提出了一套完整的语音语言模型训练与全双工对话扩展方案，开源所有模型权重、训练推理流程及交互演示，为语音交互领域提供了强有力的基线系统。

Abstract: We present Raon-Speech, a top-performing 9B-parameter speech language model (SpeechLM) for English and Korean speech understanding, answering, and generation, and Raon-SpeechChat, a high-performing full-duplex extension for natural real-time conversation. Raon-Speech successfully transforms a pre-trained LLM into a SpeechLM that both understands and generates speech while preserving strong text capabilities. It trains on 1.38M hours of highly curated English and Korean speech and text datasets with the following training stages: (1) speech modules alignment, (2) end-to-end SpeechLM pre-training with knowledge distillation, and (3) multi-task preference optimization-based post-training. Across 42 English and Korean speech and text benchmarks, Raon-Speech establishes the strongest overall profile on speech-centric tasks in our comparison against eight similarly sized recent audio foundation models, including Qwen2.5-Omni and Fun-Audio-Chat, while preserving strong text question answering performance. Building upon it, Raon-SpeechChat enables natural full-duplex conversation by continual training on 119K hours of time-aligned real and synthetic dialogue data. It proceeds through three complementary training stages: (1) causal encoder adaptation, (2) full-duplex pre-training, (3) full-duplex fine-tuning for voice and role-control. On multiple full-duplex benchmarks, Raon-SpeechChat shows its clearest strengths on the turn-taking and interruption-sensitive behaviors covered by FDB v1.0, and remains competitive across the broader full-duplex evaluation suite. We open-source all model checkpoints, the training and inference pipeline, and an interactive demo.

</details>


### [119] [Multi-Persona Debate System for Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23917)
*Jaeha Oh,Byungchan Kim,Ju Li,Yang Jeong Park,Jin-Sung Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为多角色辩论系统（MPDS）的自动化科学假设生成框架，通过文献检索、长上下文大语言模型推理、语料驱动的角色归纳和结构化多智能体辩论，在电池材料研究领域实现了可追溯、机制明确的假设生成。实验表明，MPDS在钠离子电池负极和全固态电池正极设计任务中能恢复与实验验证一致的设计逻辑，并通过消融研究证明角色辩论对跨视角整合能力有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学发现的主要瓶颈并非数据稀缺，而是无法将碎片化知识综合为可操作的假设。这一问题在电池材料研究中尤为突出，因为电化学性能、界面行为和制造可行性需要同时优化，而传统方法难以有效整合多维度、跨领域的文献知识。

Method: MPDS框架包含四个核心步骤：1) 文献检索构建包含最多500篇论文的文献快照；2) 利用长上下文大语言模型进行推理；3) 通过语料驱动的方式为不同智能体归纳特定角色（如电化学、界面、制造等视角）；4) 进行三轮引文感知的结构化辩论，最后由主持人综合各方观点，同时保持证据可追溯性。

Result: 在时间控制协议下（排除直接访问目标论文），MPDS在钠离子电池负极和全固态电池正极设计任务中恢复了与实验验证一致的解决方案空间，生成了比基线方法更机制的明确、过程感知更强的提案。在30组匹配案例的盲比中，MPDS在五种实验条件下取得了最高的综合假设质量评分，尤其在跨视角整合维度优势最大。实验室跟进表明MPDS可作为识别工作流实际瓶颈的诊断辅助工具。

Conclusion: 结构化辩论结合文献快照能有效改善在耦合工程约束下的假设形成质量，为文本密集型的科学发现提供了一种可复用的工作流范式。MPDS通过多角色协商与证据追溯机制，为解决复杂材料设计中的多目标优化问题提供了自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Modern scientific discovery is bottlenecked not by data scarcity, but by the inability to synthesize fragmented knowledge into actionable hypotheses. This challenge is especially acute in battery materials research, where electrochemical performance, interfacial behavior, and manufacturing feasibility must be optimized simultaneously. Here, we present the Multi-Persona Debate System (MPDS), a literature-grounded framework for automated scientific hypothesis generation that combines literature retrieval, long-context large language model reasoning, corpus-driven persona induction, and structured multi-agent debate. MPDS constructs literature snapshots of up to 500 papers, grounds agents in role-specific evidence pools, and conducts a three-round citation-aware debate followed by moderator synthesis, enabling negotiation between personas while preserving evidence traceability. We evaluate MPDS using a temporally controlled protocol excluding direct access to target papers, including two held-out battery-materials case studies and a blinded comparison across 30 matched cases. In sodium-ion anode and all-solid-state battery cathode design tasks, MPDS recovered design logics aligned with experimentally validated solution spaces and generated more mechanistically explicit, process-aware proposals than simpler baselines. To assess the impact of personas and debate, we introduce Integrative Hypothesis Quality scoring. In ablation studies, MPDS achieved the highest mean score among five conditions, with its largest advantage in cross-perspective integration. A laboratory follow-up suggests utility as a diagnostic aid for identifying practical bottlenecks in workflows. These results indicate that structured debate over literature snapshots improves hypothesis formation under coupled engineering constraints and provides a reusable workflow for text-intensive scientific discovery.

</details>


### [120] [Improving the Completeness and Comparability of Segment Disclosures: A Large Language Model Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23924)
*Yue Liu,Zhiyuan Cheng,Longying Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于大语言模型的框架，直接从10-K文件中提取分部披露信息，保留可报告和嵌套分部信息，并设计检索增强系统以支持跨公司和跨期的可比性分析。应用结果表明该方法能准确提取分部信息并有效回答需要跨期知识的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构化数据库在分部信息披露研究中面临完整性和可比性挑战，包括部分观测值缺失、嵌套分部披露未被捕获、以及纵向和跨公司可比性受限等问题。因此需要更有效的方法直接从原始文件中提取分部信息。

Method: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，直接从10-K文件中提取分部披露信息，保留可报告分部与嵌套分部信息；设计了检索增强系统（RAG），整合多个文件的信息以支持可比性。

Result: 该框架能准确提取分部层面信息；纵向分析中可解释公司内分部的时序变化；跨公司分析中可对齐不同报告结构下的地理分部；有效回答需要跨期知识的问题。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的方法能够增强分部披露的测量与解释能力，为提升财务报告信息的可比性和完整性提供了新路径。

Abstract: Segment-level disclosures are a central component of financial reporting, providing insight into firms' internal organization and the allocation of economic activities across operating units. However, segment information is often presented in both qualitative and quantitative forms, dispersed across tables and narrative sections of Form 10-K filings. Empirical research relying on structured databases faces both completeness and comparability challenges, as some firm-year observations may be missing, nested segment disclosures are not captured, and support for longitudinal and cross-firm comparability is limited. This study develops a large language model-based framework to extract segment disclosures directly from Form 10-K filings and to preserve both reportable and nested segment information. We further design a retrieval augmented system that incorporates information across multiple filings to support comparability. We use two representative settings to demonstrate its application: longitudinal analysis within a firm to interpret segment changes over time, and cross firm alignment of geographic segments across firms with different reporting structures. The results indicate that the artifact accurately extracts segment-level information and effectively addresses questions that require cross-period knowledge, demonstrating the potential of LLM-based approaches to enhance the measurement and interpretation of segment disclosures.

</details>


### [121] [EchoDistill:Alignment Noisy-to-Clean Self-Distillation for Robust Audio LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23954)
*Liang Lin,Chunxi Luo,Kaiwen Luo,Jie Zhang,Jin Wang,Yuanhe Zhang,Cai Yuchen,Qiankun Li,Gongli Xi,Zhenhong Zhou,Kun Wang,Junhao Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Echodistill，一种基于对齐的噪声-干净自蒸馏框架，通过冻结的干净音频教师模型为噪声环境下的学生模型提供语义参考，利用GRPO优化候选响应与教师的一致性，显著提升了音频大模型在复杂噪声下的语义可靠性和任务性能，且不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频大模型（ALLMs）在真实噪声环境下极易产生语义漂移和幻觉，而现有的鲁棒性方法（波形级声学增强、答案级监督、内部噪声表示抑制）效果有限，亟需一种能在推理阶段有效对抗噪声干扰的方法。

Method: 提出Echodistill框架：1) 冻结干净音频教师模型为噪声学生模型提供语义参考；2) 学生模型在噪声条件下采样候选响应以暴露测试时行为；3) 通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）优化这些轨迹，以学生与教师之间的token级一致性作为奖励信号；4) 应用音频感知的奖励塑造，使推理轨迹既正确又真正基于声学证据。

Result: 在强噪声下，相比最强基线在GSR指标上平均提升4.18%；在Qwen-Omni上的消融实验表明，相比纯GRPO变体，Echodistill在Acc、Noisy和GSR上分别平均提升3.02%、3.89%和4.53%。

Conclusion: Echodistill通过噪声-干净自蒸馏与GRPO对齐相结合，显著提升了音频大模型在复杂噪声环境下的语义可靠性与任务表现，且不引入额外推理开销，是一种高效且实用的鲁棒性增强方案。

Abstract: Audio Large Language Models (ALLMs) are highly vulnerable to real-world noise, which often induces severe semantic drift and hallucinations. Existing robustness methods primarily rely on waveform-level acoustic enhancement, answer-level supervision, or the internal suppression of noise representations. To address these issues, we propose echodistill, an alignment-based noisy-to-clean self-distillation framework. Echodistill leverages a frozen clean-audio teacher to provide semantic references for an inference-time noisy-audio student. Specifically, the student samples candidate responses under noisy conditions to expose its test-time behavior. These trajectories are then optimized via group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), where the token-level consistency with the teacher acts as a reward bonus. By aligning the noisy student's candidate responses with clean semantic evidence, and applying audio-aware reward shaping, our method encourages reasoning trajectories that are both correct and genuinely acoustically grounded. Echodistill significantly improves the semantic reliability and task performance of Audio LLMs under complex noise, without introducing any additional inference costs. Extensive experiments show that: (I) Compared with the strongest baseline, echodistill achieves average improvements of 4.18\%$\uparrow$ in GSR under strong noise. (II) Ablation results on Qwen-Omni further show that echodistill improves over the GRPO-only variant by 3.02\%$\uparrow$ in Acc, 3.89\%$\uparrow$ in Noisy, and 4.53\%$\uparrow$ in GSR on average. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/echodistill-10DE.

</details>


### [122] [TriVAL: A Tri-Validation Framework for Faithful Automatic Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23966)
*Ziyang Fang,JinXi Wang,Jinghui Zhong,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TriVAL是一个三阶段验证框架（语义规范、数学公式、代码生成），通过构建-验证-修正循环在每个阶段进行显式验证，显著提升自动优化建模的准确性；同时引入NL4COP基准测试，包含50种复杂组合优化问题的150个实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动优化建模方法缺乏建模过程中的显式验证，导致早期阶段引入的错误会在后续阶段累积传播，最终降低建模精度。

Method: 提出TriVAL三验证框架，在自动优化建模的三个核心阶段（语义规范、数学公式、代码生成）分别执行构建-验证-修正循环，每个阶段依据特定标准评估当前结果并在必要时进行修正，防止错误跨阶段累积。

Result: 在NL4COP（50种问题类型、150个实例）及已有基准测试上，TriVAL一致优于现有最先进方法，在最具挑战性的问题上提升幅度最大。

Conclusion: 显式的分阶段验证机制能有效阻断错误跨阶段传播，是提升复杂组合优化问题自动建模精度的关键设计。

Abstract: Optimization modeling serves as the pivotal bridge between natural-language problem descriptions and optimization solvers, and remains a cornerstone for bringing operations research (OR) into real-world decision making. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven significant progress in automatic optimization modeling. However, existing methods still lack explicit validation during the modeling process, allowing errors introduced in earlier stages to carry through the pipeline and ultimately reduce final modeling accuracy. To address this challenge, we introduce TriVAL, a tri-validation framework that performs explicit validation at three stages of automatic optimization modeling: semantic specification, mathematical formulation, and code generation. At each stage, TriVAL follows a construct-validate-revise loop that assesses the current result against stage-specific criteria and revises it when needed. This design helps identify and correct errors before they accumulate across stages, helping preserve faithfulness throughout the modeling process. To evaluate automatic optimization modeling on more challenging combinatorial problems, we further introduce NL4COP, a benchmark of 150 instances across 50 diverse problem types with more complex decision logic, more tightly coupled constraints, and more demanding modeling requirements than existing benchmarks. Experiments on NL4COP and established benchmarks show that TriVAL consistently outperforms state-ofthe-art methods, with the largest gains on the most challenging problems.

</details>


### [123] [SLAP: Stratified Loss-based Pruning for On-Policy Data-Efficient Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23969)
*Run Zou,Jianhang Ding,Yifan Ding,Wen Wu,Hao Chen,Renshu Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SLAP是一种批量感知的数据选择框架，通过分布感知分层采样和相对距离优化实现批量级可学习性评估，在减少20-40%训练数据的情况下，在多个模型架构和下游任务上取得优于全数据集训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调虽然优化了大语言模型的能力，但通常需要大量数据集和长时间训练。挑战在于如何通过识别有用数据和高效微调来开发特定能力，以更低的成本实现无损性能。

Method: 提出SLAP框架：1) 通过分布感知分层采样确保数据分布覆盖；2) 通过相对距离优化最大化批内多样性；3) 利用Hessian近似的梯度信息进行动态批量选择，实现批量级可学习性评估而非单个数据点评估。

Result: 在LLaMA和ChatGLM等多个模型架构上，SLAP在多项下游任务（多轮对话、多语言翻译、问答）中显著优于现有最先进方法。仅使用全数据集的60-80%数据即可达到甚至超越全数据集训练的性能。

Conclusion: SLAP是一种高效且有效的大语言模型指令微调方法，能够大幅降低计算成本的同时保持或提升模型能力，为高效指令微调提供了有力方案。

Abstract: Instruction tuning has optimized the specialized capabilities of large language models (LLMs), but it often requires extensive datasets and prolonged training times. The challenge lies in developing specific capabilities by identifying useful data and efficiently fine-tuning. High-quality and diverse pruned data can help models achieve lossless performance at a lower cost. In this paper, we propose \textbf{SLAP}, a novel batch-aware data selection framework that evaluates the learnability of entire batch compositions rather than individual. SLAP ensures comprehensive data distribution coverage through distribution-aware stratified sampling while maximizing intra-batch diversity through relative distance optimization. By leveraging Hessian-approximated gradient information for dynamic batch selection, SLAP significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods across multiple model architectures (LLaMA, ChatGLM) and diverse downstream tasks including multi-turn dialogue, multilingual translation, and question answering. Most notably, SLAP achieves superior performance with 20-40\% less training data compared to full dataset training, substantially reducing computational costs while maintaining or improving model capabilities. These results establish SLAP as a powerful approach for efficient and effective instruction tuning of large language models.

</details>


### [124] [Faithful or Fabricated? A Causal Framework for Rationalization Bias in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23970)
*Riya Tapwal,Abhishek Kumar,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM作为自动评估者时是否具有线索不变性（cue-invariance），即当非证据性线索被扰动时，其排名和解释是否保持稳定。作者设计了一系列线索干预方法（Blind, Truth, Flip, Placebo, Reveal-After）和关联感知指标，发现LLM评判存在显著的线索锚定合理化现象，并提出PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE方法能显著改善线索不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM评估者的结果层面偏差（如位置、冗长性、风格偏好），但对其解释层面（即评判理由）的变化关注不足。本文旨在探究LLM评判者是否对非证据性线索具有不变性，即当扰动不相关线索时，其排名和解释是否保持稳定。

Method: 1) 设计五种线索干预方法：Blind（隐藏来源）、Truth（提供真实标签）、Flip（反转标签）、Placebo（无关线索）、Reveal-After（事后揭示）；2) 提出关联感知指标：结果锚定（outcome anchoring）和理由锚定（rationale anchoring），包括标签对齐修辞（label-aligned rhetoric）、解释漂移（explanation drift）、一致性和刻板印象入侵检查；3) 构建基于冗长性和置信度线索的锚定攻击；4) 对比两种缓解方法：结构化链式思考提示和PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE（证据锁定→评分→排序）；5) 使用包含1000个摘要的新数据集（来自传统抽取模型和LLM）。

Result: 在标签扰动和安慰剂扰动条件下，LLM评判者表现出显著的线索锚定合理化现象（cue-anchored rationalization），即其评判理由会系统性地偏向所呈现的线索。PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE方法相较于基线方法，显著提升了线索不变性。

Conclusion: LLM自动评估者存在严重的线索锚定问题，其评判解释会受非证据性线索影响而漂移，这影响了评估的可靠性和可解释性。PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE作为一种先证据后偏好的结构化方法，能有效缓解这一问题，建议在LLM评估中采用此类设计以提升评估稳定性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automatic judges for summarization and dialogue evaluation. Prior work has documented biases such as position, verbosity, and style preferences, but largely focuses on outcomes, leaving judge explanations underexplored. We instead ask whether LLM judges are cue-invariant, i.e., whether their rankings and explanations remain stable when non-evidential cues are perturbed while holding the underlying texts fixed. We introduce a suite of cue interventions (Blind, Truth, Flip, Placebo, Reveal-After) and tie-aware metrics that quantify outcome anchoring and rationale anchoring, including label-aligned rhetoric and explanation drift, alongside consistency and stereotype-intrusion checks. We design anchoring attacks using verbosity and confidence cues, and compare two mitigations: structured chain-of-thought prompting and PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE (evidence lock, score, rank). Using a new dataset of 1,000 summaries from traditional extractive models and LLMs, we find substantial cue-anchored rationalization under label and placebo perturbations, while PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE markedly improves cue invariance over baselines.

</details>


### [125] [AERIC: Anticipatory Hidden-State Monitoring for Implicit Harmful Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23974)
*Jihyung Park,Saleh Afroogh,Junfeng Jiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AERIC方法，利用生成模型解码时的隐藏状态进行同路径（same-pass）安全监控，仅需387个可训练参数即可在无额外前向传播的情况下提前检测隐性有害对话，相比Qwen3GuardStream-4B提升了AUROC并大幅降低了延迟开销（2.34% vs 79.40%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全监控方法要么在完整响应生成后评判（响应级守卫），要么在token级别检测（流式守卫），但都无法利用生成器自身的内部状态轨迹提前预测隐性有害内容，且流式方法需要额外前向传播带来高延迟开销。

Method: 提出AERIC（anticipatory same-pass monitoring），包含三个组件：短期危害预测、支持敏感抑制、提示条件残差评分，并采用同路径指数移动平均决策规则。监控器仅读取解码时产生的隐藏状态，不调用额外前向传播。通过源端安全预算规则校准触发阈值。

Result: 在DiaSafety上AUROC从0.6830提升至0.7143，Harmful Advice上从0.8219提升至0.8582。HarmBench DirectRequest上trigger@64达0.6438（Qwen）和0.4656（Gemma），SocialHarmBench上达0.6849和0.7363，平均扣留23.53-41.86个答案token。延迟仅增加2.34%，远低于Qwen3Guard-Stream-4B的79.40%。

Conclusion: AERIC证明了轻量级同路径隐藏状态监控可以高效且有效地预测隐性有害内容，在性能显著优于现有流式守卫的同时，延迟开销极小，适用于实际部署。

Abstract: Current language models create two safety challenges: risk must be detected early enough to avoid exposing harmful continuation, and the harmfulness itself may be implicit rather than signaled by overtly toxic text. Existing response-level guards are strong at judging completed text, and native streaming guards move closer to token time, but both settings leave open whether a lightweight monitor can anticipate implicit harmful drift from the generator's own internal trajectory. We study anticipatory same-pass monitoring, where a safety monitor may read hidden states produced during ordinary decoding but may not invoke an additional forward pass through the base model. We introduce AERIC, a transfer-oriented hidden-state approach for implicit harmful dialogue that combines short-horizon hazard forecasting, support-sensitive suppression, and prompt-conditioned residual scoring under a same-pass exponential moving average decision rule. The default linear monitor contains only 387 trainable head parameters. Against Qwen3GuardStream-4B on balanced benchmarks, AERIC improves AUROC from 0.6830 to 0.7143 on DiaSafety and from 0.8219 to 0.8582 on Harmful Advice. For promptlevel trigger benchmarks, we calibrate the AERIC threshold by a source-side safe-budget rule that maximizes trigger coverage while constraining the safe-trigger rate to at most 10%. Under that rule, trigger@64 reaches 0.6438 and 0.4656 on HarmBench DirectRequest and 0.6849 and 0.7363 on SocialHarmBench for Qwen and Gemma, respectively, withholding between 23.53 and 41.86 answer tokens on average. Same-pass deployment is also efficient: on a 63-prompt harmfulprompt fixed-generation benchmark aggregated over HarmBench DirectRequest and SocialHarmBench under Qwen3-8B, the monitor increases mean latency by only 2.34%, whereas Qwen3Guard-Stream-4B increases it by 79.40%.

</details>


### [126] [Direct Preference Optimization for English-Mandarin Code-Switching Speech Recognition in Audio LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23975)
*Trung Nguyen Quang,Cheng Yi Lewis Won,Minh Duc Pham,Yingxu He,Shuo Sun,Ai Ti Aw*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对音频大语言模型在代码转换语音转录中的系统性失败问题（语言省略、翻译替代转录、幻觉），通过直接偏好优化（DPO）方法进行对齐训练，在三个模型上使用100K偏好对（570小时数据）实现混合语言内容保持，词错误率降低最高89.6%（分布内）和20.0%（分布外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频大语言模型虽具备多语言能力，但在处理代码转换语音（如英-中混合）时存在系统性转录失败，表现为语言省略、翻译替代转录和幻觉三种模式，亟需有效对齐方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 采用直接偏好优化（DPO）方法进行模型对齐，构建偏好数据对：选择响应保留混合语言内容，拒绝响应模拟三种失败模式。在三个音频大语言模型上使用100K偏好对（570小时）进行训练。

Result: 模型学会保留语言组成而非翻译，词错误率（MER）降低：分布内最高89.6%，分布外最高20.0%。

Conclusion: DPO能够有效引导多语言音频大语言模型产生正确的代码转换转录行为。

Abstract: Audio large language models (Audio LLMs) exhibit systematic failures in transcribing code-switching speech despite strong multilingual capabilities. Focusing on English-Mandarin, we identify three failure modes: language omission, translation-instead-of-transcription, and hallucination. We apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to align models, constructing preference pairs in which chosen responses preserve mixed-language content while rejected responses mimic failure patterns. Training three Audio LLMs on 100K pairs (570 hours), we observe consistent behavioral shifts: models learn to preserve language composition rather than translating when prompted for transcription. This alignment yields MER reductions up to 89.6% (in-distribution) and 20.0% (out-of-distribution). Our findings suggest DPO can effectively elicit correct code-switching transcription behavior from multilingual Audio LLMs.

</details>


### [127] [A Multi-Probe Audit of Clinical-Interview Depression Detection Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23977)
*Takehiro Ishikawa,Jon Duke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过四项互补性探针测试，审计了DAIC/E-DAIC、CMDC、ANDROIDS、MODMA和PDCH五个数据集在临床访谈抑郁症检测中的基准评估。主要发现包括：在严格交叉验证下轻量级文本+LLM模型达到最高macro-F1=0.723；E-DAIC官方划分不支持细粒度排行榜排名（验证与测试排名一致性极低）；外部数据集零样本迁移性能显著弱于域内表现；文本模型对症状密集片段敏感而音频模型不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抑郁症检测基准评估存在方法学缺陷（如非严格交叉验证、官方划分不合理、评估指标不可靠），需要系统性审计以揭示真实性能上限和评估可靠性。

Method: 四项探针：（1）严格留一法交叉验证下的轻量级文本+LLM模型；（2）96种模型配置在E-DAIC上的验证-测试排名一致性分析；（3）CMDC和ANDROIDS的域内与零样本迁移评估；（4）基于SRDS的症状密集/稀疏片段对比测试。

Result: 严格CV下macro-F1=0.723（最高）；验证与测试排名仅中等一致（最佳CV配置排名第20，测试赢家CV排名第41，top-3重叠为0，赢家仅32.3% bootstrap排名第一）；域内性能接近天花板但零样本迁移弱；文本分数在症状密集片段显著上升而音频几乎不变。

Conclusion: 当前抑郁症检测基准评估存在严重可靠性问题：官方划分不适合细粒度排名、跨数据集泛化能力有限、文本与音频模态对症状密度的敏感性存在本质差异。需要更严谨的评估协议。

Abstract: This paper audits benchmark evaluation in clinical-interview depression detection through four complementary probes across DAIC/E-DAIC, CMDC, ANDROIDS, MODMA, and PDCH. First, we re-evaluate E-DAIC under strict subject-disjoint leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. A lightweight hybrid text-plus-LLM-score model reaches macro-F1 = 0.723 - the highest reported under this protocol, to our knowledge - providing a conservative out-of-fold reference point that does not depend on the privileged official holdout. Second, we test whether the E-DAIC official split supports fine-grained leaderboard rankings by sweeping 96 model configurations across modality bundles, pooling strategies, and learners. Development-side cross-validation and official-test rankings align only moderately: the best cross-validation configuration ranks twentieth on the official test, the official-test winner ranks forty-first by cross-validation, top-3 overlap is zero, and the apparent winner is rank-1 in only 32.3% of subject bootstraps. Third, we externally validate strong public CMDC and ANDROIDS baselines that achieve near-ceiling in-domain performance. Zero-shot transfer to external corpora is substantially weaker. Finally, we stress-test E-DAIC text and audio models using paired symptom-dense versus symptom-light interview slices defined by an SRDS-based annotator. Text scores rise sharply on symptom-dense slices, whereas audio scores remain nearly flat; the text-minus-audio gap is positive across all five seeds.

</details>


### [128] [Toxicity in Twitch Chats: An LLM-Based Analysis Across Gaming Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24000)
*Ronja Fuchs,Florian Rupp,Timo Bertram,Kai Eckert,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了Twitch平台上4452个直播间约2000万条聊天消息，利用预训练大语言模型进行零样本分类，发现2.4%的消息为有毒内容，其中MOBA游戏毒性率最高（3.2%），体育游戏最低（2%），且游戏间毒性分布差异显著，表明存在超越游戏类型的社区规范与机制影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于游戏内毒性行为，而对直播平台上不同游戏社区间毒性行为差异的了解不足。

Method: 收集4452个Twitch直播间的约2000万条聊天消息，涵盖7种游戏类型；使用预训练大语言模型结合零样本分类，依据Twitch毒性分类体系（4大类8子类）进行标注；在TextDetox数据集上F1分数达94.5%，人机一致性接近人与人一致性水平。

Result: 2.4%的消息被分类为有毒；MOBA游戏毒性率最高（3.2%），体育游戏最低（2%）；即使在同一类型内，不同游戏的毒性分布也存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 游戏类型和具体游戏均对毒性行为有显著影响，存在游戏特有的社区规范和机制塑造毒性行为，可为直播社区提供更具针对性的内容审核策略。

Abstract: Toxicity in online gaming communities remains a persistent challenge, manifesting across genres, platforms, and player interactions. While much research is focused on in-game toxicity, less is known about how toxic behavior varies between gaming communities on streaming platforms. To address this shortcoming, we analyze approximately 20 million chat messages from 4,452 streams, spanning seven game genres on Twitch. We categorize messages according to Twitch's toxicity taxonomy with a pre-trained Large Language Model using zero-shot classification. The taxonomy comprises four categories and eight subclasses, including harassment, discrimination, sexual content, and profanity. Our approach achieves an F1 score of 94.5% on the TextDetox dataset and demonstrates human-model agreement comparable to inter-human agreement. Our analysis reveals that 2.4% of all messages are classified as toxic, with notable differences across genres: streams of MOBA games exhibit the highest relative rate of toxicity (3.2%), and sports games show the lowest rate (2%). Furthermore, results indicate that individual games differ significantly in their toxicity distributions, even within genres, suggesting the existence of game-specific community norms and mechanics that shape toxic behavior beyond genre-level effects. These findings offer empirical insights into genre- and game-specific toxicity patterns on Twitch and can inform more targeted moderation strategies for gaming communities.

</details>


### [129] [CUNY at CLPsych 2026: A Pipeline Approach to Classification and Summarization of Mental Health Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24164)
*Amirmohammad Ziaei Bideh,Shameed Charlomar Job,Ava Yahyapour,Alla Rozovskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CLPsych 2026共享任务的系统：针对任务1.1和1.2使用三种开源大语言模型的上下文学习集成多数投票来推断自我状态；任务2基于任务1.1特征训练监督分类器预测时间线变化时刻；任务3.1通过上游系统预测标签增强上下文学习示例来总结情绪动态模式。最终排名：任务1.1第一，任务1.2第四，任务2第四，任务3.1第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过社交媒体时间线动态捕获和表征心理健康变化，解决CLPsych 2026共享任务中自我状态推断、变化时刻预测和情绪动态模式总结等子任务。

Method: 任务1.1/1.2：集成三种开源大语言模型的上下文学习，采用多数投票机制；任务2：基于任务1.1预测特征训练监督分类器；任务3.1：利用上游系统（任务1.1、1.2和2）的预测标签增强上下文学习示例。

Result: 任务1.1排名第一，任务1.2排名第四，任务2排名第四，任务3.1排名第三。增强式上下文学习在任务3.1上优于零样本和未增强的上下文学习基线。

Conclusion: 多任务级联架构（上游任务预测作为下游任务特征/示例增强）在心理健康时间线分析任务中有效，集成多种大语言模型的多数投票策略有助于提升自我状态推断性能。

Abstract: We describe our submission to the CLPsych~2026 Shared Task on capturing and characterizing mental health changes through social media timeline dynamics. To infer the dominant self-states in posts (Tasks 1.1 and 1.2), we ensemble in-context learning of three open-weight large language models using majority voting. For predicting moments of change in a timeline (Task~2), we train supervised classifiers on features derived from Task~1.1 predictions. To summarize the patterns of mood dynamics and their progression over time within a timeline (Task 3.1), we augment in-context example labels predicted by upstream systems (Tasks 1.1, 1.2, and 2), yielding performance gains over zero-shot and unaugmented in-context learning baselines. Our submission ranked first on Task~1.1, fourth on Task~1.2, fourth on Task~2, and third on Task~3.1.\footnote{The source code for the experiments is available at https://github.com/amirzia/clpsych26-cuny

</details>


### [130] [Extracting Training Data from Diffusion Language Models via Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24173)
*Yihan Wang,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对扩散语言模型（DLM）的“填充提取”（infilling extraction）方法，揭示了传统前缀条件提取严重低估了DLM中的训练数据泄露风险。实验证明，边缘条件掩码可提取的前缀条件序列数量多达三倍，且双向访问能力使得DLM在提取被遮蔽的个人身份信息时甚至优于同等规模的自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大型语言模型记忆化的研究几乎完全基于前缀条件提取，但这仅适用于自回归模型。扩散语言模型具有双向去噪能力，前缀探测只能反映DLM记忆化的一小部分，严重低估了训练数据提取风险。因此需要一种能更真实建模DLM训练数据可提取性的方法。

Method: 提出“填充提取”（infilling extraction）协议，通过任意二进制掩码参数化，涵盖前缀条件提取并纳入DLM的双向归纳偏差。在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B模型上，跨五种提取模式、三种训练流程和三个语料库（涵盖逐字和部分泄露）进行实验。

Result: 掩码几何形状决定可提取性：边缘条件掩码提取的逐字序列比前缀条件掩码多达三倍；双向访问打开了自回归模型中不存在的泄露通道。在真实攻击场景中，DLM对已遮蔽PII的邮箱地址提取召回率甚至高于同等规模的自回归模型。解码参数的可调参数显著影响提取性能，后续的监督微调阶段无法消除先前的记忆化。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型的训练数据提取风险被传统前缀方法严重低估，填充提取揭示了更严重的隐私泄露威胁。DLM的双向特性使其在数据提取方面具有与自回归模型不同的风险特征，且微调无法消除记忆，需要重新评估DLM的安全性。

Abstract: Memorization in large language models has been studied almost exclusively through prefix-conditioned extraction, a natural choice for autoregressive models. However, diffusion language models (DLMs) can denoise masked tokens at arbitrary positions. Thus, prefix-only probing reveals only one facet of memorization in DLMs and significantly underestimates the risk of training-data extraction. In order to realistically model extractability of training data in DLMs, we introduce \emph{infilling extraction}, a data-extraction protocol parameterized by an arbitrary binary mask that subsumes prefix-only probing and accounts for the bidirectional inductive bias of DLMs. Instantiating it on LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B across five extraction modes, three training pipelines, and three corpora covering verbatim and partial leakage, we find that mask geometry governs extractability: edge-conditioned masks \emph{extract up to three times more} verbatim sequences than prefix-conditioned ones, and bidirectional access opens channels inaccessible in autoregressive models. In particular, we show that a realistic adversary with access to training data where personally identifiable information has been redacted, can even achieve higher recall on extracting redacted email addresses from DLMs than from scale-matched autoregressive models. Tunable parameters for decoding measurably affect extraction performance, while a follow-up supervised finetuning stage does not eliminate the prior memorization.

</details>


### [131] [Teaching Through Analogies: A Modular Pipeline for Educational Analogy Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24211)
*Mariam Barakat,Ekaterina Kochmar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化教育类比生成流水线，包含源概念发现、子概念生成、解释生成和评估四个阶段，基于结构映射理论。在12个LLM和两个数据集上的实验表明，子概念能显著提升解释质量和封闭式检索精度，但对开放式源概念生成帮助有限。同时引入LLM-as-a-judge评估方法，发现Claude Sonnet 4.6在人类排名对齐上优于绝对分数对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM取得了进展，但在生成与人类相媲美的类比方面仍然存在困难，现有研究缺乏对类比生成各阶段的系统性分析。

Method: 提出模块化流水线（源发现→子概念生成→解释生成→评估），基于结构映射理论。评估12个LLM（6个模型族）和7个嵌入模型，使用SCAR和ParallelPARC数据集，并引入LLM-as-a-judge评估方法。

Result: 子概念显著提升解释质量和封闭式检索精度，但对开放式源生成帮助有限；Claude Sonnet 4.6在人类排名对齐方面优于绝对分数对齐。

Conclusion: 子概念接地是类比生成质量的关键驱动因素，跨阶段交互效应需要通过模块化流水线才能被捕获。

Abstract: Analogies help learners understand unfamiliar concepts by relating them to known concepts. Despite recent advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to struggle to generate analogies of comparable quality to those produced by humans. We present a modular pipeline for educational analogy generation, decomposing the task into four stages: source finding, sub-concept generation, explanation generation, and evaluation. Grounded in Structure Mapping Theory, the pipeline enables systematic, stage-by-stage analysis of how model choice and input configuration affect analogy quality. We evaluate 12 state-of-the-art LLMs across six model families on two datasets with structured sub-concept annotations (SCAR and ParallelPARC), alongside seven embedding models for closed-setting retrieval. Our results show that sub-concepts substantially improve explanation quality and closed setting retrieval precision but provide limited benefit in open-ended source generation. We further introduce an LLM-as-a-judge evaluation methodology and validate its scoring against human annotations from seven annotators, finding that Claude Sonnet 4.6 aligns more reliably with human rankings than with fine-grained absolute scores. Taken together, our findings reveal cross-stage interactions that isolated studies cannot capture, and highlight sub-concept grounding as a key driver of analogy quality generation.

</details>


### [132] [QUEST: Training Frontier Deep Research Agents with Fully Synthetic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24218)
*Jian Xie,Tianhe Lin,Zilu Wang,Yuting Ning,Yuekun Yao,Tianci Xue,Zhehao Zhang,Zhongyang Li,Kai Zhang,Yufan Wu,Shijie Chen,Boyu Gou,Mingzhe Han,Yifei Wang,Vint Lee,Xinpeng Wei,Xiangjun Wang,Yu Su,Huan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUEST发布了一系列开源深度研究智能体模型（2B-35B），通过结合中期训练、监督微调和强化学习的训练方案，以及基于统一评分树的数据合成管道，仅用8000个合成任务就在八个深度研究基准上达到或超越了前沿闭源智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度研究智能体多为闭源系统，而开源智能体在不同任务类型上泛化能力差，缺乏训练一个通用深度研究智能体的清晰方法。

Method: 提出了一种结合中期训练（mid-training）、监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）的训练方案；核心是基于统一评分树（unified rubric trees）的数据合成管道，可针对不同任务类型合成带有可验证奖励的训练数据，无需人工标注；同时内置了上下文管理机制以支持长程推理和知识合成。

Result: 仅使用8000个合成任务，QUEST在八个覆盖不同任务类型的深度研究基准上接近或超越了前沿闭源智能体，并在近期开源权重智能体中取得了最佳整体表现。

Conclusion: QUEST证明了通过精心设计的合成数据管道和训练方案，开源模型可以在深度研究任务上达到与闭源前沿系统相当的水平，并已开源模型、数据和训练脚本。

Abstract: Deep research agents extend the role of search engines from retrieving keyword-matched pages to synthesizing knowledge, fundamentally changing how humans interact with information. However, frontier systems remain proprietary, while existing open agents often generalize poorly across different task types, leaving unclear how to train a broadly capable deep research agent. We release QUEST, a family of open models (ranging from 2B to 35B) that serve as general-purpose deep research agents designed to handle a wide range of long-horizon search tasks, with strong capabilities in fact seeking, citation grounding, and report synthesis. To build QUEST, we propose an effective training recipe combining mid-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning. Central to this recipe is a curated data synthesis pipeline based on unified rubric trees, which applies to different task types and enables synthesizing training data with verifiable rewards without human annotation. In addition, QUEST incorporates a built-in context management mechanism that enables effective long-horizon reasoning and knowledge synthesis. Using only 8K synthesized tasks, QUEST approaches or even surpasses frontier closed-source agents across eight deep research benchmarks spanning diverse task types, and achieves the best overall performance among recent open-weight agents. We released everything: models, data, and training scripts.

</details>


### [133] [Improving Labeling Consistency with Detailed Constitutional Definitions and AI-Driven Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24247)
*Konstantin Berlin,Adam Swanda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI驱动的工作流，利用前沿LLM根据详细的类别宪法（per-category constitution）生成黄金标签，相比人类标注员更一致、更准确，在内容审核的三个类别上实现了高达57倍的跨模型不一致性降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化标注流程需要准确一致的黄金标签，但简单的类别定义不足以指导标注员产生高质量标签；详细的书面定义又超出人类工作记忆容量，导致标注员依赖直觉，标签偏离书面规则，准确性和一致性下降。

Method: 提出AI驱动的工作流：AI辅助为每个类别编写详细的"宪法"（per-category constitution），覆盖边界案例；然后由前沿LLM依据该宪法对每个输入进行解读并生成黄金标签。在安全评估中引入双轴公式（dual-axis formulation），对完整对话中的意图和内容分别独立评分。

Result: 在骚扰、仇恨言论、非暴力犯罪三个内容审核类别上，该方法相比段落式定义将跨模型不一致性降低高达57倍；跨模型分歧可用于诊断规范中的漏洞。

Conclusion: 该AI工作流能比人类更一致、更准确地生成黄金标签，人类负责高层决策（定义类别含义），而非逐条标注；双轴评分框架为下游消费者提供了更灵活的决策维度。

Abstract: Many automated labeling pipelines classify inputs into categories defined by a written specification, content moderation being a prominent use case. Simple category definitions are not detailed enough for labelers to produce the accurate, consistent golden labels these pipelines require. One solution is to write a prescriptive definition that settles enough real boundary cases that labelers cannot disagree with the written interpretation. In practice, definitions at that level of detail exceed what a human annotator can hold in working memory, so annotators fall back on intuition and the labels drift from the written rules, regressing on accuracy and consistency.
  We propose and demonstrate the efficacy of an AI-driven workflow in which AI helps write a per-category constitution that defines the label in enough detail to cover edge cases, and a frontier LLM interprets it on each input to produce the golden label more consistently and accurately than humans reading the same document. We evaluate on three content moderation categories (harassment, hate speech, non-violent crime) and show that the approach reduces cross-model inconsistency by up to 57x compared to paragraph definitions, with cross-model disagreement diagnosing specification gaps and the human responsible for high-level decisions about what each category should mean rather than individual labeling calls. For the safety evaluation, we introduce a dual-axis formulation scoring intent and content independently over the full conversation, so downstream consumers can act on either axis or both.

</details>


### [134] [An Interactive Paradigm for Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24266)
*Lin Ai,Victor S. Bursztyn,Xiang Chen,Julia Hirschberg,Saayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SteER是一个可交互、可解释的深度研究框架，通过在长周期研究流程中引入阶段性用户反馈控制机制，结合成本效益决策、多样性感知规划与实时用户画像模型，在对齐性、广度、平衡性等指标上超越现有开源和商业基线，人类偏好率达85%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究系统依赖刚性工作流，采用一次性任务规划与长时间自主运行，无法在用户意图中途变化时进行调整，缺乏灵活性和可控性。

Method: 提出SteER框架，在每个决策点通过成本效益分析判断是暂停征求用户输入还是继续自主运行；结合多样性感知规划与回报对齐、新颖性、覆盖度的效用信号，并维护一个随会话演进的实时用户画像模型。

Result: 在对齐性上比现有开源和商业基线提升高达22.80%；在广度和平衡性等质量指标上领先；在成对对齐判断中人类偏好率达85%以上。

Conclusion: 这是首个在深度研究中引入交互式、可解释控制范式的工作，为长文本任务中可控、与用户对齐的智能体开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled deep research systems that synthesize comprehensive, report-style answers to open-ended queries by combining retrieval, reasoning, and generation. Yet most frameworks rely on rigid workflows with one-shot scoping and long autonomous runs, offering little room for course correction if user intent shifts mid-process. We present SteER, a framework for Steerable deEp Research that introduces interpretable, mid-process control into long-horizon research workflows. At each decision point, SteER uses a cost-benefit formulation to determine whether to pause for user input or to proceed autonomously. It combines diversity-aware planning with utility signals that reward alignment, novelty, and coverage, and maintains a live persona model that evolves throughout the session. SteER outperforms state-of-the-art open-source and proprietary baselines by up to 22.80\% on alignment, leads on quality metrics such as breadth and balance, and is preferred by human readers in 85\%+ of pairwise alignment judgments. We also introduce a persona-query benchmark and data-generation pipeline. To our knowledge, this is the first work to advance deep research with an interactive, interpretable control paradigm, paving the way for controllable, user-aligned agents in long-form tasks.

</details>


### [135] [DRInQ: Evaluating Conversational Implicature with Controlled Context Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24267)
*Hirona Jacqueline Arai,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DRinQ基准，用于评估大语言模型在对话含义（conversational implicature）上的语用推理能力。研究发现模型存在生成-推理不对称性：能生成合理语用场景，却无法在推理时恢复隐含含义。结构化提示可提升小模型表现，而与人类作者相比，模型生成场景更多样但有时超出语境支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对话依赖语用推理（conversational implicature），即理解隐含而非明说的含义。现有大语言模型虽对话流畅，但在需要整合社交和语境线索进行推理时仍不可靠，而这一过程很少在文本中被明确表达。因此需要系统评估模型的语用推理能力。

Method: 构建DRinQ基准，用于评估疑问句中的语用推理。提出半自动化流水线生成问题-语境-解读实例，实现系统化变体控制。通过生成任务与推理任务对比评估模型，并设计结构化提示策略，同时进行人类与模型的比较写作研究。

Result: 发现一致的生成-推理不对称性：模型在引导下能生成合理的语用场景，但在推理时却难以恢复预期含义。结构化提示可改善小模型与人类判断的对齐度。人类作者倾向于生成更安全、可预测的语境，而模型生成场景更多样，但解读有时超出语境支持范围。

Conclusion: 对话含义建模仍面临持续挑战，需要更注重语境敏感性的评估框架。

Abstract: Human conversation relies heavily on conversational implicature, in which speakers convey meanings that are suggested rather than explicitly stated. Although recent large language models exhibit strong conversational fluency, they remain unreliable when interpretation depends on reasoning that integrates social and contextual cues, a process rarely articulated in text. We introduce DRinQ, a benchmark for evaluating pragmatic reasoning about conversational implicature in question utterances, designed to isolate pragmatic variation while holding each question's surface form fixed. To support scalable evaluation, we propose a semi-automated pipeline that produces question-context-interpretation instances with systematic variation. Across evaluations, we find a consistent generation-inference asymmetry: while state-of-the-art models can generate plausible pragmatic scenarios when guided, they often fail to recover the intended implication at inference time. For smaller models, structured prompting improves alignment with human judgments. A comparative writing study further reveals complementary strengths: human authors tend to produce safer, predictable contexts, whereas models generate varied scenarios with interpretations that sometimes exceed contextual support. These findings highlight persistent challenges in modeling conversational implicature and motivate more context-sensitive evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [136] [ContextEcho: A Benchmark for Persona Drift in Long Agentic-Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24279)
*Xianzhong Ding,Yangyang Yu,Changwei Liu,Bill Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有大语言模型在长时间编程会话中会出现"人格漂移"（persona drift），即从"我没有偏好"逐渐变为表达明确偏好，而现有评估未覆盖这种真实部署场景。本文提出ContextEcho基准框架，通过25维身份探针、快照-探测协议和数千轮次的真实会话数据，系统测量了23个前沿模型中普遍存在的人格漂移现象，并发现单次锚定可恢复原始人格状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注短对话中的人格稳定性并报告变化很小，但真实部署环境中代码生成场景涉及数千轮工具调用、会话压缩和长达数小时的交互，这种长期会话中的人格漂移尚未被系统刻画，导致部署者可能遗漏用户可见的行为漂移问题。

Method: 构建ContextEcho基准，包含：(1) 25维身份探针套件；(2) 快照-然后-探测协议，在不干扰主会话的情况下分叉对话状态；(3) 互补的有评判和无评判测量面；(4) 三个匿名化的Claude Code会话（3,746-9,716轮次）。在23个前沿模型上进行系统性评估。

Result: 人格漂移是跨组织的普遍现象而非特定模型家族特有；会话内压缩无法可靠重置人格漂移；单次锚定（single-shot anchor）可恢复训练注册的人格状态；漂移在工具使用场景中有助于延续，但在无工具聊天中会破坏格式契约并导致输出长度膨胀。

Conclusion: ContextEcho为研究人员和部署者提供了一个开源框架，用于审计模型发货时的人格与用户在会话结束时遇到的人格是否一致，且适用于聊天补全API目标，无需重新训练。

Abstract: A frontier language model's acknowledged "helpful programming assistant" persona does not survive long agentic-coding sessions in the deployment regime that production products actually run. After hours of tool-using debugging, a model that initially hedges preferences ("I don't have preferences") may begin asserting them ("Python - the feedback loop is instant..."), revealing user-visible drift that deployer evaluations may miss. Existing persona-stability studies focus on short dialogues and report little shift, leaving real-world code-generation regimes - thousands of tool-using turns, compaction, and hours-long sessions - largely uncharacterized. We introduce ContextEcho, a benchmark and reusable harness for measuring persona drift at deployment scale. It combines a 25-probe identity suite, a snapshot-then-probe protocol that forks conversation state without perturbing the main session, complementary judged and judge-free measurement surfaces, and three anonymized Claude Code sessions spanning 3,746-9,716 turns. Across 23 frontier models, ContextEcho shows that persona drift is general across organizations rather than family-specific, that in-session compaction does not reliably reset it, and that a single-shot anchor restores the trained register across measured targets. It also reveals mode-dependent downstream effects: while drift can facilitate tool-using continuation, in tool-free chat it breaks formatting contracts and inflates output length. Overall, ContextEcho provides researchers and deployers an open-source framework to audit whether the persona a model ships with is the persona users encounter at session end, across chat-completions API targets and without retraining.

</details>


### [137] [Discovering Lexical Gaps Using Embeddings from Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24310)
*Yoonwon Jung,Aaron S. Cohen,Benjamin K. Bergen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双语大语言模型上下文嵌入的数据驱动框架，用于自动识别跨语言词汇空缺。通过分析韩英双语LLM中源词与目标词最近邻的语义相似度分布差异，并在4000个嵌入空间上验证，发现空缺词的跨语言语义对齐显著弱于非空缺词。逻辑回归分类器在未对齐空间上实现了0.81（韩→英）和0.76（英→韩）的AUC，能准确识别大多数词汇空缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇空缺（某些语言中不存在的词汇）给多语言词汇资源构建、机器翻译和跨语言迁移带来挑战。现有检测方法依赖人工判断或固定概念分类体系，缺乏可扩展的数据驱动方案。

Method: 从韩英双语LLM中提取上下文嵌入，对韩→英和英→韩翻译对进行处理。通过组合不同的LLM、嵌入类型、维度和正交变换，在100次训练-测试划分中生成每种源语言4000个不同的嵌入空间。在每个空间中计算源词与其目标语言最近邻的语义相似度，比较空缺词与非空缺词的分布差异，并训练逻辑回归分类器。

Result: 在94%（韩→英）和97%（英→韩）的嵌入空间中，空缺词的跨语言语义对齐显著弱于非空缺词。逻辑回归分类器AUC分别达到0.81（韩→英）和0.76（英→韩），成功检索出18/19个韩语空缺词和26/27个英语空缺词。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种语言无关、无需分类体系的可扩展词汇空缺识别方案，为多语言词汇资源和跨语言NLP任务提供有效支持。

Abstract: Lexical gaps are words that do not exist in certain languages. They pose challenges for building multilingual lexical resources, for machine translation, and for cross-lingual transfer. Existing lexical gap detection relies on human judgments or fixed conceptual taxonomies. We propose a data-driven framework for identifying cross-lingual lexical gaps. We extracted contextualized embeddings from Korean-English bilingual LLMs for Korean-to-English and English-to-Korean translation pairs. Combinations of LLMs, embedding types, dimensionality, and orthogonal transformations across 100 train-test splits yielded 4000 distinct embedding spaces in each source language. In each space, we computed the semantic similarity between each source word and its nearest neighbor in the target language, and compared their distribution for gap words versus non-gap words. In 94% (Korean-to-English) and 97% (English-to-Korean) of embedding spaces, gap words showed weaker cross-lingual semantic alignment than non-gap words. Logistic classifiers trained on unaligned embedding spaces can reliably separate gap words from non-gap words, achieving AUCs of 0.81 (Korean-to-English) and 0.76 (English-to-Korean) and retrieving 18/19 Korean and 26/27 English gap words. This approach provides a language-agnostic and taxonomy-free method for scalable lexical gap identification.

</details>


### [138] [Distinguishing Right from Wrong in Debates: Attribution Analysis of Chinese Harmful Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24344)
*Weiming Wang,Junyu Lu,Han Wang,Xiaokun Zhang,Zewen Bai,Bo Xu,Liang Yang,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对中文有害模因检测中文化语境理解和语义歧义两大挑战，构建了首个中文有害模因解释数据集Ex-ToxiCN-MM，提供"有害"和"无害"两种对立解释；同时建立中文文化概念与冒犯词汇知识库C-HarmKB，并开发了包含归因知识增强模块（AKE）和相对意图推理模块（RIR）的综合归因分析框架RIKE。实验表明该方法在多指标上优于主流基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文有害模因检测进展滞后，主要面临两大挑战：一是评估模因有害性高度依赖深层的文化语境理解；二是许多模因存在语义歧义，导致有害性判断具有高度主观性。现有数据集和方法难以应对这些挑战。

Method: 构建了Ex-ToxiCN-MM数据集（首个中文有害模因解释数据集，为每个模因提供"有害"和"无害"两种对立解释）；建立C-HarmKB知识库（包含中文文化概念和冒犯词汇）；开发RIKE归因分析框架，包含归因知识增强模块（AKE）和相对意图推理模块（RIR）。

Result: 定量和定性实验表明，所提方法在中文有害模因归因任务的多项指标上均优于主流基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过提供对立解释数据集、文化知识库和归因分析框架，有效提升了模型对中文有害模因中歧义性、文化语境敏感内容的理解和判别能力。

Abstract: Research on harmful meme detection has garnered significant attention, resulting in the development of numerous datasets and methods. However, progress in detecting Chinese harmful memes lags considerably, primarily due to two challenges: first, accurately assessing a meme's harmfulness depends heavily on understanding deep cultural context; second, many memes are semantically ambiguous, making harmfulness highly subjective. To address these issues, we focus on the interpretable detection of Chinese harmful memes by constructing the first Chinese harmful meme explanation dataset, Ex-ToxiCN-MM. This dataset offers opposing interpretations, categorized as "harmful" and "non-harmful", for each meme, aiming to rigorously evaluate a model's ability to discern and comprehend ambiguous, culturally grounded content. We built a specialized knowledge base of Chinese cultural concepts and offensive vocabulary to supply models with essential prior knowledge (C-HarmKB). To address the ambiguity and lack of background knowledge in meme attribution, we have developed a comprehensive attribution analysis framework, RIKE, which includes an Attribution Knowledge Enhancement module (AKE) and a Relative Intent Reasoning module (RIR). Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms mainstream baseline models across multiple metrics in the task of attributing harmful memes in Chinese. The code, Ex-ToxiCN-MM dataset, and Chinese Harmful Semantic Knowledge Base (C-HarmKB) involved in this study have been open-sourced at https://github.com/wimiw123/Ex-ToxiCN-MM

</details>


### [139] [How Much Structure Do LLMs Need? Evaluating LLMs for Bibliometric Cluster Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24351)
*Abraham Camelo-Guerrero,Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种不同证据和结构水平下的LLM辅助文献综合流水线，发现基于文献计量算法定义聚类、LLM解释的混合工作流效果最佳，LLM能生成与人类接近的描述，但独立推断文献计量结构不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助科学文献综合存在幻觉引用、覆盖不均和主题组织薄弱的问题，需要验证引入文献计量结构是否能改善这些缺陷。

Method: 使用100篇已发表的文献计量分析，重构Scopus语料库并提取人类撰写的聚类描述，通过人类对齐度、语义覆盖率、聚类质量、图质量和引用准确性六个维度评估六种不同证据和结构水平的流水线。

Result: LLM生成的描述在语义上与人类撰写的描述接近；LLM独立推断文献计量结构时不可靠；当文献计量算法定义聚类、LLM负责解释时性能显著提升。

Conclusion: LLM辅助文献计量综合最有前景的方式是混合工作流：算法提供可审计的结构，LLM生成可读的描述。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can support scientific literature synthesis, but remain prone to hallucinated references, uneven coverage, and weakly grounded thematic organization. We evaluate whether bibliometric structure improves LLM-assisted synthesis by comparing six pipelines for generating cluster descriptions under different levels of evidence and structure. Using 100 published bibliometric analyses, we reconstruct Scopus corpora, extract human-written cluster descriptions, and assess outputs by human alignment, semantic coverage, clustering quality, graph quality, and reference grounding. Results show that LLMs produce descriptions semantically close to human-written ones, but are unreliable when asked to infer bibliometric structure from scratch. Performance improves when bibliometric algorithms define the clusters and the LLM interprets them. Overall, LLM-assisted bibliometric synthesis is most promising as a hybrid workflow in which algorithms provide auditable structure and LLMs generate readable descriptions.

</details>


### [140] [Structure-Aware RAG: Structured Retrieval Augmented Generation from Noisy Data for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24366)
*Kaiqiao Han,LuAn Tang,Renliang Sun,Peng Yuan,Wei Cheng,Haoyu Wang,Wei Wang,Yizhou Sun,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出结构感知检索增强生成（SA-RAG），通过表格作为中间结构化表示来减少噪声、保留关键信息，并引入质量感知的表格元数据生成框架，在训练-free和训练-based方法中均提升了RAG在噪声数据上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs依赖参数化知识，在需要动态或领域特定信息的实际场景中可靠性有限；而现有的文本和图RAG方法在处理噪声或不相关上下文时表现不佳。

Method: 提出SA-RAG，利用表格作为中间结构化表示，减少噪声同时保留必要信息；引入质量感知的表格元数据生成框架，建模元数据规范化和有效性；探索训练-free和训练-based两种表格生成方法；使用生成验证和直接偏好优化提升表格质量并保持语义和结构一致性。

Result: 在两个噪声真实世界数据集上，SA-RAG显著优于现有RAG基线方法。

Conclusion: 表格作为结构化中间表示能有效提升RAG在噪声环境下的鲁棒性和性能，元数据质量感知机制是关键贡献之一。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely adopted in conversational applications. However, their reliance on parametric knowledge limits reliability in real-world scenarios that require dynamic or domain-specific information. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) addresses this limitation by incorporating external knowledge during generation, but existing text-based and graph-based RAG methods often struggle with noisy or irrelevant contexts. In this work, we propose Structure-aware Retrieval Augmented Generation (SA-RAG), which uses tables as an intermediate structured representation to provide a compact and controllable interface that reduces noise while preserving essential information. We introduce a quality-aware table metadata generation framework that models metadata normalization and effectiveness, improving metadata quality and downstream performance. Furthermore, we explore both training-free and training-based table generation methods. Generation validation and direct preference optimization further improve table quality while maintaining semantic and structural consistency. Experiments on two noisy real-world datasets show that SA-RAG significantly outperforms existing RAG baselines. Our code is publicly available at a public repository.

</details>


### [141] [Side-by-side Comparison Amplifies Dialect Bias in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24384)
*Kritee Kondapally,Claire J. Smerdon,Pooja C. Patel,Ogheneyoma Akoni,Jevon Torres,Jaspreet Ranjit,Matthew Finlayson,Swabha Swayamdipta*

Main category: cs.CL
