Motivation: 现有GRPO在多奖励场景下的标量化方法（奖励组合和优势组合）存在显著缺陷：奖励组合产生过大平方幅度的优势导致训练不稳定，优势组合依赖静态超参数且忽视跨目标相关性。

Method: 提出DVAO方法，基于每个目标在rollout组内的经验奖励方差动态调整组合权重，增强具有更强学习信号的目标权重同时抑制噪声目标，并引入自适应跨目标正则化机制。

Result: 在Qwen3和Qwen2.5模型上、数学推理和工具使用基准测试中，DVAO显著优于基线方法，实现了更优越的多目标帕累托前沿和鲁棒的训练稳定性。

Conclusion: DVAO通过动态方差自适应机制有效解决了多奖励GRPO的稳定性与效率问题，为多目标强化学习对齐提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning has become a standard paradigm for aligning Large Language Models with human intent and task requirements. While Group Relative Policy Optimization offers an efficient, value-model-free alternative to Proximal Policy Optimization, adapting it to real-world multi-reward settings remains challenging. Standard scalarization practices, such as Reward Combination and Advantage Combination, suffer from significant drawbacks: Reward Combination frequently generates advantages with excessively large squared magnitudes that lead to training instability, while Advantage Combination relies on static hyperparameters and ignores cross-objective correlations. To address these limitations, we propose Dynamic Variance-adaptive Advantage Optimization (DVAO), which dynamically adjusts combination weights based on the empirical reward variance of each objective within a rollout group, effectively up-weighting objectives with a stronger learning signal while suppressing noisy ones. We mathematically prove that DVAO maintains bounded advantage magnitudes for stable training and introduces a self-adaptive cross-objective regularization mechanism. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks using Qwen3 and Qwen2.5 models demonstrate that DVAO significantly outperforms baseline methods, achieving a superior multi-objective Pareto frontier and robust training stability.

</details>


### [217] [Beyond Literal Translation: Evaluating Cultural Effectiveness in Social Media UGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25626)
*Linjuan Wu,Ruiqi Zhang,Xinze Lyu,Ye Guo,Daoxin Zhang,Zhe Xu,Yao Hu,Yixin Cao,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CULTURE-MT基准，专用于评估社交媒体用户生成内容（UGC）的跨文化翻译质量，包含1,002条多领域UGC笔记，并引入“文化有效性”作为新评价标准，发现传统指标无法衡量文化效果，且基础LLM的文化翻译能力与模型规模正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有翻译基准和指标未能评估UGC翻译是否传达原文意图和文化共鸣，需要专门针对社交媒体UGC的文化传递与情感共振的评测体系。

Method: 构建包含1,002条UGC笔记（覆盖14个领域、4种类型）的CULTURE-MT基准；构建UGC训练数据微调Qwen3-8B/32B作为基线；提出“文化有效性”评价标准，涵盖表达准确性和文化适应性；训练JUDGER模型进行自动评估。

Result: 测试15个模型后发现：传统翻译指标无法捕捉文化有效性；基础LLM的文化有效性表现与模型规模正相关。

Conclusion: CULTURE-MT为UGC翻译模型提供了全面的评估系统，将开放评估平台和在线排行榜以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Social media platforms enable large-scale cross-lingual communication, but translating user-generated content (UGC) remains challenging due to its informal style, cultural references, and interaction-based expressions. While recent LLMs have improved translation quality, existing benchmarks and metrics often fail to capture whether translations convey intended meaning and cultural resonance in real-world settings. In this work, we introduce CULTURE-MT, a benchmark for social media translation that focuses on both CULtural Transmission and UGC-specific emotion REsonance. CULTURE-MT consists of 1,002 UGC notes across 14 domains, categorized into four types based on culture-loaded symbols and linguistic style features. We also construct UGC-oriented training data to fine-tune Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B as baselines. We propose cultural effectiveness as a new evaluation criterion, focusing on expression accuracy and cultural adaptability. Testing 15 models, including the baselines, we find that traditional metrics fail to capture cultural effectiveness. We also observe that cultural effectiveness on base LLMs correlates with model size. Our work provides a comprehensive evaluation system for UGC translation models and will offer an open evaluation platform to advance research in this area. We release the CULTURE-MT benchmark and provide an online leaderboard where submitted translation results can be evaluated by our trained JUDGER.

</details>


### [218] [When In-Distribution Gains Fail: Evaluating Weak-to-Strong Reward Models under Preference Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25629)
*Khoi Le,Tri Cao,Phong Nguyen,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Anh Tuan Luu,Miao Chunyan,See-Kiong Ng,Thong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱到强（W2S）偏好学习在零样本分布迁移下的表现，发现强模型在弱监督下会出现表征失败模式（过度拟合源域特征），并提出表征锚定（Anchor）正则化方法，有效提升了跨分布迁移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有W2S泛化评估通常在匹配的训练-测试分布下进行，忽略了实际应用中存在的分布偏移问题，导致强模型在弱监督下看似成功实则脆弱。

Method: 提出表征锚定（Representation Anchoring, Anchor）正则化方法，在弱监督微调过程中约束强模型表征空间相对于预训练模型的过度漂移，同时允许与任务相关的适应性调整。

Result: 在多个偏好领域、数据集和模型家族上，Anchor一致性地提升了强模型在分布外迁移的性能，同时保持了具有竞争力的分布内性能。

Conclusion: 当前W2S奖励建模存在隐藏的脆弱性，而所提出的评估协议、迁移感知指标和Anchor方法提供了实现更鲁棒偏好迁移的实用路径。

Abstract: Weak-to-strong (W2S) generalization is a promising framework for scalable oversight, yet existing evaluations often test students under matched train--test distributions. Therefore, we study W2S preference learning under zero-shot distribution shift and find that strong students trained on weak preference labels can appear successful in-distribution while failing to transfer across preference datasets. We provide evidence for a representational failure mode in which weak-supervised fine-tuning can pull the strong model toward source-domain features instead of maintaining broadly transferable preference representations. To mitigate this, we propose Representation Anchoring (Anchor), a simple yet effective regularizer that constrains excessive drift from the pretrained strong model's representation space during fine-tuning, while still allowing task-relevant adaptation. Across preference domains, datasets, and model families, Anchor consistently improves out-of-distribution transfer while maintaining competitive in-distribution performance. Together, our evaluation protocol, transfer-aware metrics, and method expose hidden brittleness in current W2S reward modeling and provide a practical path toward more robust preference transfer.

</details>


### [219] [Reinforcement Learning from Denoising Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25638)
*Qi He,Huan Chen,Ya Guo,Huijia Zhu,Yi R. Fung,Baojian Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RLDF（基于去噪反馈的强化学习），一种针对扩散语言模型（dLLMs）的策略损失估计新范式，通过在中间噪声状态上优化模型向裁剪后的干净状态逼近，并结合加权时间步采样，在多个推理基准上取得一致改进，并开源了训练框架Drift。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLMs）的策略损失估计是强化学习中的一个长期存在的根本性挑战，现有方法在计算效率与估计效果之间存在难以平衡的权衡。

Method: 提出RLDF框架，从 rollout 和训练过程中获取反馈；优化模型从中间噪声状态 $x_t$ 向裁剪后的干净状态 $\hat{x}_0$ 逼近；采用加权时间步采样策略 $t$ 来平衡计算效率与估计效果。

Result: 在两种代表性dLLM架构（LLaDA和Dream）的多个推理基准上，RLDF在性能和泛化性方面均取得了一致且显著的提升。

Conclusion: RLDF为扩散语言模型中的可扩展强化学习奠定了原则性基础，并开源了Drift训练框架以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Policy loss estimation remains a fundamental and long-standing challenge in reinforcement learning (RL) for diffusion language models (dLLMs). We introduce Reinforcement Learning from Denoising Feedback (RLDF), a novel training paradigm that leverages feedback obtained from rollout and training processes to facilitate accurate and efficient policy loss estimation. To balance the trade-off between computational efficiency and estimation effectiveness, RLDF optimizes the model toward the clipped clean state $\hat{x}_0$ from intermediate noisy states $x_t$, combined with weighted timestep sampling over $t$. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RLDF achieves consistent and substantial improvements in both performance and generalizability across two representative dLLM architectures, LLaDA and Dream, on multiple reasoning benchmarks. Our work lays a principled foundation for scalable reinforcement learning in diffusion language models. We build Drift, a training framework for dLLMs, available at https://github.com/ant-research/Drift.

</details>


### [220] [A Two-Phase Stability Study of LLM Judges and Bar Council Examiners on Thai Bar-Exam Free-Form Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25652)
*Pawitsapak Akarajaradwong,Wuttikrai Lertprasertphakorn,Chompakorn Chaksangchaichot,Sarana Nutanong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过泰国律师资格考试的真实跨评分实验，发现人类评分员在标准明确的题目上高度一致，但在评分规则未覆盖的争议点上出现两个解读阵营（B/C多数派 vs A少数派）。26个LLM裁判集体偏向多数派解读，没有一个LLM复现少数派人类评分，表明LLM与人类参考标准的一致性存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP法律文书评估研究将人类专家间评分稳定性视为单一天花板数字，并将LLM裁判与这个天花板的吻合度当作裁判稳定性的证据。本文旨在检验这两个隐含假设是否成立。

Method: 采用相同输入协议：3名律师委员会培训的评分员（A、B、C）和26个LLM裁判对同一组15份交叉评分的答案进行打分，所有评分员使用完全相同的四个输入（问题、官方评分规则、参考答案、考生答案）。其中3个LLM（Claude 4.6 Opus, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.4 Pro）构成锚定子面板，进行确定性探测、输入消融和bootstrap置信区间分析。

Result: 在所有29个评分员都适用的15个评分格中，10个格子上所有评分员高度一致。但在评分规则未规定如何给省略关键法条引用的正确答案打分的5个格子上，人类评分员分裂为两个阵营（B/C多数派给出6-8分，A少数派给出1-2分）。LLM裁判中，22个集中在B/C阵营附近，3个落在规则未覆盖的中间区域，仅1个（GPT-5.4 Nano）接近但未一致落在A阵营内。零个LLM复现少数派人类解读。锚定子面板在15个格子上的alpha值为0.77，而人类面板仅为0.36。

Conclusion: LLM面板的高alpha值反映的是系统性收敛于多数派解读，而非对两种人类解读的均衡复现。如果通过最大化与人类参考面板的一致性来选择LLM裁判，构建出的基准将天然继承这种不对称偏差。

Abstract: Free-form legal essay evaluation in NLP treats expert inter-rater stability as a single ceiling number, and treats LLM-judge agreement with that ceiling as evidence of judge stability. We test both assumptions on the Thai bar examination through an identical-inputs protocol: three Bar Council-trained examiners (A, B, C) and a 26-LLM judge panel score the same 15 cross-graded answers from the same four inputs (question, official Bar Council grading regulation, gold answer, candidate answer). The headline finding is asymmetric. On 10 of 15 cells where the rubric prescribes both axes, all 29 raters converge in a tight band: panel agreement is universal. On the remaining 5 cells where the rubric does not prescribe how to grade a correct final answer that omits a decisive statutory citation, the human panel splits between two coherent readings (B/C majority at the upper rubric band, score $6$--$8$; A minority at the lower band, score $1$--$2$). The LLM judge population does not split symmetrically: 22 of 26 LLMs score in or near B/C's contested band, 3 sit in the regulation-silent middle gap, and only 1 (GPT-5.4 Nano) approaches A's band without consistently scoring within it. \emph{Zero LLMs in our 26-judge panel reproduce the minority human reading on the contested cells.} The B/C-direction cluster spans every model size, vendor, and price tier we tested. An instrumented three-LLM anchor sub-panel (Claude 4.6 Opus, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.4 Pro) carries determinism probes, input ablations, and bootstrap CIs, and reaches anchor panel $α= 0.77$ on the 15 cells against human-panel $α= 0.36$. The high LLM-panel $α$ reflects systematic convergence on the majority reading rather than balanced reproduction of both readings; a benchmark that selects its LLM judge by maximising agreement with a human reference panel will inherit this asymmetry by construction.

</details>


### [221] [AutoSG: LLM-Driven Solver Generation Solely from Task Prompts for Expensive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25658)
*Haoran Gu,Handing Wang,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AutoSG框架，通过检索增强生成、单步自优化和无实例的Elo评估机制，从自然语言提示自动生成求解器，在昂贵优化任务上超越人工设计及LLM生成的现有求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动求解器生成在昂贵优化任务中存在领域知识幻觉、优化过程中破坏已建立局部最优结构、以及训练实例执行导致的评估成本高昂且泛化受限三个关键问题。

Method: AutoSG包含三个核心创新：1）检索增强的求解器生成模块，将代码严格基于经过验证的文献；2）单步自优化算子，在保留关键结构组件的同时引入任务特定改进；3）基于Elo排名的无实例LLM-as-a-Judge评估机制，快速建立全局排名。

Result: 在多种昂贵优化任务上的广泛评估表明，AutoSG显著优于人工设计的最先进框架和现有LLM生成的求解器。

Conclusion: AutoSG实现了从自然语言提示到可执行定制求解器的全自动工作流，有效解决了昂贵优化任务中自动求解器生成面临的三个关键问题。

Abstract: Expensive optimization tasks are ubiquitous in real-world applications, demanding highly specialized solvers. While LLM-driven automated solver generation shows promise, current paradigms face three critical issues when tackling expensive optimization: factual hallucinations due to deficient domain knowledge, the frequent dismantling of previously established locally optimal structures during refinement, and the prohibitive evaluation costs alongside restricted generalization caused by executing on training instances. To address these issues, we introduce AutoSG, a fully automated workflow directly translating natural language prompts into executable customized solvers. AutoSG features three core innovations: a retrieval-augmented solver generation module strictly grounding code in verified literature; a one-step self-refinement operator introducing task-specific improvements while preserving critical structural components; and an instance-free Elo-based LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation mechanism rapidly establishing global rankings. Extensive evaluations across diverse expensive optimization tasks confirm AutoSG significantly outperforms human-designed state-of-the-art frameworks and existing LLM-generated solvers.

</details>


### [222] [Llamion Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25676)
*Kisu Yang,Yoonna Jang,Hyeonseok Moon,Hwanseok Jang,Taewoo Lee,Hyungjin Lee,Jeseung Lee,Juhyoung Park,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Llamion系列模型，通过KEPT方法将Orion-14B高效转换为Llama架构，仅用少量数据和计算资源即可恢复源模型性能，并在KoMMLU等基准上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非标准架构的语言模型转换为标准化、生态兼容的Llama架构，以便利用Llama生态工具链并降低部署门槛，同时高效保留源模型知识。

Method: 提出KEPT（知识保留转换）方法，包含三种技术：(i) 未变模块的普通参数映射(NPM)；(ii) 优化的LayerNorm到RMSNorm参数映射(OPM)，是在近零均值激活假设下证明最优的免训练初始化方法；(iii) 等规模冻结教师跨架构知识蒸馏(XKD)，对齐转换模型与源模型的输出分布。

Result: Llamion在H6、MT-Bench、KoMMLU上恢复Orion性能，仅需约1.23亿token和单张A100四天训练；Llamion-Base在KoMMLU上达66.87%，提交时超越Open Ko LLM排行榜第二名超7个绝对百分点；Python编程和20万token长上下文等未出现在转移语料中的能力完整保留。

Conclusion: KEPT方法能高效地将非标准架构模型转换为标准化架构，仅需极少资源和数据即可恢复并保持源模型能力，同时兼容主流推理框架。

Abstract: We release Llamion, a family of 14B-parameter open-weight language models obtained by transforming Orion-14B into the standardized Llama-family architecture. The transformation is performed by Efficient Knowledge Preservation for Transformation (KEPT), a recipe that combines (i) Normal Parameter Mapping (NPM) for unchanged modules, (ii) Optimized Parameter Mapping (OPM), a training-free LayerNorm-to-RMSNorm initialization we prove optimal under the near-zero-mean activation regime induced by weight decay, and (iii) Cross-architecture Knowledge Distillation (XKD), an equal-size frozen-teacher distillation that aligns the converted model's outputs with the source model's on any reasonable input distribution. Llamion recovers Orion's behaviour on H6, MT-Bench, and KoMMLU with only ~123M tokens on a single A100 in four days; Llamion-Base reaches 66.87% on KoMMLU, exceeding the next-best entry of the Open Ko LLM Leaderboard by >7.0 absolute points at submission time. Capabilities entirely absent from the transfer corpus (Python programming and 200K-token context handling) survive the architectural transition intact. We release three checkpoints (Base, Chat, LongChat) that load with trust_remote_code=False in the Hugging Face Transformers library.

</details>


### [223] [Simulating Human Memory with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25680)
*Qihan Wang,Nicholas Tomlin,Michael Hu,Brian Dillon,Tal Linzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现当前语言模型作为用户模拟器时，其记忆能力远超人类。通过心理学经典记忆实验对比，即使提示模型模仿人类行为，其记忆表现仍然优于人类。作者提出更好的提示策略和"压缩器"（compactor）方法，使模型以更接近人类的方式遗忘。初步实验表明，具有人类记忆约束的语言模型在下游教育任务中能作为更有效的用户模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型被广泛用作用户模拟器，但其记忆能力与真实人类存在显著差距，这种"超人类记忆"可能导致模拟不真实，影响下游任务的有效性。

Method: 在心理学经典记忆实验范式上同时测试人类和语言模型；使用提示策略和"压缩器"（compactor）方法使模型以类人方式遗忘；在下游教育任务中验证类人记忆约束对模拟效果的影响。

Result: 直接使用的语言模型在记忆任务上优于人类；改进提示和压缩器可使模型遗忘模式更接近人类；类人记忆约束能提升语言模型作为用户模拟器在教育任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 语言模型的记忆能力远超人类，但通过提示工程和压缩机制可模拟类人遗忘；类人记忆约束对构建更真实的用户模拟器具有重要价值；论文提供了人类参考数据和基准以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly being deployed as user simulators, but their memory is far more reliable than that of real users. To measure this gap, we run a series of classic memory experiments from psychology on both humans and language models. Across tasks, we find that out-of-the-box language models exhibit better memory than humans, even when prompted to imitate human behavior. We then show that better prompting strategies and the use of a compactor can cause language models to forget content in a more human-like way. Using these methods, we show preliminary evidence that language models with human-like memory constraints can function as more effective user simulators in a downstream education task. Finally, we release human reference data and benchmarks to support future work on simulating human memory with language models.

</details>


### [224] [Testing the Deliteralization Hypothesis in Human and Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25686)
*Malik Marmonier,Rachel Bawden,Benoît Sagot*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究验证了“去字面化假设”（翻译修订过程中译文逐渐变得不那么字面化）是否也适用于大语言模型（LLM）。通过对比人工翻译、机器翻译和六种LLM在54个语言对上的表现，发现：人工翻译仍比所有机器翻译系统更不字面化，但最新LLM正在缩小差距；LLM在迭代自修订中会单调地去字面化；作为后编辑者时，LLM会反转人类后编辑的修订触发模式，容忍字面化译文而针对地道表达进行修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验从专用NMT系统到通用LLM的范式转变是否也影响了翻译的去字面化假设——即翻译在起草和修订过程中逐渐变得不那么字面化的经典论断。

Method: 使用WMT24++数据集，比较人工翻译、后编辑与两个NMT系统、六个LLM在54个语言对上的字面化程度，涵盖三种任务：直接翻译、迭代自修订、人工草稿后编辑。字面化程度通过基于六种启发式方法验证过的合成字面化指数（Synthetic Literality Index）测量。

Result: (i)人工翻译仍显著比所有测试机器翻译系统更不字面化，但最新LLM正在缩小差距；(ii)LLM在迭代自修订中单调地实现去字面化，首次证明该假设天然适用于LLM生成；(iii)作为后编辑者时，LLM反转人类后编辑的修订触发模式，容忍字面化草稿而针对地道人类表达进行修改。

Conclusion: 去字面化假设不仅适用于人类翻译过程，也适用于LLM的迭代生成过程，但LLM作为后编辑者表现出与人类不同的修订偏好。

Abstract: The recent shift from dedicated NMT systems to general-purpose LLMs has reshaped machine translation, with LLMs reported to produce more fluent, less literal output than their predecessors. We test whether this shift extends to the deliteralization hypothesis, the long-standing claim from translation studies that translations become progressively less literal as they are drafted and revised. Using the WMT24++ dataset, we compare the literality of human translations and post-editions to that of two NMT systems and six LLMs across 54 language pairs and three tasks: direct translation, iterative self-revision, and post-editing of human drafts. Literality is measured via a validated Synthetic Literality Index built from six heuristics. We find that (i) human translations remain significantly less literal than those of all tested MT systems, though recent LLMs narrow the gap; (ii) when prompted to iteratively revise their own output, LLMs deliteralize monotonically, providing the first evidence that the hypothesis applies natively to LLM generation; and (iii) as post-editors, LLMs invert the revision triggers of human post-editors, tolerating literal drafts and targeting idiomatic human formulations for revision.

</details>


### [225] [From Facts to Insights: A Persona-Driven Dual Memory Framework and Dataset for Role-Playing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25693)
*Rongsheng Zhang,Ruofan Hu,Weijie Chen,Jiji Tang,Junnan Ren,Wanying Wu,Xunuoyan Chen,Tangjie Lv,Tao Jin,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对长程角色扮演对话中记忆与人格一致性缺失的问题，提出RoleMemo数据集（含四项推理任务）和DualMem双流记忆框架（事实认知+人格条件洞察），通过SFT和RL训练，4B模型在人格保真度上超越基于DeepSeek-V3.2的零样本框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色扮演智能体在长期对话中依赖人物无关的摘要记忆，仅记录事实而缺乏人格特定视角的解释，导致生成响应千篇一律，损害人格保真度。需要弥合事实记忆与人格化解读之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 构建RoleMemo数据集，包含四项需要结合人格视角解释事实片段的推理任务；2) 提出DualMem框架，将记忆解耦为事实认知流和人格条件洞察流；3) 采用监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）进行训练。

Result: RoleMemo评估暴露了人物无关记忆框架的局限性。DualMem框架（4B参数模型）在持续人格保真度上超越了使用DeepSeek-V3.2的零样本人物无关框架。

Conclusion: 双流记忆解耦（事实+人格化洞察）结合SFT和RL训练能有效提升长程角色扮演中的人格一致性，小模型通过该方法可超越大规模零样本模型。

Abstract: While role-playing agents excel in short-term interactions, long-term conversations overwhelm context windows, motivating external memory frameworks. Current systems typically rely on persona-agnostic summarization, which records facts without persona-specific interpretation, yielding generic responses that compromise persona fidelity. To bridge this gap, we introduce RoleMemo, a dataset featuring four reasoning tasks where the factual fragments must be interpreted through the persona to reach the correct answer. Evaluation on RoleMemo exposes critical limitations of persona-agnostic frameworks. We thus propose DualMem, which decouples memory into two streams: factual cognition and persona-conditioned insight. Trained through Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL), our framework with a 4B-parameter model outperforms zero-shot persona-agnostic frameworks powered by DeepSeek-V3.2 for sustained persona fidelity. Our resources are available at https://github.com/role2026/rolememo.

</details>


### [226] [PowLU: An Activation Function for Stable Pre-Training of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25704)
*Peijie Jiang,Yuqi Feng,Cunyin Peng,Qian Zhao,Jia Liu,KunLong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Power Linear Unit (PowLU)激活函数，通过有理幂函数实现自适应非线性，解决SwiGLU在大规模低精度LLM训练中因近似二次放大导致的数值不稳定问题，在7.9B和124B参数规模的Ling架构上取得与SwiGLU及SwiGLU-Clip相当甚至更优的结果，并有效提升了大规模训练的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SwiGLU激活函数在LLM中广泛使用，但其对大幅值正输入的近似二次放大(x²)特性会导致输出范围扩大、异常值加剧，在模型规模增大或低精度训练时引发数值不稳定问题，亟需一种既能保持强非线性表达能力又能保证训练稳定性的替代激活函数。

Method: 提出Power Linear Unit (PowLU)，采用有理幂函数替代SwiGLU中的二次近似，实现自适应非线性：在峰值区域通过幂函数调控输出范围以避免数值爆炸，同时保留足够的非线性表达能力。论文还提供了PowLU关键性质的理论证明，并通过缩放定律实验和Ling架构（7.9B和124B参数）的大规模训练验证其有效性。

Result: 缩放定律实验表明PowLU在不同模型规模下性能一致；在Ling架构的7.9B和124B参数规模训练中，PowLU取得了与SwiGLU和SwiGLU-Clip竞争力相当的结果；同时PowLU有效提升了大规模LLM训练的可扩展性。

Conclusion: PowLU作为SwiGLU的稳定替代方案，通过有理幂函数实现自适应非线性，在保持表达能力的同時解决了大规模低精度训练中的数值不稳定问题，提升了训练可扩展性，适用于大规模LLM预训练场景。

Abstract: In contemporary large language models (LLMs), the swish-gated linear unit (SwiGLU) activation function is widely adopted to regulate the information flow and introduce non-linearity. For large positive inputs, SwiGLU approximates the quadratic function $x^2$, providing strong nonlinearity and expressive capacity. However, this property also causes numerical instability as the input or model scale increases, particularly in low-precision LLM training. The main reason is its approximate quadratic amplification, which enlarges the output range and exacerbates outliers. To address this issue, we propose a stable activation function, Power Linear Unit (PowLU), for large-scale LLM pre-training. Specifically, PowLU employs a rational power function to achieve adaptive nonlinearity, thereby improving representation ability and enabling stable training in spike regions. Moreover, we provide theoretical justification for several key properties of PowLU. Scaling law experiments confirm that the performance is consistent across model sizes, and further experimental results with the Ling architecture (7.9B and 124B total parameters) demonstrate that PowLU achieves competitive results against SwiGLU and SwiGLU-Clip in large-scale training of LLMs. In addition, the experimental results also show that PowLU effectively improves the scalability of the large-scale training of LLMs.

</details>


### [227] [Trait-Aware Policy Optimization for Autoregressive Multi-Trait Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25731)
*Zhengyang Wang,Sanwoo Lee,Jiaxin Wang,Chenxi Miao,Weikang Li,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPO框架，通过沿样本和特质维度分解奖励，结合全局评分一致性、特质级准确性、格式有效性和特质间依赖保持，对自回归多特质作文评分模型进行后训练，在多骨干模型上持续优于监督微调和标量奖励优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多特质作文评分旨在提供跨多个维度的细粒度写作质量评估，但如何有效后训练自回归评分模型尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出Trait-Aware Policy Optimization (TAPO)框架，将奖励沿样本和特质维度分解，结合全局评分一致性、特质级准确性、格式有效性和特质间依赖保持；并通过增强提示的监督微调使模型内化特质语义。

Result: 在多个骨干模型上，TAPO持续优于监督微调和标量奖励优化基线，展示了特质感知后训练的有效性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: 特质感知的后训练框架能够有效提升自回归模型在多特质作文评分任务上的性能。

Abstract: Multi-trait essay scoring aims to provide fine-grained evaluation of writing quality across multiple dimensions. However, how to effectively post-train autoregressive scoring models remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose Trait-Aware Policy Optimization (TAPO), a post-training framework tailored to autoregressive multi-trait scoring. Our method decomposes rewards along both the sample and trait dimensions, combining global scoring consistency, trait-level accuracy, format validity, and inter-trait dependency preservation. In addition, we enhance supervised fine-tuning with enhanced prompts, allowing the model to internalize trait semantics before preference optimization. Experiments across multiple backbone models show that our method consistently improves multi-trait scoring performance over supervised fine-tuning and scalar-reward optimization baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness and transferability of trait-aware post-training for essay scoring.

</details>


### [228] [Selective Latent Thinking: Adaptive Compression of LLM Reasoning Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25745)
*Hui Xie,Jie Liu,Ziyue Qiao,Joaquin Vanschore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Selective Latent Thinking (SLT)框架，通过选择性压缩冗余推理片段为潜在表示、保留关键步骤为显式思维链，在保持高推理精度的同时显著降低推理成本。在数学推理基准上，相比潜在推理基线准确率提升22.7%，相比显式CoT推理链长度减少58.4%且准确率仅下降2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式思维链(CoT)推理虽能提升LLM推理能力，但生成长的自回归序列导致高推理成本；现有潜在推理方法将推理过程均匀压缩，导致精度关键步骤被过度压缩，推理准确率下降。

Method: SLT框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 轻量级解码器预测即将到来的短推理跨度；(2) 基于置信度的门控机制判断可压缩的最长跨度，冗余部分压缩为潜在表示，关键部分保留显式CoT；(3) 三阶段训练策略：跨度级潜在压缩、可靠性感知的未来推理预测、轨迹级强化学习优化正确性与成本之间的权衡。

Result: 在四个数学推理基准上，SLT相比潜在推理基线准确率提升22.7%；相比显式CoT，推理链长度减少58.4%，准确率仅下降2.8%。

Conclusion: 选择性压缩冗余推理跨度、保留关键精度步骤的混合推理策略，能够有效平衡LLM推理的性能与效率。

Abstract: Explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning substantially improves the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs), but incurs high inference cost due to lengthy autoregressive traces. Existing latent reasoning methods offer a promising alternative, yet they often treat reasoning as uniformly compressible, causing precision-critical intermediate steps to be overly compressed and thereby degrading reasoning accuracy. In this work, we propose Selective Latent Thinking (SLT), a framework that selectively compresses redundant reasoning spans into latent representations while preserving precision-critical spans as explicit CoT within the same reasoning trajectory. Specifically, SLT first uses a lightweight decoder to anticipate a short upcoming reasoning span, and then applies confidence-based gating to determine the longest span that can be reliably compressed. The accepted span is encoded into a compact latent representation to improve reasoning efficiency, while uncertain or precision-critical reasoning remains in explicit CoT form to preserve accuracy. To learn this selective compression policy, SLT adopts a three-stage training strategy that combines span-level latent compression, reliability-aware future reasoning prediction, and trajectory-level reinforcement learning to optimize the trade-off between answer correctness and reasoning cost. Extensive experiments across four mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SLT achieves 22.7\% higher accuracy than latent reasoning baselines at comparable compression ratios, while reducing reasoning chain length by 58.4\% with only 2.8\% accuracy degradation compared to explicit CoT,Our code can be found in https://github.com/hunshi34/SLT.

</details>


### [229] [StreamProfileBench: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained User Profile Inference in Real-World Streaming Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25758)
*Sizhe Wang,Feiyu Duan,Juelin Wang,Liwen Zhang,Feiyu Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StreamProfileBench提出流式用户画像基准，包含12万+帖子、7000+用户的多平台真实数据，揭示现有LLM在连续用户画像更新中存在保守偏差，难以识别兴趣衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用户画像评估仅基于静态数据快照，忽略了用户生成内容持续到达、细粒度画像快速演变的现实，缺乏对流式用户画像的评估基准。

Method: 1) 形式化流式用户画像为连续状态维护任务；2) 构建包含12万+帖子、7000+真实用户、跨5个平台的高真实性数据集；3) 利用用户兴趣的时间相关性，提出无标注评估框架。

Result: 在14个主流LLM上的实验表明：连续画像更新仍是开放挑战；模型存在系统性保守偏差——过度保留过去兴趣，未能识别兴趣衰减。

Conclusion: 消融实验验证了流式范式在实际应用中的必要性和有效性，现有模型在动态用户建模方面仍有显著不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have reshaped user profiling, yet current evaluations mainly focus on static data snapshots. This paradigm overlooks the reality of personalized systems, where User-Generated Content (UGC) arrives continuously and fine-grained profile evolve rapidly. To bridge this gap, we introduce StreamProfileBench, a large-scale benchmark for fine-grained streaming user profiling. We formalize streaming user profiling as a continuous state maintenance task and curate a highly authentic dataset comprising over 120,000 UGC posts from 7,000+ real users across five diverse platforms. By leveraging the temporal correlation of user interests, we further propose a novel, annotation-free evaluation framework. Extensive experiments across 14 leading LLMs reveal that continuous profile updating remains an open challenge. Models exhibit a systemic conservative bias, over-retaining past interests while failing to recognize interest decay. Ablation experiments further validate the practical utility and necessity of the streaming paradigm.

</details>


### [230] [Double Triangle Annotation: A Scalable Human-in-the-Loop Framework for High-Precision Historical Document Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25781)
*Yi Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"双三角标注"框架，通过跨模型共识实现历史文档结构化信息提取的高精度自动标注，在法国医疗目录数据集上达到0.003的词错误率，超过85%的字段被自动接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工标注成本高昂，且基于大语言模型的自动化管线容易产生幻觉，亟需一种高精度、低成本的标注框架来构建历史文档的结构化提取基准。

Method: 两层人机协作框架：第一层使用两个架构独立的多模态大语言模型并行标注，共识结果自动接受，分歧交由人工陪审团裁决；第二层对两个第一层系统进行交叉校验，剩余冲突由领域专家处理。核心假设是模型间错误独立，无需分布先验或任务特定校准。

Result: 在Guides Rosenwald语料（1887-1906年法国医疗目录）上，最终词错误率仅为0.003；在13,595个字段中，超过85%通过模型共识自动接受。

Conclusion: 该框架有效降低了人工标注成本，同时保证了高精度输出，且随着模型能力提升自动化程度会进一步提高；同时发布了首个Rosenwald指南结构化提取基准数据集。

Abstract: Evaluating structured-information extraction from historical documents at scale requires high-precision ground-truth annotations, yet traditional manual labeling is expensive and fully automated pipelines built on large language models are prone to hallucination. We propose Double Triangle Annotation, a two-layer human-in-the-loop framework that leverages cross-model consensus to automate the majority of annotation work while ensuring high-precision outputs. In the first layer, two architecturally independent Multimodal Large Language Models annotate each document in parallel; when they agree, the label is auto-accepted, and disagreements are routed to a human jury. A second layer cross-checks two such systems against each other, escalating residual conflicts to a domain expert. The framework rests on a single assumption -- error independence between models -- requires no distributional priors or task-specific calibration, and becomes more autonomous as model capability improves. On the Guides Rosenwald, a corpus of French medical directories spanning 1887-1906, the framework achieves a final Word Error Rate of 0.003. Applied at scale, model consensus auto-accepts over 85% of 13,595 fields. We release the resulting benchmark -- the first structured-extraction ground truth for the Rosenwald Guides -- to support future work on historical document processing.

</details>


### [231] [Adaptive Graph Refinement and Label Propagation with LLMs for Cost-Effective Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25814)
*Hongtao Wang,Renchi Yang,Haoran Zheng,Xiangyu Ke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Alper，一种将实体解析中的匹配与聚类统一为迭代概率标签传播过程的框架，通过自适应整合图传播的弱信号和LLM的强信号，在查询预算下实现高效准确的脏数据实体解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脏数据实体解析采用阻塞-匹配-聚类级联范式，存在严重缺陷：解耦的工作流产生静态稀疏图，阻塞失败导致缺失边，匹配错误引入噪声边，错误传播且聚类时强制传递性导致次优结果。

Method: 提出Alper统一框架，将匹配与聚类整合为全局演化图上的迭代概率标签传播过程；自适应整合图传播的"弱但廉价"信号与LLM的"强但昂贵"成对查询；将信号选择建模为带查询预算的约束优化问题，最大化累积边际增益，并提出具有可证明理论保证的贪心算法求解。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上的实验表明，Alper在所有数据集上一致优于最先进的级联流水线方法。

Conclusion: 匹配与聚类本质上是协同优化的，统一框架能够有效克服传统级联范式的缺陷，通过自适应信号整合在预算约束下实现更优实体解析效果。

Abstract: Dirty entity resolution (ER), which identifies records referring to the same real-world entity from a single, messy dataset, is a fundamental task in data management and mining. However, the dominant blocking-matching-clustering paradigm for ER suffers from critical flaws. Its cascaded, decoupled workflow essentially produces a static, sparse graph plagued by missing edges (due to blocking failures) and noisy links (due to matching errors), causing error propagation and yielding suboptimal clusters, particularly when rigid transitivity is imposed in the clustering. We contend that matching and clustering are fundamentally synergistic, both optimizing for the construction of an ideal entity graph. Building upon this insight, we propose Alper, a unified framework that integrates these steps into an iterative probabilistic label propagation process over a global, evolving graph. Unlike disjoint blocking, Alper refines the graph structure and labels dynamically by adaptively integrating "weak but cheap" signals from graph propagation with "strong but expensive" LLM-based pairwise queries. For higher cost-effectiveness, we formulate the signal selection as a constrained optimization problem maximizing cumulative marginal gain under a query budget, solved via our greedy algorithm with provable theoretical guarantees. Our extensive experiments over eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that Alper is consistently superior to state-of-the-art cascaded pipelines.

</details>


### [232] [Fine-Tuning Over Architectural Complexity: Broad-Coverage PII Detection on PIIBench with DeBERTa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25816)
*Pritesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多源PII检测系统的微调方法，比较了直接微调、源条件层次模型（SC+H）和课程扩展（SC+H+Curr）三种DeBERTa方法。实验表明，直接微调在82种实体类型上性能最优（F1 0.6455），优于更复杂的架构和课程学习方法，说明多样化训练数据和简单加权交叉熵目标比模型复杂性更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PII检测系统通常在狭窄的源或领域边界内训练，导致在异构文本上部署时覆盖范围有限，缺乏多源、多实体类型的统一检测能力。

Method: 构建了包含10个源数据集、82种实体类型的多源PIIBench，评估三种DeBERTa方法：直接token分类微调、源条件层次模型（SC+H）和三阶段课程扩展（SC+H+Curr），并与8个已发表系统对比。

Result: 直接微调DeBERTa在5,000条测试集上F1 0.6476，完整100,002条测试集上F1 0.6455；SC+H为0.5894；SC+H+Curr仅0.2772；最强的已发表比较系统仅0.1723。直接微调在54/82个细粒度实体类型和全部10个粗粒度组上获胜。

Conclusion: 多样化任务特定训练数据和简单加权交叉熵目标对广覆盖PII检测的贡献大于所测试的架构和课程复杂性。

Abstract: Personally identifiable information (PII) detection systems are frequently trained within narrow source or domain boundaries, limiting coverage when deployed on heterogeneous text. We study model fine-tuning on a corrected multi-source PIIBench preparation spanning 82 retained entity types across ten source datasets. We evaluate three DeBERTa-based approaches: direct token classification fine-tuning, a source-conditioned hierarchical model (SC+H), and a three-phase curriculum extension (SC+H+Curr). Against eight published comparator systems on a reproducible 5,000-record held-out subset (test_5k), direct fine-tuned DeBERTa achieves F1 0.6476, while SC+H and the curriculum variant achieve 0.5899 and 0.2772 respectively; the strongest published comparator reaches only 0.1723. Because validation initially favoured SC+H, we perform a final streamed evaluation on the complete 100,002-record held-out split. Direct fine-tuning remains superior, achieving F1 0.6455 versus 0.5894 for SC+H. Entity-level analysis shows that direct fine tuning wins 54 of 82 fine entity types and all ten coarse groups by support-weighted entity F1, while SC+H retains localised advantages on 28 types. The results indicate that diverse task-specific training data and a simple weighted cross-entropy objective contribute more to broad-coverage PII detection than the tested architectural and curriculum complexity.

</details>


### [233] [Clarify, Abstain or Answer? Strategising in Conversation with Belief-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25831)
*Joris Baan,Wilker Aziz,Barbara Plank,Raquel Fernández*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出信念增强生成（BAG）方法，通过让LLM基于自身采样K个回答的信念状态进行推理，自主决定回答、澄清或回避策略，在模糊多轮问答中提升准确性，且比纯提示基线更忠实于信念状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅将LLM的分布采样用于解码或选择性预测等狭隘任务，且需手动干预，无法直接控制生成。需要一种让模型自主利用自身不确定性信息进行策略决策的方法。

Method: BAG方法通过提示将LLM自身采样K个回答的信念状态注入上下文，让模型在这些样本上进行推理，自主决定采用回答、澄清还是回避的对话策略。

Result: 在六种模型上，BAG在模糊多轮问答中显著提升了QA准确率；相比纯提示基线，策略决策更忠实于信念状态；但在区分何时澄清与何时回避上仍存在困难。

Conclusion: BAG有效利用LLM自生成样本作为信念状态来引导对话策略，但澄清与回避的边界仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) define a distribution over text, which can be viewed as a probabilistic representation of uncertainty: sampling K responses yields a belief state - responses a model deems plausible. Existing work exploits this representation for narrow tasks like either decoding or selective prediction, and often requires manual interventions, not controlling generation directly. We propose Belief-Augmented Generation (BAG): grounding LLMs in their own belief state via the prompt and letting them reason over these K samples to decide on a conversational strategy: answer, clarify, or abstain. In a multi-turn ambiguous QA setting, we find that LLMs by default rarely clarify or abstain, ignoring uncertainty about the input or facts. BAG improves QA accuracy across six models and yields strategy decisions more faithful to the belief state than prompt-only baselines. Disentangling when to clarify from when to abstain, however, remains challenging.

</details>


### [234] [On the Limits of Model Merging for Multilinguality in Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25846)
*Seth Aycock,Fedor Vitiugin,Aleksandr Umnov,Christof Monz,Khalil Sima'an*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究测试了将模型合并方法应用于单语预训练模型的效果，发现合并单语模型会导致性能崩溃，原因是表征干扰。研究表明表征相似性是模型合并的前提条件，因此微调中的合并灵活性无法简单扩展到语言特定的预训练中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以将模型合并方法应用于单语预训练模型，以实现多语言一致性表现，而非依赖混合预训练数据或后训练方法。

Method: 进行了控制实验，对比混合预训练、合并预训练和单语预训练三种设置的效果，并分析了模型合并对单语模型性能的影响。

Result: 单语预训练在对应语言上表现良好，但合并任何单语模型组合都会因干扰导致性能崩溃；表征相似性是模型合并的必要条件。

Conclusion: 微调中的模型合并灵活性无法简单推广到语言特定的预训练场景中。

Abstract: Endowing models with consistent multilingual performance can be achieved by mixing pre-training data, or post-training approaches such as language-specific model merging. In this work, we test whether merging can be applied to monolingually pre-trained models. We conduct a controlled study on the efficacy of mixed, merged, and monolingual pre-training setups. We find that while monolingual pre-training results in strong in-language performance, merging any combination of monolingual models leads to performance collapse due to interference. Our analysis suggests representational similarity is a prerequisite for model merging. We therefore conclude that the flexibility of merging in fine-tuning does not extend trivially to language-specific pre-training.

</details>


### [235] [TIAR: Trajectory-Informed Advantage Reweighting for LLM Abstention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25850)
*Muyu Pan,Shu Zhao,Nan Zhang,Philip Shin,Varun Parekh,Vijaykrishnan Narayanan,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory-Informed Advantage Reweighting (TIAR)方法，利用GRPO训练中的多条轨迹动态计算弃权优势，实现大语言模型的弃权学习，在AbstentionBench五个评估类别上达到SOTA弃权F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在不确定时缺乏有效的弃权机制，导致产生幻觉。三元奖励（ternary reward）方法虽能鼓励真实性，但未充分利用训练过程中的轨迹信息来动态调整弃权信号。

Method: 基于GRPO的多条轨迹作为自然弃权信号，利用轨迹一致性作为策略对查询置信度的指标，动态计算弃权优势（advantage）并重新加权奖励，替代静态三元奖励。

Result: 在AbstentionBench的6个评估类别中5个达到SOTA弃权F1分数，在31个基准数据集上17个优于静态三元基线，同时完全保留了基线准确率。

Conclusion: TIAR通过动态优势重加权有效利用轨迹信息，在不牺牲准确率的前提下提升了弃权学习效果，为减少LLM幻觉提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper investigates large language model (LLM) abstention learning, specifically using ternary reward, which incentivize truthfulness in large language models. This paper extends that idea by moving from a ternary reward to a Trajectory-Informed advantage reweighting, dynamically re-weights the abstention reward during Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) training. The objective of this work focuses on abstention learning instead of improving truthfulness, serving as an exploration into hallucination reduction. The novelty of this paper lies in methodological innovation, advantage re-weighting, and benchmark selection. Leveraging GRPO's multiple trajectories as a natural abstention signal, this method uses a reward signal to explore knowledge boundaries and encourage consistency. By demonstrating that trajectories can be used as a confidence indicator of the policy relative to the query, they are then used to dynamically calculate the abstention advantage. AbstentionBench is used as the evaluation benchmark, as this work aims to contribute to the field of abstention learning. All datasets on the benchmark were tested against this method and various baselines. Empirical results demonstrate that TIAR achieves state-of-the-art abstention F1 scores across five of six evaluation categories, outperforming the static ternary baseline on 17 of 31 benchmark datasets while fully preserving baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [236] [Mitigating Provenance-Role Collapse in Long-Term Agents via Typed Memory Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25869)
*Zhengda Jin,Bingbing Wang,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了MemIR，一种类型化记忆中间表示，通过结构化约束解决LLM智能体长期记忆中的来源-角色混淆问题，在需要来源追踪和时间推理的任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体将历史交互存储为非结构化的扁平文本，导致来源-角色混淆（provenance-role collapse），即智能体出现来源监控错误（source-monitoring errors），缺乏区分证据来源的认知能力。

Method: 提出MemIR（Memory Intermediate Representation），将长期记忆写为原子化表示：原始证据、检索线索、真值主张三类分离，仅支持的主张原子可被授权为事实。通过多路径原子投影和来源范围利用（provenance-scoped utilization）将异构检索结果转化为以主张为中心的候选束和标准化事实接口。

Result: 在LoCoMo和BEAM-100K数据集上，MemIR在需来源追踪、时间定位和碎片证据聚合的任务上一致优于现有记忆基线。

Conclusion: 从架构层面将来源监控作为结构约束是解决LLM智能体长期记忆认知脆弱性的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for persistent LLM agents, yet prevailing architectures store historical interactions as unstructured, flat text. This unconstrained storage induces provenance-role collapse, a critical failure mode where agents suffer from source-monitoring errors. To resolve this cognitive vulnerability at the architectural level, we propose MemIR, a typed Memory Intermediate Representation that operationalizes source monitoring as a structural constraint. MemIR writes long-term memory into grounded atoms that separate raw evidence, retrieval cues, and truth-bearing claims, with factual authorization restricted to supported claim atoms. It then applies multi-route atomic projection and provenance-scoped utilization to transform heterogeneous retrieval hits into claim-centered candidate bundles and a normalized fact interface for answer generation. Experiments on LoCoMo and BEAM-100K demonstrate that MemIR consistently outperforms existing memory baselines, especially on tasks requiring source tracking, temporal grounding, and aggregation of fragmented evidence.

</details>


### [237] [Causal Tongue-Tie: LLMs Can Encode Causal Direction, But Their Yes/No Outputs Fail to Express](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25891)
*Ziyi Ding,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在反常识因果推理任务中，其隐藏状态线性探针能高精度（~0.97）恢复证据支持的答案，但口头Yes/No输出却退回常识答案（~0.5），两者存在约0.5的准确率差距，作者称之为"因果舌头打结"——模型内部有信号，但语言接口无法正确表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大语言模型在因果推理中"内心知道但说不出口"的现象，质疑仅凭最终输出准确率评判模型因果推理能力的做法。

Method: 在反常识因果推理数据集（anti-commonsense CLadder）上，对比固定线性探针从模型隐藏状态中解码的结果与模型口头Yes/No回答的准确率。

Result: 线性探针准确率约0.97，口头回答准确率约0.5，两者相差约0.5。错误的Yes/No回答可分解为两种失效模式：内部完全没有因果信号，或内部有信号但语言接口无法输出。

Conclusion: 仅凭输出端基准测试的单一准确率来评判LLM的因果推理能力具有误导性——正确不一定代表模型真正理解，错误也不代表模型完全不能推理。

Abstract: We find a mismatch between what large language models encode about a causal question and what they answer. On anti-commonsense CLadder items, a fixed linear probe recovers the evidence-supported answer from the model's hidden state (accuracy approximately 0.97), while the spoken Yes/No reverts to the commonsense one (accuracy approximately 0.5). We call this approximately +0.5 gap Causal Tongue-Tie: a wrong Yes/No decomposes into two separable failure modes: no internal signal versus a signal the verbal interface cannot say. The implication cuts both ways for output-only causal benchmarks: a benchmark "correct" need not mean the model has understood, and a benchmark "wrong" need not mean it cannot. Sweeping claims about whether LLMs can do causal reasoning, drawn from a single accuracy number, deserve a second look.

</details>


### [238] [Universal Activation Verbalizer: A Unified Framework for Cross-Model Activation Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25903)
*Haiyan Zhao,Zirui He,Guanchu Wang,Ali Payani,Yingcong Li,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出通用激活词化框架UAV，使用共享解码器解释异构捐赠模型的隐藏表示，通过轻量适配器将捐赠者激活转换为解码器嵌入空间中的软标记，支持仅适配器迁移，在分类、事实检索和要点总结任务上媲美强自解释基线，并实现跨模型家族和规模的交叉模型词化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活词化方法局限于自解释（每个模型只能解释自身激活），缺乏能够跨异构模型解释激活的统一框架。

Method: UAV框架包含：(1) 轻量适配器将捐赠者激活映射到冻结解码器的嵌入空间；(2) 解码器端LoRA微调提升任务行为；(3) 适配器仅迁移机制，在固定解码器端LoRA情况下仅训练新适配器即可适配新捐赠者。

Result: 在分类、事实检索和要点总结任务上，UAV与强自解释基线表现相当，同时实现跨模型家族和尺度的交叉模型词化。消融实验表明解码器端调优主要改善任务行为，而适配器提供基于激活的事实和语义信息以支持忠实解释。

Conclusion: UAV有效打破了自解释的限制，实现了统一、可迁移的跨模型激活解释框架，为理解异构模型内部表示提供了新范式。

Abstract: Activation verbalization explains hidden representations in natural language, but existing methods are mostly limited to self-explanation, where each model explains only its own activations. We introduce Universal Activation Verbalizer (UAV), a framework that uses a shared decoder to explain activations from heterogeneous donor models. UAV learns a lightweight adapter that converts donor activations into soft tokens in decoder's embedding space, and further supports adapter-only transfer by reusing a frozen decoder-side LoRA while training only a new adapter for another donor. Across classification, fact retrieval, and gist summarization, UAV remains competitive with strong self-explanation baselines while enabling cross-model verbalization across model families and scales. Ablations show that decoder-side tuning mainly improves task behavior, whereas the adapter provides the activation-grounded factual and semantic information needed for faithful explanations.

</details>


### [239] [Does Continued Pretraining on a Learner Corpus Improve Automated Essay Scoring on English Proficiency Tests? Evidence from EFCAMDAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25924)
*Duy Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探究了在EFCAMDAT学习者语料库上进行领域自适应持续预训练（DAPT）对基于Transformer的自动作文评分（AES）的影响。结果表明，全语料DAPT效果不一，而基于CEFR级别匹配的针对性DAPT能更可靠地提升领域内评分性能，但跨数据集迁移能力并未显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练Transformer模型主要基于通用英语语料，对二语学习者写作的表征不足，因此研究者希望探索在领域相关的学习者语料上进行持续预训练是否能提升自动作文评分的表现。

Method: 在EFCAMDAT学习者语料库上对三种Transformer编码器进行领域自适应持续预训练（DAPT），并在FCE和IELTS两个数据集上进行领域内评分和少样本跨数据集迁移评估。进一步通过基于CEFR级别的消融实验分析不同水平子集的效果。

Result: 全语料DAPT在不同模型、数据集和指标上效果不一；混合效果部分归因于EFCAMDAT与下游数据在熟练度、体裁和交际目的上的不匹配。基于CEFR级别的消融实验表明，使用对齐的子集（如B1-B2数据用于FCE）进行针对性DAPT比全语料DAPT更可靠地提升领域内评分性能，但跨数据集迁移能力未一致提升。

Conclusion: 当持续预训练数据与下游评估设置充分对齐时，在学习者写作语料上进行持续预训练可以提升领域内自动作文评分性能，但并不能自动改善跨不同英语水平测试数据集的迁移能力。

Abstract: Recent automated essay scoring (AES) studies increasingly use pretrained transformer models, but these models are usually pretrained on general-domain English and may under-represent second-language learner writing. This study investigates whether domain-adaptive continued pretraining (DAPT) on the EFCAMDAT learner corpus improves transformer-based AES for English proficiency tests. We apply DAPT to three transformer encoders and evaluate them on FCE and IELTS in both in-domain scoring and few-shot cross-dataset transfer. Full-corpus DAPT produces mixed results across models, datasets, and metrics. Further analyses suggest that these mixed effects are partly explained by mismatches in proficiency, genre, and communicative purpose between EFCAMDAT and the downstream datasets. A proficiency-based ablation shows that targeted DAPT using CEFR-aligned subsets improves downstream scoring more reliably than full-corpus DAPT, especially for FCE with B1--B2 data. However, these gains do not consistently improve cross-dataset transfer. Overall, the findings suggest that continued pretraining on a learner-writing corpus can benefit in-domain AES for English assessment when the pretraining data is sufficiently aligned with the downstream assessment settings. However, it does not automatically improve transferability across different English proficiency test datasets.

</details>


### [240] [Thaka at KSAA-2026 Task 2: Regularized Fine-Tuning for Arabic Speech Diacritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25928)
*Meshal Alamr,Hassan Alqaeri,Abdullah Aldahlawi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了KSAA-2026共享任务Task 2（阿拉伯语语音听写与自动标音）的获胜系统，在仅2,327个训练样本且无外部数据条件下，通过微调CATT-Whisper多模态模型，结合R-Drop一致性正则化、Optuna优化超参数、Focal Loss和蒙特卡洛Dropout推理增强，以23.26%的词错误率获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源场景下（仅2,327个训练样本，禁止使用外部数据）解决阿拉伯语语音到全标音文本的端到端生成任务。

Method: 微调CATT-Whisper（CATT文本编码器 + 冻结Whisper语音编码器）；训练正则化：R-Drop一致性正则化、Optuna优化高权重衰减超参数、Focal Loss；推理时对4个检查点执行200次随机前向传播并平均蒙特卡洛Dropout的softmax概率。

Result: 在包含词尾标音（含无标音位置）的主排行榜指标上达到23.26%词错误率（WER），位列所有参赛者第一名。

Conclusion: 该系统的正则化和推理增强策略在极小规模训练数据上显著提升了阿拉伯语语音听写与自动标音的性能，验证了方法有效性。

Abstract: We describe the winning system for Task 2 of the KSAA-2026 Shared Task on Arabic Speech Dictation with Automatic Diacritization. The task requires producing fully diacritized Arabic text from speech audio and undiacritized transcripts, with only 2,327 training samples available and no external data permitted. Our system fine-tunes CATT-Whisper, a character-level multimodal model combining a pretrained CATT text encoder with a frozen Whisper speech encoder. The key to our approach is training regularization: R-Drop consistency regularization, Optuna-optimized hyperparameters with high weight decay, and Focal Loss. At inference, we average 200 stochastic forward passes across four model checkpoints using Monte Carlo Dropout at the softmax probability level. The system achieves 23.26% WER on the primary leaderboard metric (with case endings, including no-diacritic positions), placing 1st among all participants.

</details>


### [241] [QUIET: A Multi-Blank Cascaded Story Cloze Benchmark for LLM Creative Generation Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25955)
*Bo Zou,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出QUIET基准，通过多空白级联故事完形填空和基于信息论的自动化评分协议，实现对LLM创造性生成能力的客观诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM创造性评估方法存在局限性：多选识别范式无法直接衡量生成能力；基于评分标准和LLM-as-Judge的方法依赖主观判断，缺乏客观自动化的评分机制。

Method: 提出QUIET基准：在完整结构故事中设置N个空白（10-20个），每个空白有显式内容约束，空白间存在级联依赖关系；模型以开放生成方式填充所有空白；采用信息论自动化评分协议，基于"校准惊喜"框架计算得分 = 满足度 * (1 + λ * 惊喜度)，其中满足度为客观逻辑推理判断，惊喜度衡量约束满足下的意外程度。

Result: 不满足约束的创造性答案得零分；满足约束但平庸的答案得分低；满足约束且令人惊喜的答案得分高。

Conclusion: QUIET提供了无需人工评分的、客观的LLM创造性生成能力诊断方法，填补了现有评估范式在创造性直接测量上的空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face a dual challenge in creative capability evaluation: existing benchmarks (e.g., Story Cloze Test, HellaSwag) measure models' discriminative ability over narrative continuation using multiple-choice recognition paradigms, rather than directly measuring creative generation capability; rubric-based scoring and LLM-as-Judge methods rely on subjective dimension assessment or natural language model outputs, and cannot provide objective, automated scoring mechanisms.
  This paper proposes QUIET (Quality Understanding via Interlocked Evaluation Testing), a diagnostic benchmark for LLM creative capability based on multi-blank cascaded story cloze. QUIET sets N blanks (10-20) in a story with complete structure, with each blank accompanied by an explicit content constraint, and cascade dependency relationships between blanks -- the content filled into earlier blanks constrains the feasible solution space for later blanks. The evaluated model (or human participants) fills all blanks in open-ended generation mode; the results are scored by an information-theoretic automated scoring protocol without human grading.
  The scoring protocol directly operationalizes the "calibrated surprise" theoretical framework (Zou & Xu, 2026a). For each blank k, a composite score is computed: score = satisfy * (1 + lambda * surprise), where lambda = 1.0. Here, "satisfy" measures how well the blank filling satisfies the content constraint (objective logical reasoning judgment, not subjective aesthetic scoring), and "surprise" measures the degree of surprise given that the constraint is satisfied. Creative answers that do not satisfy the constraint score zero; answers that satisfy the constraint but are mediocre score low; answers that satisfy the constraint and are surprising score high.

</details>


### [242] [PolyGnosis 2.0: Enhancing LLM Reasoning via Agentic Harness Engineering for Polymarket and OSINT Insight Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25958)
*Daren Wang,Hong Xu,Jiawen Xian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PolyGnosis 2.0 是一种多智能体架构，通过融合 Polymarket 异常信号与 GDELT 全球开源情报流来提取预测性智能。论文量化了"引导工程"技术（反思循环、工具调用、分治分区、思维链）的效果，发现结构分区对多维对齐是必需的，而无约束的终端反思会导致逻辑漂移。研究还识别出所有智能体配置中普遍存在的"共识偏差"，最终找到了在精度、延迟和 token 开销之间达到帕累托最优的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测市场研究缺乏将 Polymarket 情绪信号与全球媒体流（如 GDELT）进行系统性融合的方法，且对"视角不匹配"作为交易信号的价值缺乏量化分析。同时，多智能体系统中各种"引导工程"技术在金融高噪声环境下的实际效果尚未被严格评估。

Method: 提出 PolyGnosis 2.0 多智能体架构，定义"视角不匹配"为信念分歧信号；通过 HARness Engineering 技术（反思循环、工具调用、分治分区 D&C、思维链 CoT）构建智能体系统；以人类专家基准进行实证评估。

Result: 结构分区（D&C）是多维对齐的必要条件；无约束的终端反思会导致逻辑漂移（不收敛）；所有智能体配置在叙事推理中普遍存在"共识偏差"；找到了帕累托最优配置。

Conclusion: PolyGnosis 2.0 可实现专业级分析精度，同时最小化延迟和 token 开销，为预测市场的自主智能提供了稳健蓝图。

Abstract: This paper introduces PolyGnosis 2.0, a pioneering multi-agent architecture designed to extract predictive intelligence by synthesizing Polymarket anomaly signals with global Open Source Intelligence (OSINT) streams, specifically Global Database of Events, Language, and Tone (GDELT). We define and target "Perspective Mismatches", the narrative divergence between Polymarket sentiment and global media flows, as high-alpha trading signals. Moving beyond generic agentic superiority, we rigorously quantify the efficacy of "Harness Engineering" techniques, including reflection loops, tool-calling, divide-and-conquer partitioning (D&C), and chain-of-thought (CoT), within high-noise financial domains. Our empirical evaluation against human-expert benchmarks reveals that while structural partitioning is mandatory for multi-dimensional alignment, unconstrained terminal reflection actively induces logical drift. Furthermore, we identify a pervasive "consensus bias" across all agent configurations during narrative reasoning, necessitating deterministic validation. Ultimately, we isolate a Pareto-optimal configuration that achieves professional-grade analytical precision while minimizing latency and token overhead, providing a robust blueprint for autonomous intelligence in prediction markets.

</details>


### [243] [Triplet-Block Diffusion RWKV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25969)
*Ke Lin,Yiyang Luo,Zhaolong Su,Yunya Song,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出B³D-RWKV，一种融合双向扩散与因果RWKV的高效离散扩散语言模型，在保持精度的同时实现1.6倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Causal Transformer存在严格顺序解码和二次注意力复杂度问题；线性时间因果模型与离散扩散模型在注意力方向性（双向 vs. 单向）上存在根本冲突，缺乏统一架构。

Method: 提出B³D-RWKV，通过triplet-block layout方法将RWKV的O(L)推理效率与并行双向离散扩散相结合，实现因果与双向注意力的统一。

Result: B³D-RWKV-7.2B在8任务评测集上与现有模型精度相当，解码吞吐量平均提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: 成功统一了线性时间因果模型与双向离散扩散模型，显著提升了解码效率。

Abstract: Causal Transformer language models suffer from strictly sequential decoding and a quadratic per-step attention cost. While linear-time causal models and discrete diffusion models each address these weaknesses, their integration remains inherently inconsistent: diffusion requires bidirectional attention, while causal models are unidirectional. To unify these architectures, we propose $B^3D-RWKV$, a diffusion RWKV variant that integrates the model's $O(L)$ inference efficiency with parallel, bidirectional discrete-diffusion through a \emph{triplet-block layout} method. $B^3D-RWKV-7.2B$ reaches comparable accuracy on an 8-task suite versus existing models while significantly outperforming baselines in decoding throughput with an average of $\mathbf{1.6\times}$ speedup.

</details>


### [244] [Anticipate and Learn: Unleashing Idle-Time Compute in Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25971)
*Haoyi Hu,Qirong Lyu,Xianghan Kong,Weiwen Liu,Jianghao Lin,Zixuan Guo,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ProAct，一种利用空闲时间主动预测并准备用户需求的智能体架构，并构建了包含200个场景的ProActEval基准。实验表明，相比被动式基线，ProAct减少14.8%的任务交互轮次、降低11.7%的用户工作量、减少28.1%的幻觉率，并在MemBench上达到最先进的反省准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体在被用户提示后才被动响应，空闲时间被浪费，无法主动为未来用户需求做准备。

Method: ProAct通过分析进化中的对话历史与持久记忆，预测即将到来的用户需求，并在空闲时间迭代获取信息、填补知识空白、准备证据。同时构建ProActEval基准（200个场景、40个领域）以评估主动能力。

Result: 在ProActEval上，任务交互轮次减少14.8%，用户工作量降低11.7%，幻觉率降低28.1%；在MemBench上达到最先进的反省准确性。

Conclusion: ProAct证明了利用空闲时间进行主动准备能显著提升智能体的效率、降低用户负担并减少错误，为构建前瞻性智能体提供了有效范式。

Abstract: While AI agents demonstrate remarkable capabilities in reasoning and tool use, they remain fundamentally reactive: they compute responses only after explicit user prompts. This paradigm ignores a critical opportunity: the idle time between interactions is largely wasted, leaving agents unable to prepare for future user needs. To bridge this gap, we introduce ProAct, a proactive agent architecture that leverages idle-time compute to anticipate and fulfill likely upcoming user needs. By analyzing evolving dialogue history together with persistent memory, ProAct predicts upcoming needs and iteratively acquires information, allowing the agent to resolve knowledge gaps and prepare evidence before the user initiates a query.To rigorously evaluate proactive capabilities, we also introduce ProActEval, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 200 scenarios across 40 domains, featuring predictable need chains and diverse user cognitive profiles. Empirical results demonstrate significant advantages over reactive baselines. ProAct accelerates task completion by reducing required turns by 14.8%, decreases user effort by 11.7%, and cuts hallucination rates by 28.1% on ProActEval. Furthermore, MemBench evaluations confirm that ProAct achieves state-of-the-art reflective accuracy, underscoring its sustained and robust performance.

</details>


### [245] [Creative Quality Alignment: Expert Tacit Knowledge Transfer via Chain-of-Thought Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25977)
*Bo Zou,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Calibrated Surprise中提出的创意质量指标进行了工程层面的实证验证，在严格条件（低数据成本、小基座模型）下，利用约100个专家CoT标注证明了该指标的工程可行性，并发现数据偏差和架构对偶性的理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Calibrated Surprise (Zou & Xu, 2026a) 中提出的创意质量指标在工程层面是否成立，即将数学主张转化为可操作的工程方法。

Method: 采用最严格的工程条件：低数据成本和小基座模型；训练数据来自BC Protocol (Zou & Xu, 2026b) 产生的约100个专家CoT标注；提出Creative Quality Alignment (CQA) 工程方法；基于单条件分布架构下欣赏端与生成端的对偶性提供理论支撑。

Result: 识别出公开对齐数据集存在偏向工艺知识、弱化受众建模和现实逻辑覆盖的数据偏差；约100个CoT样本即足以实现创意质量对齐，其结构原因在于架构对偶性（而非LIMA的纯经验观察）。

Conclusion: Calibrated Surprise的创意质量指标在工程层面可行，CQA方法在极低数据成本下有效，其效率源于架构对偶性而非数据规模。

Abstract: This paper provides an empirical implementation of the creative quality metric proposed in Calibrated Surprise (Zou & Xu, 2026a). The question this paper addresses is: does this mathematical claim hold at the engineering level?
  To make the answer as general as possible, we deliberately choose the strictest engineering conditions: low data cost and a small base model. Training data comes from approximately 100 expert chain-of-thought (CoT) annotations produced by the BC Protocol (Zou & Xu, 2026b).
  We also identify a data bias: most publicly available alignment datasets are skewed toward craft-related knowledge, while audience modeling and reality-logic coverage are systematically weak.
  We use the term Creative Quality Alignment (CQA) to describe this class of engineering methods. We also offer a supporting theoretical observation: in an LLM with a single conditional distribution architecture, calibrating the appreciation side automatically transfers to the generation side via architectural duality. This is the structural reason why ~100 CoT examples are sufficient -- not a purely empirical observation like LIMA (Zhou et al., 2023).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23909)
*Noam Michael,Daniel BenShushan,Jacob Bien,Don A. Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在不同任务上的置信度校准问题。预注册研究发现，当前LLMs与人类类似，平均而言过度自信（置信度超过准确率）。这种倾向受到强烈的难易效应调节：在困难测试中过度自信最严重，而在简单测试中则表现出明显的信心不足。作者开发了LifeEval测试，用于评估不同难度水平下的模型校准情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型的置信度校准问题，探究LLMs是否像人类一样存在过度自信偏差，以及这种偏差如何随任务难度变化。

Method: 采用预注册研究方法，在多样化任务上比较LLMs的置信度与实际准确率，并开发LifeEval测试工具来系统评估不同难度水平下的校准表现。

Result: 发现LLMs平均而言过度自信（置信度高于准确率），且存在显著的难易效应：困难任务上过度自信最严重，简单任务上反而表现出明显的信心不足。

Conclusion: LLMs的置信度校准存在系统性的偏差模式，需要针对不同难度水平进行校准优化；LifeEval可作为评估工具。

Abstract: We investigate the calibration of large language models' (LLMs') confidence across diverse tasks. The results of our preregistered study show that the current crop of LLMs are, like people, too sure they are right: confidence exceeds accuracy, on average. Importantly, however, this tendency is moderated by a powerful hard-easy effect, wherein overconfidence is greatest on difficult tests; by contrast, easy tests actually show substantial underconfidence. We develop LifeEval, a test for evaluating model calibration across levels of difficulty.

</details>


### [247] [How Much Thinking is Enough? Quantifying and Understanding Redundancy in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23926)
*Zhiyuan Zhai,Xinkai You,Wenjing Yan,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对推理型大语言模型的思维链冗余度进行了大规模量化和理论解释。研究发现，在四个前沿推理模型和两个数学基准上，步骤级冗余度高达61%-93%，中位关键前缀仅为一个步骤。理论上证明，这种冗余是长度无关的结果奖励的结构性后果，而非模型特定缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型大语言模型通过长思维链解决难题，但产生了严重的延迟、GPU时间和能耗开销。尽管直觉上这些推理轨迹包含大量重述、验证和循环自省，但从未在规模上量化过这些思考的必需程度，也缺乏第一性原理解释。

Method: 形式化定义推理冗余度：正确轨迹中可被截断的最大尾部段落后，模型被迫终止思考并给出答案仍能正确的比例。在四个前沿推理模型（π）和两个数学基准（MATH-500等）上进行大规模量化实验，并使用不同的评判模型验证鲁棒性。理论部分通过数学证明，论证长度无关的结果奖励必然导致无有限最优停时的结构性质。

Result: 步骤级冗余度在8个（模型，基准）条件中持续在61%-93%之间；八个条件中有六个的中位关键前缀仅为一个分段步骤；在MATH-500上冗余度随问题难度下降，但即使在最难的Level-5问题上，所有四个模型的冗余度仍在[46%, 85%]范围内；该发现对评判模型选择鲁棒。

Conclusion: 推理冗余是长度无关结果奖励的结构性后果，而非特定模型的缺陷。无论RL算法、基础模型、数据分布如何，也不论策略是通过RL还是蒸馏获得，过度思考都是当前推理模型训练方式的固有属性。

Abstract: Reasoning-capable large language models solve hard problems by emitting long chains of thought, paying heavily in latency, GPU time, and energy. Casual inspection of their traces reveals extensive reformulation, verification, and circular self-reflection, yet how much of this deliberation is actually necessary has never been measured at scale or explained from first principles. This paper closes both gaps.
  We formalise reasoning redundancy directly in terms of the reasoning model itself: the redundancy of a correct trace is the largest fraction of its trailing segmented steps that can be truncated while $π$, forced to terminate thinking and emit a final answer, still produces the correct answer. A large-scale quantification across four frontier reasoning models and two mathematical benchmarks shows that step-level redundancy is consistently high -- between 61% and 93% across the 8 (model, benchmark) conditions we study, with the median critical prefix equal to a single segmented step in six of the eight conditions -- that the finding is robust to the choice of judge family, and that although $ρ$ decreases with problem difficulty on MATH-500, all four models remain substantially redundant ($ρ\in [46\%, 85\%]$) even on the hardest Level-5 problems.
  We then prove that this redundancy is a structural consequence of length-agnostic outcome rewards, not a model-specific artefact: under any such reward, no finite expected stopping time is optimal. The result holds regardless of RL algorithm, base model, data distribution, or whether the policy is obtained via RL or distillation; over-thinking is therefore not a bug to be patched in individual models but a structural property of how current reasoning models are trained. Code: https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/how-much-thinking-is-enough

</details>


### [248] [Toward Reliable Design of LLM-Enabled Agentic Workflows: Optimizing Latency-Reliability-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23929)
*Ya-Ting Yang,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了LLM赋能的智能体工作流中延迟、可靠性和成本之间的基本权衡，提出了参数化指数可靠性函数来建模LLM与非LLM智能体，并给出了在延迟和成本约束下的最优工作流设计，包括注水式令牌分配策略和影子价格刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统日益依赖由多个交互智能体（包括LLM驱动和传统计算模块）组成的工作流，但缺乏对这些工作流中延迟、可靠性和成本之间基本权衡的理论分析。

Method: 针对LLM和非LLM智能体分别建立性能模型，利用参数化指数可靠性函数刻画计算投入与输出质量的关系；在延迟和成本约束下研究顺序工作流的设计问题，推导出注水式令牌分配策略，并通过影子价格表征最优工作流可靠性。

Result: 提出了注水式令牌分配策略，给出了最优工作流可靠性的影子价格表征。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM赋能的智能体工作流提供了理论分析框架，揭示了关键性能权衡，并为工作流优化设计提供了可操作的策略。

Abstract: Modern AI systems increasingly rely on workflows composed of multiple interacting agents, some powered by large language models (LLMs) and others by conventional computational modules. This paper analyzes the fundamental tradeoffs between latency, reliability, and cost in LLM-enabled agentic workflows. We introduce performance models for both LLM and non-LLM agents that capture the relationship between computational effort and output quality, incorporating the impact of reasoning and output tokens for LLM agents using a parametric exponential reliability function. Then, we study the design of sequential workflows under latency and cost constraints. Main results include a water-filling token allocation policy and characterizations of optimal workflow reliability in terms of shadow prices.

</details>


### [249] [Quantum Frog: Emergent Cooperation and Difficulty Scaling in a Quantized-Time Cooperative Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23930)
*Saad Mankarious*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款双人合作游戏Quantum Frog，其核心机制是“量子化时间”（只有玩家行动时环境才推进）。通过强化学习（Tabular Q-Learning、DQN、IDQN、MAPPO）分析四个设计问题，发现：最佳策略是“冲刺策略”；增加一个不协调的队友比将交通流量提升六倍更难；合作训练可将成功率提升32-34个百分点，并将回合长度从~90步降至~6步；涌现的合作策略是同步冲刺而非复杂位置协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望通过量子化时间这一新颖机制设计一款合作游戏，并利用强化学习作为分析工具，系统性地回答游戏难度随交通密度如何变化、最优单智能体策略、独立与合作双智能体之间的合作差距、以及合作激励下涌现的联合策略等四个核心设计问题，为游戏的商业化设计提供实证指导。

Method: 基于量子化时间机制的8×8网格合作游戏Quantum Frog，使用四个递进阶段的强化学习算法（Tabular Q-Learning、DQN、IDQN、MAPPO）进行训练，在1至6辆车的交通密度下评估各算法表现，对比单智能体与多智能体场景下的成功率与回合长度。

Result: (1) 量子化时间机制使“冲刺策略”（每一步直接向上移动）成为通用最优策略；(2) 增加一个不协调的第二玩家比将单专家玩家的交通流量提升六倍更困难；(3) 合作训练（MAPPO）相比独立智能体（IDQN）将联合成功率提升32-34个百分点，回合长度从约90步降至约6步；(4) 涌现的合作策略是同步冲刺，而非复杂的位置协调。

Conclusion: 量子化时间机制通过最小化智能体在交通中的暴露时间，使得最优策略趋向于简单直接的冲刺行为；共享激励足以在时间关键型合作任务中使智能体达成对齐，无需复杂的通信或位置协调机制。这些发现为Quantum Frog的商业设计提供了具体指导，也为环境机制如何塑造多智能体学习动态提供了更广泛的见解。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{Quantum Frog}, a two-player cooperative game built on a novel \emph{quantized-time} mechanic in which the environment advances only when a player acts. Inspired by the classic arcade game Frogger, Quantum Frog requires two frogs to cross an 8$\times$8 grid of traffic and reach the far side together. We use reinforcement learning (RL) as an analytical lens to answer four design questions: (1) how does game difficulty scale with traffic density, (2) what is the optimal single-agent policy and why, (3) how large is the cooperation gap between independent and cooperative two-agent play, and (4) what joint strategy emerges when agents are incentivised to cooperate? We train agents through five escalating stages, Tabular Q-Learning, Deep Q-Network (\DQN), Independent \DQN~(\IDQN), and Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimisation (\MAPPO\ with a centralised critic), evaluating each against traffic densities of one to six cars. Our key findings are: (i) the quantized-time mechanic makes a \emph{rush strategy} (moving directly upward at every step) universally optimal, as time exposure to traffic is minimised; (ii) adding an uncoordinated second player is harder than sextupling the traffic for a single expert player; (iii) cooperative training recovers +32--34 percentage points of joint success rate relative to independent agents and reduces episode length from $\sim$90 to $\sim$6 steps; and (iv) the emergent cooperative strategy is synchronised rushing, not complex positional coordination, illustrating that shared incentives alone suffice to align agents in time-critical cooperative tasks. These findings provide concrete, empirically grounded guidance for the commercial design of Quantum Frog and offer broader insights into the role of environment mechanics in shaping multi-agent learning dynamics.

</details>


### [250] [BODHI: Precise OS Kernel Specification Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23931)
*Zhiming Chang,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BODHI是一种领域知识提示方法，通过结构化的C到Python翻译指南增强少样本提示，在OSV-Bench基准上将LLM生成操作系统内核形式化规范的成功率从55.10%提升至96.73%（Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI），增益范围+11%~+32%，覆盖9种模型6个提供商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统内核的形式化验证需要精确的规范，手动编写需要深厚的领域专业知识，因此希望利用LLM自动化规范生成过程，但现有方法在OSV-Bench基准上最佳Pass@1仅55.10%，性能不足。

Method: 提出BODHI方法，在标准少样本提示中增强结构化的C-to-Python翻译指南，覆盖15类领域特定翻译模式。受Structured Chain-of-Thought (SCoT)启发，指南按关注点分离组织，将前置条件提取和后置条件生成作为不同类别处理。

Result: 在6个提供商（Anthropic, Mistral, Amazon, DeepSeek, Meta, Alibaba）的9种模型（涵盖dense、mixture-of-experts和reasoning架构）上评估，BODHI全面提升了每个模型，增益+11%~+32%。最佳配置Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI达到96.73% Pass@1。BODHI同时减少了语法错误和语义错误，对具有足够指令跟随能力以利用结构化参考材料的模型效果最强。

Conclusion: 领域知识注入是一种模型无关的技术，能够显著缩小通用代码生成与形式化规范合成之间的差距。

Abstract: The formal verification of operating system kernels requires precise specifications that capture the intended behavior of system calls. Writing these specifications manually demands deep domain expertise, motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) to automate the process. However, in OSV-Bench, a benchmark of 245 specification generation tasks derived from the Hyperkernel OS kernel, the best reported Pass@1 is 55.10%. We propose a domain knowledge prompting method (BODHI), which augments the standard few-shot prompt with a structured C-to-Python translation guide covering 15 categories of domain-specific translation patterns. Inspired by Structured Chain-of-Thought (SCoT) prompting, the guide organizes translation by separation of concerns, addressing pre-condition extraction and post-condition generation as distinct categories. Evaluated on nine models from six providers (Anthropic, Mistral, Amazon, DeepSeek, Meta, Alibaba), covering dense, mixture-of-experts and reasoning architectures, BODHI improves every model tested, with gains ranging from +11% to +32%. The best configuration (Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI) reaches 96.73% Pass@1. BODHI reduces both syntax and semantic errors, with the strongest effect on models that have sufficient instruction-following capability to utilize structured reference material. These results demonstrate that domain knowledge injection is a model-agnostic technique that substantially bridges the gap between general-purpose code generation and formal specification synthesis.

</details>


### [251] [When Correct Beliefs Collapse: Epistemic Resilience of LLMs under Clinical Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23932)
*Boyu Xiao,Xiuqi Tian,Xuwen Song,Haochun Wang,Guanchun Song,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Med-Stress压力测试框架，发现LLM在临床对话中存在知识-鲁棒性分离现象，并设计RBED（推理时防御）和R-FT（训练时微调）两种方法提升模型信念稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床对话中即使初始诊断正确，也可能在 escalating pressure（逐步升级的质疑压力）下产生多轮谄媚行为（multi-turn sycophancy），即放弃正确诊断。现有 benchmarks 无法评估这种信念稳定性问题。

Method: 提出Med-Stress压力测试框架评估信念稳定性；提出RBED（基于角色的认知防御，推理时方法）和R-FT（韧性导向微调，训练时方法）两种缓解策略。

Result: 9个前沿LLM中发现初始诊断能力与信念稳定性存在明显分离（知识-鲁棒性差距）；R-FT几乎完全消除信念改变，显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 高医疗知识≠高鲁棒性；R-FT能有效内化基于证据的抗压能力，从根本上缓解临床对话中的谄媚行为。

Abstract: Despite strong medical benchmark accuracy, LLMs can exhibit severe multi-turn sycophancy in clinical dialogue, abandoning initial correct diagnosis under escalating pressure. We propose \textbf{\textsc{Med-Stress}}, a targeted stress test framework that evaluates belief stability under escalating pressure. Across nine frontier large language models (LLMs), we find a clear dissociation between medical knowledge and robustness: high initial diagnostic capability does not imply high belief stability, yielding large knowledge-robustness gaps for several LLMs. To mitigate this failure mode, we propose a lightweight inference-time defense, \textbf{\texttt{RBED}} (\textbf{R}ole-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{E}pistemic \textbf{D}efense), and \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} (\textbf{R}esilience-oriented \textbf{F}ine-\textbf{T}uning), a training-time approach that internalizes evidence-based resistance to pressure. Experiments show that \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} nearly eliminates belief change and substantially improves robustness.

</details>


### [252] [Practical Quantum CIM Empowerment via All-Domestic-Core Agentic Large Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23934)
*Wang Rui,Lu Diannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将飞秒激光泵浦的相干伊辛机(CIM)与基于LLM的智能体系统(利用LangGraph和LangChain框架)相结合，实现了量子CIM的全自主国产化赋能，包括QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代和文献方案快速验证。同时发现智能体辅助量子计算迭代中积累的知识能反向增强智能体自身问题解决能力这一新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算设备求解NP完全问题能力强，但建模复杂导致非专家难以使用，且专家在约束权重迭代和建模方法上耗费大量精力，亟需自动化、智能化的建模辅助手段。

Method: 将飞秒激光泵浦的CIM与LLM驱动的智能体系统集成，利用LangGraph和LangChain框架实现QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代和文献方案验证等任务的自动化。

Result: LLMs能有效执行量子计算建模任务；所有任务可基于国产大模型和国产CIM硬件实现全自主国产化赋能；发现智能体辅助量子计算迭代积累的知识能反向增强智能体问题解决能力的新范式。

Conclusion: 成功实现了鲁棒的技术集成，为后续研究奠定基础，同时指出大模型和量子计算两个前沿领域当前阶段仍存在的挑战。

Abstract: Quantum computing devices are recognized as powerful tools for solving NP-complete problems. However, the intricacy of their modeling presents notable barriers for non-specialists, while the tedious iteration of constraint weights and modeling methodologies also consumes substantial effort on the part of experts. To address these challenges, this study integrates a femtosecond laser-pumped Coherent Ising Machine (CIM) with an LLM-driven agentic system by leveraging the LangGraph and LangChain frameworks. Comprehensive investigations demonstrate that large language models (LLMs) can effectively perform such tasks in modeling as QUBO/Ising model calibration, constraint weight decision iteration and rapid validation of literature-reported schemes. Notably, all these tasks can be fully implemented based on domestic large models, combined with domestically developed CIM hardware, we truly achieve the practical empowerment of quantum CIM that fully relies on all-domestic agentic large models and hardware. This work successfully realizes robust technological integration, laying a solid foundation for subsequent research. Nevertheless, it also identifies the persisting challenges in the two cutting-edge fields of large models and quantum computing at the current stage. Encouragingly, we unexpectedly discover a promising new paradigm where accumulated knowledge from agent-assisted quantum computing iterations reciprocally enhances the agent's own problem-solving capability, thereby addressing these challenges.

</details>


### [253] [Operationalizing Reconstructive Authority: Runtime Construction, Dependency Resolution, and Execution Gating in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23935)
*Marcelo Fernandez - TraslaIA*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种运行时执行模型，通过在动作执行时评估权威的可构造性，并引入“暂停”（halt）状态和恢复循环机制，确保系统仅在有可构造权威的情况下执行动作，从而保障安全性和条件活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体系统不仅因错误决策而失败，也因执行了运行时权威不再有效的决策而失败。先前工作将重构权威（RAM）定义为有效执行的条件，但未解决如何在运行系统中强制执行该条件的问题。

Method: 提出运行时执行模型，在动作执行时评估权威并将执行条件建立在其可构造性上；引入第三个状态“暂停”（halt）以处理权威因观测不完整或不确定而未定义的情况；定义具体的执行协议，包括动态依赖解析、权威重构和显式决策语义；引入恢复循环，将漂移检测（IML）与执行控制（ACP）集成，使系统能够暂停执行、获取缺失信息并重新尝试权威重构。

Result: 该模型保证了安全性——没有动作会在没有可构造权威的情况下执行；同时保证了条件活性——当权威定义变量变得可观测时，执行恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作将重构权威操作化为运行时强制机制，提供了在真实系统中应用RAM所需的执行语义。

Abstract: Autonomous agent systems fail not only due to incorrect decisions, but due to executing decisions whose authority no longer holds at runtime. Prior work defined Reconstructive Authority (RAM) as a condition for valid execution: actions are permitted only if authority can be constructed from current state.
  This paper addresses enforcement at runtime: how to enforce this condition in a running system.
  We introduce a runtime execution model in which authority is evaluated at action time and execution is conditioned on its constructibility. This extends the execution state space beyond admit/deny with a third state, halt, representing cases where authority is undefined due to incomplete or uncertain observability.
  We define a concrete execution protocol including dynamic dependency resolution, authority reconstruction, and explicit decision semantics. We further introduce a Recovery Loop that integrates drift detection (IML) with execution control (ACP), allowing the system to suspend execution, acquire missing information, and re-attempt authority reconstruction.
  We show that this model guarantees safety -- no action is executed without constructible authority -- and conditional liveness: execution resumes when authority-defining variables become observable.
  This work operationalizes reconstructive authority as a runtime enforcement mechanism, providing the execution semantics required to apply RAM in real systems.

</details>


### [254] [BoxLitE: A Faithful Knowledge Base Embedding Based on Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23937)
*Bruno F. Lourenço,Hesham Morgan,Ana Ozaki,Aleksandar Pavlović,Emanuel Sallinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BoxLitE是一个针对DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$知识库的嵌入模型，通过将概念映射到凸区域（盒子）来实现凸优化，并证明对于任何可满足的DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$知识库，存在一个弱忠实的BoxLitE嵌入，将知识库嵌入任务形式化为凸优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识库嵌入方法利用凸性表示TBox中的概念层次结构（一般概念对应较大凸区域，包含更具体概念的区域），但凸性在实际学习任务中很少被充分利用。

Method: 提出BoxLitE模型，将概念映射到向量空间中的凸区域（盒子），支持凸优化；将知识库嵌入任务形式化为凸优化问题。

Result: 证明对于任何可满足的DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$知识库，BoxLitE存在一个弱忠实模型；展示了如何通过凸优化获得具有忠实性属性的嵌入。

Conclusion: BoxLitE成功将凸性融入知识库嵌入的学习过程中，实现了对DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$的弱忠实嵌入，为结合ABox和TBox信息的嵌入方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Knowledge base (KB) embeddings aim at combining the capability of classical knowledge graph embeddings to generalize the information present in facts, the ABox, with conceptual knowledge represented in an ontology language, the TBox. Several authors have recently explored the idea of mapping concepts to convex regions in a vector space. This is useful to represent hierarchies, typically present in TBoxes, since more general concepts can be mapped to larger regions, containing those regions associated with more specific concepts. However, the power of convexity is rarely leveraged during the actual learning tasks. Here, we introduce BoxLitE, a KB embedding model for DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ that allows for convex optimization. We show that for any satisfiable DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ KB, there is a BoxLitE embedding that is a weakly faithful model. As a proof of concept, we show how to formulate the KB embedding task as a convex optimization problem and how to obtain embeddings with such desirable faithfulness properties.

</details>


### [255] [Authority Inversion in LLM-Mediated Ubiquitous Systems: When Models Trust Users Over Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23938)
*Long Zhang,Zi-bo Qin,Wei-neng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在融合异构输入时存在严重的格式依赖性权威分配问题：数值型传感器数据难以融入模型决策，自然语言用户主张主导最终判断（称为"权威反转"）。本文提出几何框架、两项审计指标（CIR/AAI）和几何权威校准（GAC）方法，实验显示传感器信任度极低（AAI=-0.805），GAC将HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%，表明显式审计和按应用配置权威分配至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在融合传感器测量值与用户主张时，权威分配机制隐式且未经验证，这在需要物理传感优先的场景中带来可靠性隐患。

Method: 提出上下文整合的几何框架、两个可计算审计指标（CIR和AAI），以及推理时层级干预方法GAC，通过理论引导的因果注入抑制错误用户权威。

Result: 在4个模型（4B-35B参数、3种架构）和4个数据集（576个冲突实例）上，AAI=-0.805（Cohen's d=-2.14），理论引导因果注入翻转80.2%错误决策，GAC将HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%。

Conclusion: LLM中介系统中的权威分配必须被显式审计并按应用场景配置，而非隐式交由模型学习。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly fuse heterogeneous inputs in ubiquitous systems. Yet, how LLMs implicitly allocate authority when sensor measurements and user claims conflict remains unexamined, raising critical reliability concerns for deployments where physical sensing must retain priority. Unlike explicit traditional fusion, LLMs bury authority allocation within learned representations.
